P1478|It is the document summary of party information, property information, valuation of the property and calculation of stamp duty & fees and also contains witnesses’ particulars along with the check & verification of align data before sending it to the SRO
P1482|Functional Step by Step Process
P1484|The system generates the ‘online document summary’ report, this report gives summary of all the details entered for the document registration. The user shall be able to go through the document summary as presented by the system. Document summary shall contain below details:
P1485|Address details (District, Taluka, Hobli, Village, SRO, Property Type, Survey No, Hissa No)
P1486|Property(s) details
P1487|Property data – Bhoomi and Article selected
P1488|Court order details
P1489|Liability details
P1490|Market Valuation and Fee calculation details
P1491|Party(s) details (Claimant, Executant, Identifier(s) details)
P1492|Upload Deed Document Along with Enclosures: The user shall be required to upload the deed document along with the enclosers in the application. The enclosure documents shall be according to the articles and type of registration
P1493|The date of execution of the document also needs to be selected by the applicant
P1494|The user shall be asked to select the Sub-Registrar office from the dropdown, and the application shall be forwarded to the selected SRO for evaluation
P1496|The user shall verify the above details in order to check any data entry mistakes, the user shall be required to accept the declaration and only after then the application shall be submitted for approval.
P1499|User Interface (UI)
P1508|DEPARTMENT LOGIN & DASHBOARD
P1510|Brief Description
P1512|Departmental login shall enable the authorized officers from SRO (Sub-Registrar Office) or any other officers from the department to login in the system. Login credentials which are ‘username’ and ‘password’ shall be provided by the department to the respective SRO or to other officers from the department. After system verify the login credentials and biometric authentication, concerned authorized officers from department shall be able to login in the system.
P1516|Functional Step by Step Process
P1518|Department Login: The authorized officers from SRO or any other officers from the department shall login in the system by entering Username and Password provided to them by the department
P1519|The system shall check the validation of login credentials entered by the user. In case login credentials are valid, then the user shall verify the biometrics. In case login credentials are invalid then the system shall prompt to enter username and password again
P1520|In case the biometrics captured by the system gets verified then the user shall be able to be logged in the system otherwise system shall prompt to capture biometrics again
P1522|On the Departmental login dashboard, officers from SRO or any other officers from the department shall be able to view the below details:
P1523|Total number of documents registered which will be categorized as Book 1, Book 3 & Book 4
P1525|Book 1: Book 1 will capture the contents of all registered non testamentary documents in respect of immovable property (e.g., Deeds of Sale, Gift, Mortgages, Lease, Release Deed, Exchange, Partition, Rectification, Cancellation etc.)
P1526|Book 3: Book 3 will capture the contents of testamentary documents like Will, Authority to adopt, Codicils Cancellations of wills etc.
P1527|Book 4: Book 4 will capture the contents of instruments like Power of Attorney, Adoption Deed, Divorce Deed, Trust, and other transactions involving movable assets etc.
P1529|Total number of marriages registered which will be categorized as Hindu, Special or other marriages
P1530|Hindu Marriage: For Hindu marriage, the bride and groom shall submit declaration of marriage in the form of form no.1. Declaration will be from bride and groom having married outside the marriage officer in presence of witnesses
P1531|For Special marriage, Bride and groom shall submit notice for intended marriage after completion of 30 days from date of notice after that marriage will be solemnize by SRO
P1532|Special marriage in other forms section 13, notice will be served by the married couple other than Hindus or inter religion marriage to get their marriage registered from date of their original marriage
P1534|Total amount of revenue generated which will be categorized on the basis of stamp duty and registration fees
P1535|Total Stamp Duty: It is the addition of Government duty, Surcharge duty & Cess duty. As per the user selection of article, Total Stamp Duty shall be auto calculated by the system
P1537|Total Registration Fees: Registration Fees will be determined by the department. Registration Fees will be calculated based on the market value, consideration amount and cap amount. Registration fee shall be auto calculated as per user’s selection by the system
P1539|Total number of transactions of Encumbrance certificate, Certified copy, and Number of firm registrations
P1540|Encumbrance Certificate: Encumbrance certificate is a record showing registered transactions over a property. For obtaining EC or CC applicant shall submit application form no. 22
P1542|Certified Copy: Agencies like banks refer to certified copies of registered sales deed before giving loan on a mortgaged property to verify the ownership. IT department may also refer to a certified copy to ascertain value of transaction and assess the income of the property buyer or seller. CC of registered documents relating to immovable properties is obtained to verify the ownership of property and legality of the transaction effected through it
P1544|Firm Registration: The applicant shall submit deed along with declaration to registrar of firms (District Registrar). The user shall select the firm option. Any two or more major citizen can enter partnership and register the partnership firm. The firm is registered in the office of District Registrar (Registrar of firms) of the concerned jurisdiction
P1546|The above details shall be accessible to the officers from SRO or any other officers from the department by providing the date range in the dashboard
P1548|Function:
P1550|User Interface (UI)
P1553|SRO CONFIRMATION
P1554|Brief Description
P1556|The user shall review the application and upload the deed document, the applicant shall be required to send the request to the SRO for getting the approval for the document registration. The Sub-Registrar shall check the deed document along with enclosures and the details submitted by the applicant shall also be checked i.e., document information, party details, property schedule, market valuation and fee calculated as per the article selection.
P1557|All the details entered in the application shall be checked according to the deed document, which is uploaded by the user before sending the request to SRO.
P1559|Functional Step by Step Process
P1561|Overview the Application: After submitting the application the user shall be re-directed to the application viewer where the details submitted by the user shall be merged in a single view. The application viewer could be downloaded in the PDF format by the user. If user found any of the information incorrect then, he could edit the application and save it again
P1563|Submit the Request to SRO: After verifying the application information and uploading of the documents is complete, the user shall be required to accept the declaration and send the request to SRO for approval
P1565|Application Alert Sent to SRO: After the application is successfully submitted by the user in the Kaveri portal then the application shall be forwarded to the respective SRO. An alert shall be notified to the respective Sub-Registrar after successfully receiving the application
P1567|Departmental Portal Login: The Sub Registrar from the department shall login using their login credentials and do the Biometric authentication. In case the sub registrar’s login credential and biometric authentication gets verified in the system then sub registrar will be logged in otherwise system will redirect to enter the login credential and do the biometric authentication again
P1569|Allocate Application to FDA/SDA: The responsibility of the Sub-Registrar would be to allocate the applications to the respective Second Division Assistant (SDA) or First Division Assistant (FDA) and after the application gets reviewed by the SDA/FDA then it will be sent to the Sub-Registrar for final evaluation of the application
P1571|Application Received by SDA/FDA
P1572|Login and Select Active Applications: The SDA/FDA shall login in the system and check the applications received by the SR for evaluation under the Active Application section
P1574|Open Application and check the enclosure Document & Property schedule: All the application which is being received by the SDA/FDA shall be accessible in FIFO (First In First Out) manner and there shall be a time frame of 24 – 48 hours to respond back to all the received applications. Firstly, SDA/FDA shall check the deed documents and enclosures which is submitted by the applicant in the portal. The SDA/FDA shall be required to check the property schedule as per the document
P1576|Check Market Valuation and Fee Calculation: The SDA/FDA shall be required to check the market valuation and fee calculation in the application as per the deed document and article selected by the user in the application. The SDA/FDA shall have the functionality to calculate the Market Value and Fee Calculation separately.
P1578|Evaluate Party Details and Article Selection: The SDA/FDA shall be checking the Party details submitted in the application as per the deed document uploaded by the user. The confirmation of the article selection shall also be checked by the SDA/FDA
P1580|Verified by SDA/FDA: After checking the information in the application as per the deed document, the SDA/FDA user shall verify each section and mention remarks if necessary. If any discrepancy is found in the application, then the section will not be verified, and SDA/FDA shall mention the recommendation which needs to be corrected in the application by the Applicant.
P1582|Note: In case, if any one of the sections is not verified by the SDA/FDA then the remarks shall be mandatory.
P1583|Application Received by SR
P1584|Verified by Sub-Registrar: The FDA/SDA will submit the application to Sub-Registrar after verification of the application. The SR will also check the information as per the deed document and verify each section and mention remarks if necessary.
P1586|Check Market Valuation and Fee Calculation: The SR shall be required to check the market valuation and fee calculation in the application as per the deed document and article selected by the user in the application. The SR shall also have the functionality to calculate the Market Value and Fee Calculation separately
P1588|Confirm & Approved by Sub-Registrar: After the assessment is completed by the SRO the Sub-Registrar shall be required to decide either to approve or not approve the application. The decision shall be made as per the rule book and guidelines of Stamps & Registration Department and in case the Sub-Registrar found discrepancies in the application then application evaluation result shall be sent to applicant along with the remarks from Sub-Registrar
P1590|Application Approved: In case the application is approved by the SRO, then the applicant would be able to continue with the payment process and appointment scheduling for the document registration
P1592|(Note: 1. The applicant will only receive the decision made by the Sub-Registrar and remarks entered for each section by the SR. The decision or remarks of the SDA/FDA will not be viewable to the Applicant and only SR shall have the access to view the SDA/FDA remarks; 2. After the application is received from FDS/SDA, the SR shall login and shall view the remarks made by FDA/SDA and shall add his/her own remarks and shall send it back to the applicant for either to attend to the remarks made and resend the application or to proceed to make payment; 3. When the changes (attend to SR remarks) are requested by the SR to the applicant, the system takes the applicant to the 1st page with the pre-filled data being retained as earlier over which he can over-write to make changes and then shall send back to the SR; 4. If the applicant is willing to attend to the remarks made by SR, then resend the application after corrections are made. If not willing to attend to the remarks made by SR, then the system shall generate the Endorsement with Reason for Rejection)
P1594|Function:
P1595|Applicant
P1596|FDA/SDA
P1598|Sub Registrar
P1605|User Interface (UI)
P1606|Split Screen View

P1617|PAYMENT
P1619|Brief Description
P1621|After computing Stamp duty and registration fee based on valuation or amount of consideration and obtaining SRO confirmation for the property registration, the applicant shall be required to enter the challan number, amount, and date of challan. The system shall check the details in Khajane-2 based on the challan details entered by the user.
P1623|Functional Step by Step Process
P1625|The user shall be required to visit Khajane-II portal for the payment of approved Stamp Duty and Registration Fees. The re-directed link to Khajane-II shall be available after user gets approval from the SRO.
P1627|Payment Details: For payment details, the system shall display the purpose of challan. The user shall enter the challan number, amount (in rupees) and date of challan transaction. After the user enters the details, the system shall check challan details in Khajane-II as per below mentioned condition:
P1629|Case 1:
P1631|In case the system finds challan details in Khajane-II then the system shall check if challan amount is greater than or equal to payable amount (as per stamp duty calculation). In case challan amount is greater than or equal to payable amount then user will get the message ‘payment details successfully submitted’, otherwise system shall prompt user to add another challan for the deficit amount.
P1633|Case 2:
P1634|In case the system doesn’t find challan details in Khajane-2 then the system shall redirect the user to enter proper challan details in the system.
P1636|Note: 1. The user shall be given option to add multiple challan for the single document registration. The process to add another challan shall remain same as mentioned above;
P1637|2. During the payment process, the applicant shall have 3 options:
P1638|a. Normal – where the party has to pay the SD, RF & SF as per the calculation of the SR
P1639|b. Under-valuation – where the party mayn’t accept SR’s valuation, but he prefers to pay as per his valuation with an option to refer the document for determination under section 45-A to DR. After the DR’s Order, the party has to make payment as per the orders of the DR. There should be a provision for making payment by Add Challan. Once the payment is made, an option has to be made to submit the challan to the SR. After receiving the challan, the SR shall order for Registration (Check & Register)
P1640|c. Impound – where the party mayn’t pay the proper SD by preferring to pay short levy of SD and the document gets impounded and sent to DR for ordering for the recovery of deficit SD with appropriate penalty. There should be a provision for making payment by Add Challan of deficit SD with appropriate penalty. Once the payment is made, an option has to be made to submit the challan to the SR. After receiving the challan, the SR shall order for Registration (Check & Register)

P1657|APPOINTMENT SCHEDULAR
P1658|Schedule Appointment to Visit SRO
P1659|Brief Description
P1661|The registered user will be able to book an appointment to visit sub registrar office (SRO) through Appointment scheduling function. User will be able to schedule appointment for certain duration and have the option to reschedule and cancel the appointment. The user will be required to select appointment date and available slots shall be displayed. After the confirmation from the user the system will generate unique token number.
P1664|Functional Step by Step Process
P1666|The user shall select the appointment booking option only after successfully made the payment in Khajane - II
P1667|User Type and Name: The user type and Name shall be auto fetched from the party details for booking the appointment
P1668|Sub Registrar office: Based on the selection of SRO in Online Document Summary page, the SRO Name shall be displayed, and appointment shall be booked for the same SRO
P1669|Date: The user will select the date as per his convenience and the next available slot shall be displayed
P1670|Confirm Appointment:
P1672|Case 1 – Appointment confirmed
P1673|In case the Appointment is confirmed, the system will generate unique token number
P1674|After token number is successfully generated, the system will display the slot booking confirmation to the user
P1675|Case 2 – Appointment not confirmed
P1676|In case the user doesn’t want to continue with the appointment, then the user will have an option either to change date or cancel the appointment
P1677|After slot booking confirmation, the User will receive all the appointment details over registered Email and SMS
P1679|Note: One hour time slot shall be left to discretion of Sub Registrar for emergency registration i.e., wills.
P1683|Reschedule Appointment
P1684|This rescheduling feature will get locked before pre-defined period which is configured by Sub-Registrar.
P1686|Brief Description
P1688|After scheduling the appointment, the user will have an option to Reschedule the appointment as per his convenience before actual appointment.
P1691|Functional Step by Step Process
P1693|Appointment scheduling service: The user shall select appointment scheduling option, then the system will display all the unique tokens generated for the user. The rescheduling option will be available to the user 24 hours before appointment gets expired
P1694|Token Number: The user shall select the token number to Reschedule the Appointment, the user will select the reschedule option
P1695|Token request validation:
P1697|Case 1 – Token Expired
P1698|In case the Token Number selected by the user is expired, then the system will end the process
P1699|Case 2 – Token not Expired
P1700|In case the Token Number selected by the user is available, the system will allow the user to Reschedule the appointment
P1702|User Type and Name: The user type and Name shall be auto fetched from the party details for booking the appointment
P1703|Sub Registrar office: Based on the selection of SRO in Online Document Summary page, the SRO Name shall be displayed, and appointment shall be booked for the same SRO
P1704|Date: The user will select the new date as per his convenience and the next available slot shall be displayed
P1705|Confirm Appointment:
P1707|Case 1 – Appointment confirmed
P1708|In case the user confirms the appointment, the system will generate unique new token number
P1709|After new token number is successfully generated, the system will display the slot booking confirmation to the user
P1710|Case 2 – Appointment not confirmed
P1711|In case the user doesn’t want to continue with the appointment, then the user will cancel the appointment
P1713|After slot booking confirmation, the User will receive all the appointment details over registered Email and SMS
P1716|Cancel Appointment
P1717|Brief Description
P1719|After scheduling the appointment, the user will also have an option to cancel the appointment as per his convenience before the actual appointment. or token number may get automatically expire in case of over-run of date and time.
P1723|Functional Step by Step Process
P1725|Appointment scheduling service: The user shall select appointment scheduling option, then the system will display all the unique tokens generated for the user
P1727|Token Number: The user shall select the token number to cancel the Appointment, the user will select the reschedule option
P1729|Token numbers to cancel: The user shall enter the reason behind the cancellation of the appointment and cancels the token

P1742|citizen on the status of application and to track timely completion of registration services
P1743|in all SRO offices. It keeps track of various things such as time taken for registration of a particular
P1744|document, scanning of a document etc..

P1756|APPLICATION REVIEW AND ALLOCATION TO DEO BY SR
P1758|Brief Description
P1759|As per the time slot booked by the Parties, they visit the Sub-Registrar Office and present the documents Infront of the Sub-Registrar. The Sub-Registrar shall start the registration process and perform the verification of Executant, Identification of Party, Seller/Purchaser Name, Classification of Document, Full Stamp Duty & Registration Fee Paid.
P1761|After the verification is completed by Sub-Registrar then the allocation to DEO shall be done for the assessment of application. Sub registrar shall allocate the received applications based on the slot timing which will allow the DEO to process the application for the verification of Parties, capture the photograph and biometrics of all the parties, generate summary report and endorsement to present for approval by Sub Registrar.
P1764|Functional Step by Step Process
P1766|SRO Login: The authorized Sub Registrar, shall login in the system by entering Username and Password provided to them by the department along with the biometric verification
P1767|The Sub-Registrar shall start the registration process and perform the verification of Executant, Identification of Party, Seller/Purchaser Name, Classification of Document, Full Stamp Duty & Registration Fee Paid
P1768|After the verification is completed by the Sub-Registrar then the allocation to DEO shall be done for the assessment of application
P1769|Sub registrar shall allocate the received applications based on the slot timing
P1770|SR will select DEO (Data entry operator) from the dropdown, to allocate for the pending applications. After DEO selection, SR shall select Allocate button for successfully allocating the application
P1771|The system shall check if more application is pending, In case application is pending then the system shall redirect to allocate application based on slot timing otherwise process will end
P1773|Function:
P1774|Application Allocation to DEO: by SRO
P1777|Note: The Sub-Registrar shall possess the authority to withdraw the application from the DEO before the registration process is initiated by the user.
P1784|User Interface (UI)
P1802|Application Re-Allocation to DEO
P1803|Brief Description
P1804|If it is required, the Sub-Registrar shall possess the authority to reallocate the application to another DEO.
P1808|Functional Step by Step Process
P1810|SRO (Sub Registrar officer) Login: The authorized SRO from the department shall login in the system by entering Username and Password provided to them by the department along with the biometric verification
P1811|The SRO shall select the application allocation option, after which the system will display all the allocated applications. SRO shall select the assigned application for reallocation to the DEO
P1812|SRO will select respective DEO (Data entry operator) from the dropdown, to reallocate the application. The application shall be successfully reallocated to the DEO in the system
P1814|DOCUMENT REGISTRATION
P1816|After Document Review by the SR, the application is allocated to DEO. The authorized DEO from the department shall login in the system by entering Username and Password provided to them
P1818|The DEO shall select the application as per the scheduled time slot-based on FIFO (first in first out) process
P1820|DEO will then check if all the parties are present, the party present in the office should voluntarily provide their photos and biometric authentication. In case the documents are valid and voluntary execution is successfully done, DEO will start the Document Registration process
P1822|The system will display application details and property details:
P1823|•	In application details system will display: Application number, submission date & application type
P1824|•	In property details system will display: Property data from Bhoomi & Aligned databases and Court order details
P1826|The DEO after capturing the party details, shall generate Document Summary, Endorsement, Receipt, EC and Acknowledgement Slip
P1828|Registration: Document Registration
P1831|Registration: Party Details
P1832|Brief Description
P1834|For property registration there shall be two parties: Claimant which may be buyer, shareholder, co-owner, or transferee and second party would be executant which may be seller, shareholder, co-owner or transferer. To verify the party some of the below details will be checked by DEO:
P1835|Name, Age, Gender, Address, PAN, and Profession
P1837|In the party detail section, the DEO shall be required to verify the details of the following parties involve in the property registration process:
P1838|Presenter (Only 1) maybe either claimant or executant
P1839|Claimant (1 or multiple)
P1840|Executant (1 or multiple)
P1841|Witness (Minimum 2)
P1843|UIDAI Guidelines for Capturing Fingerprints:
P1844|The fingers have to be positioned correctly on the platen to enable capture. There should be no direct light shining on the platen. Use the Indicators on fingerprint devices for positioning of fingers. The fingers should be placed in right direction on the device.
P1845|Use a lint free cloth periodically to clean the platen of the fingerprint device for good fingerprint capture
P1846|Check devices periodically for scratches, out of focus images, only partial images getting captured. In case any such problem is noticed, then report to your Supervisor/HQ and request for change of equipment.
P1847|Fingerprints cut off, wet/smudged fingerprint; very light prints due to insufficient pressure will result in poor quality. The party’s hands should be clean (no mud, oil etc.). Ask citizen to wash hands with water and soap, if necessary.
P1848|The fingers should not be excessively dry or wet. Moisten with a wet cloth or dry finger with a dry cloth
P1849|The Party should ensure that the fingers are placed flat and till the top joint of the finger is placed well on the scanner. The top of the fingers should be within the platen area and not outside the defined area.
P1850|The operator should check the actionable feedback when capture fails. Some actionable feedbacks provided by software are:
P1851|Number of fingers present does not match with expected number of fingers
P1852|Finger not positioned correctly
P1853|Too much Pressure (duty cycle)
P1854|Too little pressure
P1855|Central region missing
P1856|Excessive moisture (wetness)
P1857|Excessive dryness
P1858|The operator should visually check the image for quality and for typical problems. In case there are problems go back to steps above to retry the capture.
P1859|When image quality is pass or if maximum number of captures are exhausted, move on to the next step
P1860|Fingerprints are best captured in standing position
P1861|Make sure your own fingerprints do not get mixed with the party’s fingerprints
P1862|Operators can carefully put small pressure on the party’s fingers to capture the fingerprints but always make sure not to mix your own fingerprints
P1864|User Interface
P1870|Functional Step by Step
P1872|DEO (Data Entry operator) Login: The authorized DEO, from the department shall login in the system by entering Username and Password provided to them by the department to check the party details
P1874|The DEO will select the application as per the scheduled time slot-based on FIFO (first in first out) process
P1876|DEO will then check if all the parties are present, then the system shall display the party details for the verification
P1878|The DEO shall select from the below party type from the dropdown in the system:
P1879|Presenter
P1880|Claimant
P1881|Executant
P1882|Witness
P1884|The system will display application details and party details:
P1885|In application details system will display: Application number, submission date & application type
P1886|In party details system will display: Name, Age, Gender, Address, PAN & Profession
P1888|Based on party type selection, DEO will select individual for appearance. DEO will then match the party detail with respect to deed document, in the system. Then the system will check if the party is witness in case the party is not witness then the DEO will proceed with e-KYC and capture the photograph in the system otherwise DEO will check if all parties are verified. In case all parties are verified then DEO will save and continue with the process in the system otherwise DEO will select from the party type option for verification of the party
P1889|(Note: In case, the of appearance of parties at different times, the document shall be kept pending for appearance of the party. Kindly refer section 24.6 for ‘Late Appearance’ under ‘Exceptional Cases Module’)
P1891|After capturing photograph of the party, system will prompt if recapture of photograph is required. In case recapturing is required then the DEO will capture photograph again in the system otherwise DEO will capture the Biometrics of the party
P1893|After capturing Biometrics of the party, system will prompt if recapture of Biometric is required. In case recapturing is required then the DEO will capture biometrics of the party again in the system otherwise DEO will check if all parties are verified. In case all parties are verified then DEO will save and continue with the process in the system otherwise DEO will select from the party type option for verification of the party
P1895|Note: eKYC shall be mandatory for all the Parties which include Presenter, Claimant, Executant and Consenting Witness except for the Identifiers (Witness)
P1897|Registration: Document Summary & Print
P1898|Brief Description
P1899|For all the Parties except Identifiers eKYC, Photo and Biometric shall be captured. After this, the Document Summary report is generated and signed by the DEO and given to the Presenter to verify the data entered in the system are proper or any correction are needed. After Presenter’s verification and signature, Document Summary is verified by the FDA/SDA. If it is found correct, the report will be given to Sub Registrar for approval.
P1901|Functional Step by Step Process
P1903|Preview of the Document Summary report will be displayed to the DEO along with the Application Details consisting of Pending Number, Date of Execution and Application Type
P1904|The DEO shall send the Document Summary to the Sub-Registrar for Approval
P1905|The Sub-Registrar evaluate the Document Summary based on the Deed Document Uploaded
P1906|The Sub-Registrar shall take the decision based on the document review; three decisions could be taken by SR:
P1907|Register and Send to DEO
P1908|Kept the Document Pending
P1909|Refused Registration
P1911|(Note: If the SR takes the decision to Keep the Document Pending, then he/she has to choose from among the following options (reasons) which shall get recorded in the Minute Book
P1912|Pending for determination of undervaluation, referred to District Registrar u/s 45A of KS Act 1957
P1913|Pending for short levy of Stamp Duty, impounded u/s 33 of KS Act 1957
P1914|Pending for condonation of delay, u/s 25 of Registration Act 1908, document exceeding 4 months from the date of last execution read with Rule 52 of Registration Rule 1965
P1915|Pending for appearance of the parties, u/s 34 of Registration Act 1908
P1916|Pending for private attendance u/s 31 of Registration Act 1908)
P1918|(Note: If the SR takes the decision to Refuse the Registration, then he/she has to choose from among the following options (reasons) which shall get recorded in the Minute Book
P1919|Refused document registration for denial of execution u/s 35 of Registration Act 1908
P1920|Refused document registration for property not belonging to jurisdiction of the Sub Registrar u/s 28 of Registration Act 1908
P1921|Refused document registration for document presented by a wrong person u/s 32 of Registration Act 1908
P1922|Refused document registration for exceeding 8 months from the date of execution u/s 25 of Registration Act 1908
P1923|Refused document registration for not fulfilling the conditions of section 21 of Registration Act 1908)
P1925|In case, document is sent back to DEO for Registration, then Print of the Document Summary report will be taken by DEO and given to the Presenter for verification
P1926|Upon verification and signed by DEO, Presenter, and FDA/SDA, Sub Registrar will approve and sign the Document Summary Report
P1928|Document Summary Report will have the following details:
P1929|Document Number Pending generated
P1930|This will be a system generated number as and when the user starts the Document Registration
P1931|For example, P – 1700. “P” indicates pending number for the document which is under process of Registration. “1700” indicated the continuation number of the documents processed for the Registration from the beginning of the Financial Year.
P1932|Book Number, Number of Pages of Document, Nature of Deed and Stamp Article details are already mentioned by the applicant in the Online Application, the same information shall be displayed in the Document Summary Report
P1935|Execution Date
P1936|The DEO shall enter the Date of Execution on the Document Summary page as per the Deed document
P1937|Stamp Duty Payment Details
P1938|In case of offline, the DEO shall enter the Stamp Duty Payment Details from the Challan of Khajane 2 or eStamp Portal of Stock Holding Corporation of India Limited (SHCIL).
P1939|In case of Online, Payment Details shall be auto fetched from the system and DEO shall verify the same
P1945|Receipt Payment Details
P1946|User shall enter all details with respect to Receipt by looking into the Challan submitted
P1947|Receipt Number - indicates the continuation number of the documents processed for the Registration from the beginning of the Financial Year.
P1948|Date of Receipt – Date of payment of Khajane2 Challan or Date of payment at SRO
P1949|Amount Paid By – This shall be auto fetched from the system
P1950|Total Receipt Amount (In Rs.) – If it is Online, Receipt Amount shall be auto fetched from the system. If it is offline, DEO shall enter the Receipt Amount
P1953|Index II Village, Property Type, Boundary Details, Valuation Details, All Duties (Consideration, Market Value, Government Duty, Cess Duty, Additional/Surcharge Duty and Total Stamp Duty), Schedule Description, Party Details, Identifying Parties are already mentioned by the applicant in the Online Application, the same information shall be displayed in the document summary
P1955|Property Number Details
P1956|Property Number Details are already mentioned by the applicant in the Online Application, the same information shall be displayed in the document summary report.
P1957|In case of Agriculture Land,
P1958|Survey Number
P1959|Hissa Number
P1960|Pyki Number
P1961|Sketch Number
P1962|In case of Non-Agriculture Land,
P1963|Site Number
P1964|Corporation Number
P1965|Municipal List Number
P1966|Property Number
P1967|Flat Number
P1968|Converted Survey Number
P1969|Apartment Number
P1975|After taking the print of the Document Summary report that document will be verified and signed by the Presenter from party side and signed by the Operator (DEO) and FDA/SDA from Department side and sent to the Sub Registrar for signature
P1977|Registration: Endorsement & Print
P1978|Brief Description
P1980|On verification of the Summary Report, if all the details found correct Sub Registrar approves for Registration. Upon approval of the Sub Registrar, system will generate document number and Endorsement will be printed. Signature of the Presenter, Claimant, Executant, Witness and Sub Registrar shall be taken on the physical copy of the Endorsement.
P1984|Functional Step by Step Process
P1986|On Approval by the Sub Registrar, the Document Number will be generated from the system.
P1987|Document details such as Document Number, Execution Date and Application Type will be displayed on the screen
P1988|Endorsement will be generated and Printed
P1989|Signature of the Presenter, Claimant, Executant, Witness and Sub Registrar are obtained on the physical copy of the Endorsement.
P1994|Endorsement will have the following details:
P1995|10A Certificate - under Karnataka Stamp Act 1957 Section 10A
P1997|10A Certificate will have the details of amount of Stamp Duty paid by the Party along with the Challan Number and Date of Remittance. The Certificate will be signed by the proper officer/Deputy Commissioner of Stamps/Sub Registrar
P2000|If Exemption is there, Exemption particulars or if Denoting is there, Denoting certificate are also recorded
P2001|Presentation Endorsement
P2002|Presentation Endorsement will have the Sub Registrar Office name where the Document is presented, Date and Time and Name of the Presenter with the following Fees
P2003|Registration Fee
P2004|Scanning Fee
P2005|Mutation Fee in case of Agriculture Land
P2006|Miscellaneous
P2008|Party Information Endorsement
P2009|Party Information Endorsement will have the Presenter details such as Presenter Name, Photograph and Signature followed by Sub Registrar Signature.
P2010|Execution Admission by the Claiming Parties which includes Claimant Name, Photograph and Signature followed by Sub Registrar Signature.
P2011|Execution Admission by the Executing Parties which includes Executor Name, Photograph and Signature followed by Sub Registrar Signature
P2012|If appearance of the Party as per section 88 exemption is given and the particulars of the exemption letter will be recorded
P2014|Identifying Witness (Identifiers) Endorsement
P2015|Identifying Witness Endorsement will have the Name, Address and Signature of the two Witness followed by Sub Registrar Signature.
P2018|Registration Certificate as per Section 60 of Registration Act
P2019|Registration Certificate will have the Book Number, Document Number which consists of Office name in Trilateral Letters i.e., Abbreviation of the Sub Registrar’s Office Name, Book Number and Serial Number of the Document of the year and Date followed by Sub Registrar Signature
P2022|Registration: Scan & Upload
P2023|Brief Description
P2024|For document registration, after verifying the property details, party details, property schedule, market valuation, stamp duty, fee calculation and payment details, DEO will scan and upload the registered documents and annexures. DEO will scan the below mentioned document, after scanning the document successfully DEO will upload the document in the server.
P2026|Registered Document
P2027|Deed
P2028|Endorsement
P2029|Annexures
P2031|The user shall have two separate sections to upload the Registered Deed document and Annexures, all the annexures shall be scanned and uploaded at once.
P2035|Functional Step by Step Process
P2038|DEO will scan and upload the below mentioned document through the scanning system in the mentioned order:
P2039|Registered Document
P2040|Deed
P2041|Endorsement
P2042|Annexures
P2044|DEO will then check if rescanning is required. In case rescanning is required then DEO will again scan the required documents otherwise DEO will save the documents. The system will display message “Scanning Complete”, DEO will then preview the scanned documents
P2046|DEO will then take confirmation of scanned document with the concern parties. In case concern parties does not agree with the scanned document then DEO will correct the discrepancies in the scanned document in the system
P2048|In case concern parties agree with the scanned document then DEO will upload the scanned document and the document will be stored in the server. After scanned document will be successfully uploaded in the system, system will display message “all the documents have been successfully uploaded” and generate unique document number
P2050|User Interface
P2053|Registration: Digital Signature by SR
P2055|Brief Description
P2057|The Sub-Registrar shall be required to digitally sign all the scanned documents which includes scanned deed document and annexures.
P2061|Functional Step by Step Process
P2063|After the deed document and annexures are scanned and uploaded by the DEO, the application is sent to SR for Digitally Signed the file
P2064|Sub-Registrar shall receive the request from the DEO for Digital Signature
P2065|The Sub-Registrar shall trigger the Digital Sign and the complete document shall be digitally signed by the SR
P2066|After which, Sub-Registrar shall send back the application to DEO to continue and print the EC for the current transaction made in the system
P2068|Registration: Encumbrance Certificate & Print
P2070|Brief Description
P2072|Encumbrance Certificate is the certificate showing the particulars of transaction taken place over a property. During the Registration process, the data i.e., Party Information, Property Information, Valuation and Consideration, Date of Execution, Date of Registration and Document Number along with Book Number are stored in Index II database. From this Index II data Encumbrance Certificate in Form Number 15 will get generated.
P2075|Functional Step by Step Process
P2077|During Registration process, after entering all the data, the data will get stored in the Index II Form database for the purpose of EC generation
P2078|DEO shall generate the EC as per the Index II data
P2079|System shall display the document details for the Encumbrance generation
P2080|Print of the Encumbrance Certificate shall be given to the Party as a confirmation of Registration
P2082|Encumbrance Certificate will have the following details:
P2083|Application Number, Encumbrance Certificate printed Date and Time and Certificate Number
P2084|Form Number 15 – Encumbrance Certificate shall get generated in the form “Form Number 15” followed by Proforma Number 15 (Rule 148)
P2085|Details of the Property which includes Property Number and Extend of the Property which has been entered in the Online application
P2086|Table of EC Proforma:
P2087|Serial Number
P2088|Property Particulars – It includes Village Name and Property Schedule Description
P2089|Date of Registration
P2090|Nature of Document and Value in Rupees
P2091|Particulars of Parties
P2092|Executing Party
P2093|Claiming Party
P2094|Number of Pages of Deed document
P2095|Document Reference Details
P2096|Document Number – Trilateral Code of the Office Name followed by Book Number, Document Number and Year of Registration
P2097|Signature of the FDA/SDA and Sub Registrar
P2098|Disclaimer shall be printed on the EC document as per the Proforma
P2100|User Interface
P2102|Registration: Acknowledgement slip and Registration complete
P2103|Brief Description
P2104|After registration and scanning of the document and EC generation, system will generate acknowledgement slip and it will be printed. Signature of the FDA/SDA and Sub Registrar are obtained having deed document is scanned and handed over to presenter then the presenter will sign on the physical of the acknowledgement slip after receiving the deed document.
P2108|Functional Step by Step Process
P2110|After scanning and uploading the document and EC generation, system will generate acknowledgement slip and will display below document details:
P2111|Document Registration
P2112|Application Number
P2113|Date of Submission
P2114|Application Type
P2116|Acknowledgement Slip
P2117|Document Number
P2118|Date of Registration
P2119|Date of Scanning
P2120|Time and date will be auto generated by the system
P2122|DEO will preview the Acknowledgement Slip in the system and take the print of the slip. Signature of the FDA/SDA and Sub Registrar are obtained, and original deed document will be handed over to presenter
P2124|Then the presenter will sign on the physical copy of the acknowledgement slip which will certify that no documents submitted by the parties are remaining with the sub registrar after receiving the original deed document
P2126|As the DEO print Acknowledgement Slip and close the window, the Document Registration will be completed, and J-slip will be sent to BHOOMI in case the document registration involves an agricultural property
P2128|User Interface
P2131|Verify document
P2132|This is a service which would be provisioned for guest user on home screen of citizen landing
P2133|page. Through this service citizen can verify the authenticity of document which is them if it was
P2134|registered in KAVERI or KAVERI 2 post 2003. This helps in avoiding forgery of document or
P2135|tampering or misleading of purchaser

P2143|ENCUMBRANCE CERTIFICATE (EC) SEARCH
P2145|Brief Description
P2146|Encumbrance Certificate is the certificate which shows the previous transactions of Registrations on the Property. It can be searched in case of Agriculture Land based on Duration (From Date and To Date), District, Taluka, Hobli, Village Name, Survey Number, Hissa Number, Pyki Number, Property Number Type, Previous Property Number, Present Property Number along with Extent of the Property and Boundaries. It also can be searched with reference to seller and purchaser.
P2147|In case of Non-Agriculture Property, it can be searched based on the Duration (From Date and To Date), District, Taluka, Hobli, Village Name/Town/Ward, Road Name, Property Number Type, Previous Property Number, Present Property Number, Extent of the Property and Boundaries. It also can be searched with reference to seller and purchaser.
P2151|Functional Step by Step Process
P2153|Applicant shall select encumbrance certificate, after which the applicant will get an option to select below option:
P2154|Search by Blockchain key: The applicant shall enter Blockchain ID/ Card Number and search. The system shall display search result and continue with the process
P2155|Search by Document details: The applicant shall select District, Taluka, Hobli, Village. After which system shall fetch data from Master Database
P2156|The user shall enter boundary details i.e., east, west, north & south and description of the property
P2157|The user shall select Property Number type and enter property number
P2158|Add Property Details:
P2159|Case 1:
P2160|In case the user selects ‘Yes’ to add property details then the user shall select Property Number type and enter property number
P2162|Case 2:
P2163|In case the user selects ‘No’ to add property details then the system will send OTP to the registered mobile number. Applicant will enter OTP received and verify
P2165|OTP Verification:
P2166|Case 1:
P2167|In case OTP is verified by the system then the applicant will search for EC and system will generate Index II. The applicant will accept the result and proceed
P2169|Case 2:
P2170|In case OTP is not verified by the system then the applicant will be asked to enter OTP again
P2175|EC Payment
P2176|Brief Description
P2177|Once the Encumbrance Certificate is found, user shall be directed to do the Payment to get the Digitally Signed Encumbrance Certificate.
P2180|Functional Step by Step Process
P2181|Applicant shall select Payment Details, after which the system will display below details:
P2182|Application Fee
P2183|Search Fee
P2184|The system will display Application Number and Application Type
P2185|Application Number will be of the format BMH-EC-A-017566-2018-19. i.e, Tri Lateral of the Sub Registrar Office (BMH) followed by Type of Application (EC) followed by Application first letter (A) followed by serial number of the application (017566) followed by present Financial Year (2018-19)
P2187|The user shall enter Remitter Name and select SRO office name from the drop down. The user will then proceed to payment. System shall be redirected to Khajane-2
P2188|Payment Confirmation:
P2189|Case 1:
P2190|In case Payment is confirmed then the system will generate transaction receipt. Applicant will be able to print the receipt
P2193|Case 2:
P2194|In case Payment is not confirmed by the system then the applicant will be redirected to retry and proceed for payment again
P2196|EC Online Application Summary
P2197|Brief Description
P2198|Once the payment is done, the application details will be displayed on the system for the applicant to verify the details entered.
P2201|Functional Step by Step Process
P2202|The document summary will be displayed to the user and the elements of the summary page will be displayed by the system
P2203|System will display application details such as application number & application type and applicant details such as Name and Aadhar number of user registered in the system.
P2204|System will display Property details with description, Payment details and transaction receipt details in document summary
P2205|Applicant will be able to see the preview of the application and unsigned EC. Then it will be sent to SRO for Approval
P2208|EC Generation
P2209|Brief Description
P2210|An EC is the certificate showing the list of transactions on the property mentioned in “Form 15”. If no transactions are found, system generates nil EC in the form of “Form 16”. This report is signed by SR.
P2215|Functional Step by Step Process
P2217|SDA
P2218|Applicant shall submit the application for online EC.
P2219|The application shall be scrutinized by the SDA as per the parameters entered by the applicant in the system
P2220|As per the details entered in the application SDA will prepare the EC
P2221|If it is found correct, SDA shall view and download the EC
P2222|After downloading, SDA shall Digitally Sign the EC by using Adobe Acrobat
P2223|After signing the EC, SDA shall update the document with remarks and send it to FDA for further Approval
P2224|FDA
P2225|FDA shall receive the digitally signed EC from SDA
P2226|FDA shall validate the EC details as per the parameters entered by the applicant in the system
P2227|If it is found correct, FDA shall view and download the EC. If it is not found correct FDA shall revert the application back to the SDA
P2228|After downloading, FDA shall Digitally Sign the EC by using Adobe Acrobat
P2229|After signing the EC, FDA shall update the document and send it to Sub Registrar for further Approval
P2230|Sub Registrar
P2231|Sub Registrar shall receive the digitally signed EC from FDA
P2232|Sub Registrar shall validate the EC details as per the parameters entered by the applicant in the system
P2233|If it is found correct, Sub Registrar shall view and download the EC. If it is not found correct Sub Registrar shall revert the application back to the FDA
P2234|After downloading, Sub Registrar shall Digitally Sign the EC by using Adobe Acrobat
P2235|After signing the EC, Sub Registrar shall update the document and send it to the Applicant
P2236|Applicant shall view and download the Digitally Signed EC
P2248|MISCELLANEOUS DOCUMENT
P2250|Brief Description
P2251|Documents coming under Section 18 of Registration Act which are optional for Registration are considered as Movable Properties. It is also referred as Miscellaneous Documents.
P2252|Any of the following documents may be Registered under Section 18 of the Registration Act
P2253|Instruments (other than instruments of gift and wills) which purport or operate to create, declare, assign, limit or extinguish, whether in present or in future, any right, title or interest, whether vested or contingent, of a value less than one hundred rupees, to or in immovable property
P2254|Instruments acknowledging the receipt or payment of any consideration on account of the creation, declaration, assignment, limitation or extinction of any such right, title or interest
P2255|Leases of immovable property for any term not exceeding one year, and leases exempted under section 17
P2256|Instruments transferring or assigning any decree or order of a Court or any award when such decree or order or award purports or operates to create, declare, assign, limit or extinguish, whether in present or in future, any right, title or interest, whether vested or contingent, of a value less than one hundred rupees, to or in immovable property;]
P2257|Instruments (other than wills) which purport or operate to create, declare, assign, limit or extinguish any right, title or interest to or in movable property
P2258|Wills and
P2259|All other documents not required by section 17 to be registered
P2261|Movable Property Registration
P2262|After Successful login, the user shall be directed to the Kaveri Services page. In Kaveri services page, the user shall be required to select the Property Registration option. Under Property Registration, the user is required to select Miscellaneous Document.
P2263|Property Description
P2264|Brief Description
P2265|In the Property Description function, user is required to enter the Property Type with Description and select the Stamp Article, Stamp – Sub Article and Registration Fee Article. Based on the selectin of Article the Book Number will be auto fetched from the system
P2273|Functional Step by Step Process
P2274|Applicant shall select the Miscellaneous Document under Property Registration option.
P2275|After selecting the Miscellaneous Document, user shall be required to enter the Property Type along with the Property Description.
P2276|The user shall select the Stamp Article, Stamp – Sub Article and Registration Fee Article.
P2277|Based on the selectin of Article the Book Number will be auto fetched from the system.
P2278|Next, the user shall select “save and continue” to continue the further process of Registration.
P2280|Stamp Duty and Fee Calculation
P2281|Brief Description
P2282|In the Stamp Duty and Fee Calculation function, user shall enter the consideration amount. Based on the consideration amount, system will calculate and display the Government Duty. Along with this, the user shall be asked to enter details with respect to Exemption, Denotation and Number of pages of the Draft Deed document to complete the Stamp Duty and Fee Calculation
P2284|Functional Step by Step Process
P2286|User shall enter the Consideration Amount. Consideration amount is the total amount involved in the transaction agreed between two parties.
P2287|The system shall display government duty to the user
P2288|Exempted for the duty:
P2290|Case 1:
P2291|In case the user selects ‘exemption on stamp duty’ then the user will be required to select ‘duty exempted’ in either ‘Percentage (%)’ or ‘Rupees (₹)’ and the value in ‘Percentage (%)’ or ‘Rupees (₹)’
P2292|The user will be required to upload exemption certificate issued by the competent authority and enter the certificate number, date, exemption description, denotation amount and Number of pages of draft deed document and annexures
P2293|Based on the details entered by the user, the system shall calculate all the duties and will display the total payable amount
P2295|Case 2:
P2296|In case the user selects denotation if he has paid stamp duty in earlier and subsequent instrument is employed for completion of transaction then duty already paid is denoted and the difference of duty will be calculated by the system automatically
P2297|In case the user does not select ‘exemption on stamp duty’ then the user will be required to enter the denotation amount if it is applicable and Number of pages of draft deed document and annexures, based on that the system shall calculate all the duties and will display the total payable amount
P2298|Party Information
P2299|Brief Description
P2301|The user is required to submit details of all the parties in ‘Party Information’ section. For property registration there shall be two parties: Claimant who may be the buyer, shareholder, co-owner, or transferee and second party would be the executant who may be seller, shareholder, co-owner or transferer. To identify the party some of the below details required:
P2302|Name
P2303|Name of parent/ husband/guardian/ authority holder
P2304|Age
P2305|Gender
P2306|Profession
P2307|Address
P2309|In the party information section, the user shall be required to mention the details of the following parties involve in the property registration process:
P2310|Presenter (Only 1) maybe either claimant or executant or both
P2311|Claimant (1 or multiple)
P2312|Executant (1 or multiple)
P2313|Witness (Minimum 2)
P2316|Functional Step by Step Process
P2318|Select the Party: The user shall select the ‘party’ from the below list to enter the party detail
P2319|Presenter
P2320|Executant
P2321|Consenting Witness
P2323|In case the user selects Presenter option, then the user shall select from either ‘Claimant’ or ‘Executant’ option
P2324|In case the user has selected Executant option, the system will ask, if the user ‘is claimant also?’
P2326|Case 1: If the user has selected ‘yes’ then the system will duplicate the information in the claimant section otherwise it will proceed to next step
P2328|Select Type of Party: The user shall select type of party from either individual or Organization option
P2330|Organization: In case the user has selected the Organization option to enter the party details, the system will prompt the user to enter the below information
P2332|Name of the Organization: The user shall enter the name of the organization
P2333|Year of Incorporation: The applicant shall enter the year of incorporation of the organization
P2334|TAN: The applicant shall enter the TAN of the organization
P2335|Registered Address: The applicant shall enter the registered address of the Organization
P2336|If individual is representing an organization, his personal PAN may not be entered
P2337|After entering the organization information, the system will prompt the user to enter the authorised signatory information
P2339|Individual Information: In case the user has identified himself as an Individual to enter the ‘selected party details’. Then individual option will be selected, and the system will prompt the user to enter below information
P2341|Enter Name: The user shall enter the first name, middle name, and last name in the system
P2342|Enter alias Name: The user shall enter the Alias name in the system
P2343|Relationship: The applicant shall select the relationship type from the dropdown
P2344|Relative Name: As per the Relationship selected by the user, the system will prompt the user to enter relative name
P2345|Enter Age (in Yrs.): The applicant shall enter the age of the party as per the government issued id
P2346|Select Gender: The applicant shall select the gender of the party Male, Female, and other gender from the dropdown
P2347|Enter Profession of the Applicant: The user shall enter the Profession of the applicant
P2348|Address Details: The applicant shall enter House No/Building Name, Street, City/Village, Taluka, District, State, Pin Code
P2349|PAN available: The system will check for the availability of user’s PAN card as per below cases
P2350|Case 1:
P2351|In case the user selected option to enter PAN then system will prompt the user to enter PAN
P2352|Case 2:
P2353|In case the user declares that it doesn’t possess PAN then the system will prompt the user to upload declaration Form 60/61
P2355|Validation: PAN Card number must satisfy the following conditions:
P2356|It should be ten characters long
P2357|The first five characters should be any upper-case alphabets
P2358|The next four-characters should be any number from 0 to 9
P2359|The last character(tenth) should be any upper-case alphabet
P2360|It should not contain any white spaces
P2362|Verify PAN: The system will verify the PAN number entered by the user. In case the PAN is invalid then the system will redirect the user to enter PAN again
P2364|Represented by Power of Attorney:
P2366|Case 1:
P2367|In case the user has not selected ‘Represented by Power of Attorney’ option then the user will save the details and continue with the process
P2368|Case 2:
P2369|In case the user has selected ‘Represented by Power of Attorney’ option then the user shall select either ‘Individual’ or ‘Organization’ and enter the details as per below option:
P2371|Individual Information: In case the user has identified himself as an Individual. Then
P2372|the user shall be required to enter the name (first name, middle name & last name), relationship, age, gender, profession and address in the system
P2373|Organization: In case the user has selected the Organization option, the system will prompt the user to enter the below information
P2375|Organization Name: The user shall enter the name of the organization
P2376|Year of Incorporation: The applicant shall enter the year of incorporation of the organization
P2377|TAN: The applicant shall enter TAN value in the system
P2378|Address Details: The applicant shall enter House No/Building Name, Street, City/Village, Taluka, District, State, Pin Code
P2379|Represented by Authorised Signatory: The user shall be required to enter the first name, middle name, and last name, relationship, relative name and age in the system
P2381|Note: In case the user has selected ‘Organization’ option then ‘Represented for minor   guardian’ and ‘Exemption from Appearance’ option shall not be applicable
P2382|Presenting the document: In property registration, there shall only be a single presenter, who would be presenting the document to Sub-Registrar at the SRO
P2384|Case 1:
P2385|In case the user is presenting the document then the system will allow the user to add new party details and enter executant details
P2386|Case 2:
P2387|In case the user is not presenting the document then the system will allow the user to select type of party
P2389|Represented for minor guardian: In case the user has selected ‘Yes’ to ‘Represented for minor guardian’ option then the user shall enter name, age, and relationship
P2391|Exemption from Appearance (Section 88): In case the user could not be present at Sub Registrar office on date and time of the document registration and the applicant possess the exemption letter from the distinguish authority, then
P2392|Case 1:
P2393|In case the user has selected ‘Yes’ in ‘Exemption from Appearance’ option then the user shall be required to enter ‘exemption reference letter number’ and upload exemption reference letter. Section 88 exemption is applicable only for court and certain functionaries as per notification of the department and provision of the law
P2395|Add Party: After filling details of claimant/ executant, the user shall have an option to add multiple individuals in both the section otherwise user could save the party information and move forward
P2403|Party Information - Identifiers
P2406|Functional Step by Step Process
P2408|In case the user is an identifier, then in the application the following identifier details shall be required:

P2416|After user enters above identifier details, system shall ask the user if number of identifiers is greater than equal to two, as for executing the property registration document, at-least 2 identifiers would be required
P2418|Case 1:

P2421|Case 2:

P2426|User Interface (UI)
P2427|Presenter

P2438|Document Summary – Applicant (Online)
P2440|Brief Description
P2441|It is the document summary of party information, property information, valuation of the property and calculation of stamp duty & fees and also contains witnesses’ particulars along with the check & verification of align data before sending it to the SRO
P2445|Functional Step by Step Process

P2451|Court order details
P2452|Liability details
P2453|Market Valuation and Fee calculation details
P2454|Party(s) details (Claimant, Executant, Identifier(s) details)
P2455|Upload Deed Document Along with Enclosures: The user shall be required to upload the deed document along with the enclosers in the application. The enclosure documents shall be according to the articles and type of registration
P2456|The date of execution of the document also needs to be selected by the applicant
P2457|The user shall be asked to select the Sub-Registrar office from the dropdown, and the application shall be forwarded to the selected SRO for evaluation
P2459|The user shall verify the above details in order to check any data entry mistakes, the user shall be required to accept the declaration and only after then the application shall be submitted for approval.
P2462|User Interface (UI)
P2470|CERTIFIED COPY (CC) SEARCH
P2472|Brief Description
P2473|Certified copy is the copy of the registered scanned document. This copy is signed by SR. Certified Copy will be generated for the documents which are coming under Book 1 of Document Registration, Marriage Registration and Marriage Notice. For the Certified Copy stamp duty need to be paid and has to be certified as per Section 10A of Stamp Act. The certificate of Stamp is as per Appendix BA under Rule 10A of Stamp Rules.
P2477|Functional Step by Step Process
P2479|Applicant shall select Certified Copy, after which the applicant will get an option to select below option:
P2480|Search by Blockchain key: The applicant shall enter Blockchain ID/ Card Number and search. The system shall display search result and continue with the process
P2481|Search by Document details: The applicant shall select Document Type (Document Type will have Document Registration, Marriage Registration, Marriage Notice), District, Sub Registrar Office, Book Type (Book-1, Book-1 Part-I Memos, Book-1 Part-III(a), Book-1 Part-III(b), Book-1 Part-IV, Book-1 Part-V, Documents as per Rule 17(2) & (3), Book-1 Part-VI), Document Number and Year of Registration. After which system shall fetch data from Master Database
P2482|OTP Verification:
P2483|Case 1:
P2484|In case OTP is verified by the system then the applicant will search for CC and system will generate the Registered Deed Document. The applicant will accept the result and proceed
P2486|Case 2:
P2487|In case OTP is not verified by the system then the applicant will be asked to enter OTP again
P2488|CC Payment
P2489|Brief Description
P2490|Once the Certified Copy is found, user shall be directed to do the Payment to get the Digitally Signed Certified Copy.
P2492|Functional Step by Step Process
P2493|Applicant shall select Payment Details, after which the system will display below details:
P2494|Application Fee
P2495|Search Fee
P2496|Stamp Duty
P2497|The system will display Application Number and Application Type
P2498|The user shall enter Remitter Name and select SRO office name from the drop down. The user will then proceed to payment. System shall be redirected to Khajane-2
P2499|Payment Confirmation:
P2500|Case 1:
P2501|In case Payment is confirmed then the system will generate transaction receipt. Applicant will be able to print the receipt
P2504|Case 2:
P2505|In case Payment is not confirmed by the system then the applicant will be redirected to retry and proceed for payment again
P2507|CC Online Application Summary
P2508|Brief Description
P2509|Once the payment is done, the application details will be displayed on the system for the applicant to verify the details entered.
P2513|Functional Step by Step Process
P2514|The document summary will be displayed to the user and the elements of the summary page will be displayed by the system
P2515|System will display application details such as application number & application type and applicant details such as Name and Aadhar number
P2516|System will display Input Search Parameters, Payment details and transaction receipt details in document summary
P2517|Applicant will be able to see the preview of the application. Then it will be sent to SRO for Approval
P2518|CC Generation
P2519|Brief Description
P2520|CC is the copy of Registered Deed Document. Upon search, as per the input parameters entered by the user the Registered Deed Document will be displayed. The user shall submit the Application to Sub Registrar for Approval. Once the Sub Registrar approves the application, the user will be able download the digitally signed CC.
P2523|Functional Step by Step Process
P2525|SDA
P2526|Applicant shall submit the application for online CC.
P2527|The application shall be scrutinized by the SDA as per the parameters entered by the applicant in the system
P2528|As per the details entered in the application, SDA will prepare the CC
P2529|If it is found correct, SDA shall view and download the CC
P2530|After downloading, SDA shall Digitally Sign the CC by using Adobe Acrobat
P2531|After signing the CC, SDA shall update the document with remarks and send it to FDA for further Approval
P2532|FDA
P2533|FDA shall receive the digitally signed CC from SDA
P2534|FDA shall validate the CC details as per the parameters entered by the applicant in the system
P2535|If it is found correct, FDA shall view and download the CC. If it is not found correct FDA shall revert the application back to the SDA
P2536|After downloading, FDA shall Digitally Sign the CC by using Adobe Acrobat
P2537|After signing the CC, FDA shall update the document and send it to Sub Registrar for further Approval
P2538|Sub Registrar
P2539|Sub Registrar shall receive the digitally signed CC from FDA
P2540|Sub Registrar shall validate the CC details as per the parameters entered by the applicant in the system
P2541|If it is found correct, Sub Registrar shall view and download the CC. If it is not found correct Sub Registrar shall revert the application back to the FDA
P2542|After downloading, Sub Registrar shall Digitally Sign the CC by using Adobe Acrobat
P2543|After signing the CC, Sub Registrar shall update the document and send it to the Applicant
P2544|Applicant shall view and download the Digitally Signed CC
P2548|COURT ORDER ENTRY MODULE
P2549|Brief Description
P2550|Department communication with the Court Orders a is statutory requirement. If there are any refraining orders of the Court to restrict Registration, the Department should comply with the orders of the Court to honor the directions of the Court.
P2551|Search and Add Court Order
P2552|Brief Description
P2553|The Court Order details are searched against the Property in the system. If any Orders found in the system, the details shall be displayed. Otherwise, Sub Registrar can add the Court Order details into the system
P2559|Functional Step by Step Process
P2560|Sub Registrar shall click on Search Court Order details.
P2561|Sub Registrar needs to enter the Court Order number to search.
P2562|System shall search the Court Order details in the system.
P2563|If the order found in the system, system shall display the order details (Order Number, Issuing Authority, Issue Date, Description, Article, Cancelled or In Force)
P2564|If the order is not found, system shall display the message “No Result Found”.
P2565|If the order is not found in the system, Sub Registrar shall add the court order details.
P2566|Sub Registrar shall enter the Court Order Details such as Court Order number, Issuing Authority, Registration Article, Date of Issue of Order, Order Description, Order status (In Force or Cancelled). Then, system shall auto save the Application Details
P2567|After entering order details, Sub Registrar shall enter the Property Details such as, Type of Property (Agriculture/Non-Agriculture), District, Taluk, Hobli, Village, Boundary Details (East, West, North, South), Property Description, Area of the Property with Measurement Unit, Extent Description (If available), Property Number Type and Property Number Details.
P2568|If the Sub Registrar wants to add more details with respect to the Property, he can click on Add More Details and he shall enter the Property Number Type and Property Number Details
P2569|If the Sub Registrar wants to add more Property, he can click on Add More Property and shall enter the required Property details
P2570|After entering Property Details, Sub Registrar shall enter the Party Details such as First Name, Middle Name, Last Name, Age and Address
P2571|If the Sub Registrar wants to add more Parties he can click on Add More Parties and shall enter the required Party details
P2572|If No, Sub Registrar shall Save the details and Submit.
P2573|Once the details are submitted, the Court Order Details get added into the system.
P2575|Edit Court Order
P2576|Brief Description
P2577|If there are any modifications in the existing Court Order, Sub Registrar can edit the Court Orders in the system.
P2580|Functional Step by Step Process
P2581|System shall display the Court Order details (Order Number, Issuing Authority, Issue Date, Description, Article, Cancelled or In Force)
P2582|Sub Registrar shall click on edit Court Order details
P2583|Sub Registrar shall be able to edit the Court Order Details such as Court Order number, Issuing Authority, Registration Article, Date of Issue of Order, Order Description, Order status (In Force or Cancelled). Then, system shall auto save the Application Details
P2584|After editing the order details, Sub Registrar shall be able to edit the Property Details such as, Type of Property (Agriculture/Non-Agriculture), District, Taluk, Hobli, Village, Boundary Details (East, West, North, South), Property Description, Area of the Property with Measurement Unit, Extent Description (If available), Property Number Type and Property Number Details.
P2585|If the Sub Registrar wants to add more details with respect to the Property, he can click on Add More Details and he shall enter/edit the Property Number Type and Property Number Details
P2586|If the Sub Registrar wants to add more Property, he can click on Add More Property and shall enter/edit the required Property details
P2587|After editing the Property Details, Sub Registrar shall be able to edit the Party Details such as First Name, Middle Name, Last Name, Age and Address
P2588|If the Sub Registrar wants to add more Parties he can click on Add More Parties and shall enter/edit the required Party details
P2589|If No, Sub Registrar shall Save the details and Submit.
P2590|Once the details are submitted, the Court Order Details get updated into the system.
P2592|Cancel Court Order
P2593|Brief Description
P2594|If the restrictions by the Court Order is cancelled, then Sub Registrar shall enter the cancellation order in the system.
P2596|Functional Step by Step Process
P2597|System shall display the Court Order details (Order Number, Issuing Authority, Issue Date, Description, Article, Cancelled or In Force)
P2598|Sub Registrar shall click on cancel Court Order details
P2599|Sub Registrar shall be able to cancel the Court Order by entering details such as Court Order number, Issuing Authority, Date of Cancellation of Order and remarks
P2600|After entering all the details, Sub Registrar shall click on Save and Submit. Then, the details get saved into the system
P2601|User Interface
P2602|Search and Add Court Order
