P2615|LIABILITY MODULE
P2616|Brief Description
P2617|Liability is the arrears of deficit stamp duty and registration fee arising due to undervaluation of the property, mis classification of documents, review of orders of the Deputy Commissioners of Stamp by Chief Controlling Revenue Authority (CCRA) and also observation made by the audit authorities. This deficit of stamp duty and registration fee is the Liability to be recorded on the property
P2618|Search Liability
P2619|Brief Description
P2620|The Liability details are searched against the Property in the system. If any details found in the system, the details shall be displayed. Otherwise, Sub Registrar can add the Liability details into the system on the orders of the District Registrar
P2621|After result display, two options shall be displayed to the user. Show similar property and show exact property. If the user selects similar property, list of all the similar properties with respect to the property number shall be displayed and from this list user can select the required property. If the user selects show exact property, the exact property with respect to the property number shall be displayed
P2623|Functional Step by Step Process
P2624|Sub Registrar shall click on Search Liability details.
P2625|Sub Registrar needs to select SRO Name, Book Number, Financial year and enter the Document Number to search.
P2626|System shall search the property details in the system.
P2627|If the property is found in the system, system shall display the property along with the Liability details (Issue Date, Issued By, Liability amount, Order Number and Liability Note)
P2628|If the property is not found, system shall display the message “No Data Found”.
P2629|Once the result is displayed, system shall show two options. Show Similar Property and Show Exact Property
P2630|If the user selects similar property, list of all the similar properties with respect to the property number shall be displayed and from this list user can select the required property. If the user selects show exact property, the exact property with respect to the property number shall be displayed
P2631|As per the selection property details shall be displayed to the user
P2633|Add Liability
P2634|Brief Description
P2635|Upon search, if the Liability details on the property are not found in the system, Sub Registrar can add the Liability details to the property.
P2637|Functional Step by Step Process
P2638|From the list of Similar Properties and the Exact Property, Sub Registrar needs to select the required property for which the liability details have to be added
P2639|Sub Registrar shall click on Add Liability details.
P2640|Sub Registrar need to enter Issue Date, Issued By, Liability Amount, Order Number and Liability Note
P2641|After entering the liability details on the property, Sub Registrar shall click on Save button to save the details
P2648|Liability Report
P2649|Brief Description
P2650|Liability Report shall be provided to each Sub Registrar to view the Liabilities entered on the property for the required period.
P2652|Functional Step by Step Process
P2653|Sub Registrar shall click on Liability report
P2654|Sub Registrar need to select SRO Name and From Date and To Date for which he needs to view the report
P2655|Sub Registrar shall click on Get Details to view the report
P2656|System shall display all the Liability entries made during that period. Each Liability entry has the following details such as Order Number, Liability Note, Final Registration Number, Property Schedule, Property Number Details and Village/ Town
P2657|After getting the details, Sub Registrar can take print of the report by click on Print report
P2659|User Interface
P2674|CROSS REFERENCE MODULE
P2675|Brief Description
P2676|On the Registration of a document which revokes or cancels or ratifies an error, in, or modifies the terms of a document previously registered in the same class or register book or a return of a lands acquired under the Land Acquisition Act or of a document received and filed under Section 89(Rule 17), or on the receipt of a communication from a Revenue Officer or from a Court which intimates a similar revocations, cancellation, ratification or modification, a note shall be entered at the foot of the entry of the later document or communication.
P2678|Add Cross Reference
P2679|Brief Description
P2680|Once document is finally registered it can’t be altered but in certain situations like
P2681|cancellation of document by court or communication by Revenue Commissioner or
P2682|rectification it becomes mandatory to mark reference of subsequent document in the
P2683|amended/modified/cancelled (previously registered) document.
P2686|Functional Step by Step Process
P2687|Before this process starts, the document to be cancelled/modified must be registered and scanned using KAVERI application.
P2688|Subsequent document of amendment/correction/cancellation follows the same process of registration as that followed for normal registration.
P2689|Sub Registrar shall click on Add Cross Reference
P2690|Sub Registrar needs to select Document Type
P2691|System shall display two Document Types such as Kaveri Registration Document and Endorsement
P2692|The registration system displays the options to select transaction type such as Modification, Rectification, Cancellation and Revocation
P2693|The registration system displays the options to select “Through” from where the cross references are originated (Court Order/Revenue Order, etc)
P2694|The registration system shows all the required information necessary to add a reference on the registered as well as previous document which is being modified/cancelled.
P2695|SR/FDA enters required details on to the displayed form.
P2696|System finds the original scanned image locations (i.e., Main Backup, Scanned etc.).
P2697|System internally links these two documents by adding references.
P2698|System alters scanned images identified in all above locations by adding a watermark with alteration description.
P2699|System also internally establish links in Index II table. Whenever, the linked document in index II is referred for encumbrance information, the reference to amending/cancelling document and vice-versa also appears in the search result.
P2700|Whenever a linked document is viewed or printed, the system displays/prints the information that the said document is amended/cancelled by the referred document having the specified document number and vice-versa.
P2701|Reference text for Kaveri Registration Document will be like
P2702|For Rectification
P2703|New Document Reference - This Document Rectifies by Document No. BMH-1-07916-2010-11”
P2704|Old Document Reference - This Document has been Rectified by Document No. BMH-1-07916-2010-11”
P2705|For Modification
P2706|New Document Reference - This Document modifies Document No. BMH-1-07916-2010-11”
P2707|Old Document Reference - This Document has been modified by Document No. BMH-1-07916-2010-11”
P2708|For Cancelled
P2709|New Document Reference - This Document Cancels Document No. BMH-1-07916-2010-11”
P2710|Old Document Reference - This Document has been Cancelled by Document No. BMH-1-07916-2010-11”
P2711|For Revocation
P2712|New Document Reference - This Document Revokes by Document No. BMH-1-07916-2010-11”
P2713|Old Document Reference - This Document has been Revoked by Document No. BMH-1-07916-2010-11”
P2714|Through Court
P2715|New Document Reference – “The communication received from Civil Judge and JMFC Court No. OS No120/2010 dated 08/04/2022 Cancels the Document No.CKB-1-00520-2017-18”
P2716|Old Document Reference – “This Document has been cancelled through communication received from Court No. OS No120/2010 dated 08/-4/2022 and filed as Document No. CKB-1-00120-2022-23“
P2718|Reference text for Endorsement will be like “Endorsement Date DD/MM/YYYY Certified that deficit Stamp Duty of Rs.1500 Registration Fee of Rs.500 has been paid by Sri/Smt Rajesh challan No.120 dt 09/04/2022 of SBI order no 12/20-21 dt 08/04/2022 of the DRO”
P2720|User Interface
P2723|User Interface through Court Orders
P2730|CONFIGURATION MODULE
P2732|Brief Description
P2733|Configuration module is the administrative tool for the management of the officer and employee’s duty, role, and responsibility within the department.
P2735|Transfer Management
P2737|Brief Description
P2738|The officers working on various designations are being transferred to different offices of Sub-Registrar, District Registrar, and IGR. The Transfer Management tool shall be working for the proper management of the transfer processes within the offices of the department.
P2742|Transfer Request raised by the District Registrar
P2745|Functional Step by Step Process
P2746|The transfer request will be raised by the District Registrar in the system and for which the DR shall login in the Kaveri account and click on the Transfer and Postings
P2747|To raise the transfer request to the IGR (Admin), the details of the employee shall be required
P2748|The user shall enter the Employee ID/Name, and based on the suggestive search, the user will select the employee’s id/name
P2749|The designation and current posting location shall be auto fetched and displayed to the user
P2750|After which the user shall be required to enter the new transfer location and the duration of new posting of the employee
P2752|The DR shall also be required to enter the reason for the transfer in the given space
P2753|After verifying the details, the DR shall submit the request in the system and the transfer request shall be sent to the IGR (Admin) for final approval
P2755|Employee Transfer History
P2758|Functional Step by Step Process
P2759|The District Registrar shall be given access to check the transfer history of all the employees that directly/in-directly report to him
P2760|To check the transfer details, the user shall be required to enter the Employee ID/Name, and based on the suggestive search, the user will select the employee’s id/name and press the Search button
P2761|The system shall generate the employee transfer history along with the location, reason, and time period for which the employee gets transfer to the new location
P2763|Employee Transfer Request (Only IGR/Admin)
P2765|Functional Step by Step Process
P2766|The IGR/Admin shall be given access to approve/reject the request received from the District Registrar office
P2767|To check all the request received, the user will Login to the account and select Transfer and Postings
P2768|Under the Transfer and Postings section, the option of “Employee Transfer Requests” under which all the requests received from the District Registrar shall be displayed and the required decision (approved/rejected) needs to be taken by the user
P2771|Case 1:
P2772|If IGR/Admin approve the transfer request, then the notification shall be triggered to the DR and to the transferred employee. The employee record shall be updated as per the new information in the database
P2774|Case 2:
P2775|If IGR/Admin user decide to reject the transfer request in the system, then the user shall be required to enter the reason for rejecting the request
P2776|After submitting the request, a notification shall be triggered in the system to the DR user account from which the request have been initiated and the reason for rejection will also be visible to the user
P2778|User Interface (UI)
P2784|Leave Management
P2786|Brief Description
P2787|Leave management is the set of processes that controls the leave application, approval and tracking of employee leave in the Department of Stamps & Registration.
P2802|Apply for Leave
P2804|Functional Step by Step Process
P2805|The leave request could be raised by the SDA/FDA/SR/DR/AIGR/DIGR in the system and for which the user shall login in the Kaveri account and click on the Leave Management
P2806|The total number of leaves utilised/available shall be displayed to the user, according to which the user could raise the request in the system
P2807|To raise the leave request in the system, the name of the employee and reporting authority shall be auto fetched and displayed to the user
P2808|The user shall be required to select the Leave type (Casual/Planned) from the dropdown and dates for which the user is applying for leaves
P2809|After the date’s selection from the user, the total number of days shall be calculated and displayed to the user for confirmation
P2810|After all the details are filled, the user shall be required to enter the reason for which he/she would like to avail the leaves and briefly describe the reason in the given space
P2811|The status of the leave (approved/rejected) shall be communicated to the user in the dashboard as well as in the notification bar
P2813|Validation of Leave Management System:
P2814|Casual Leaves – 10 leaves in a year (Jan -Dec) + 2 RH (Restricted Holidays)
P2815|Earned Leaves – 15 leaves (Jan – June) + 15 leaves (Jul – Dec) – These leaves shall be carried forwards to the next session
P2816|Note: Maximum carried forward leaves could not extend 340 leaves for an individual
P2817|Maternity Leave – For a maximum of 6 months for female employees and could only be availed for 2 children/time
P2818|Paternity Leave – It could be availed by male employees for a maximum period of 15 days
P2820|For Earned, Maternity and Paternity leaves total number of days will be counted.
P2821|For the Casual Leaves, the holidays shall not be including, to calculate the number of leaves applied
P2822|SDA and FDA shall apply for the leaves to the reporting SR. In case, if SR is not available then the Incharge shall act as the approving authority.
P2823|The leave request raised by the SR shall be approved by DR
P2824|The leave request raised by DR, AIGR, DIGR shall be approved by IGR
P2838|Leave Approval Request
P2840|Functional Step by Step Process
P2841|Only SR, DR and IGR shall have the rights to approve the leave request in the system for the officers reporting to them. For accepting the request, the user shall login in the Kaveri account and click on the Leave Management
P2842|In the Leave management dashboard, notification shall be displayed if any new request has been received for approval
P2844|The user shall be required to click on “Approval Requests” tab for accessing the pending requests assigned for the final approval
P2845|The Leave Approval Request dashboard shall display all the requests received from the officers in ascending order
P2846|The user needs to take decision and approve or reject the request as per the department policies
P2848|Case 1:
P2849|In case, the user decided to approve the leave request, then the user shall confirm and press the “Approve” button
P2850|The leave approval notification shall be sent to the requesting officer and the status shall be updated in the Leave management dashboard
P2852|Case 2:
P2853|If the user decides to reject the leave request in the system, then the user shall be required to enter the reason for rejecting the request
P2854|After submitting the request, a notification shall be triggered in the system to the requesting officer account from which the request have been initiated and the status shall be updated in the Leave management dashboard
P2856|User Interface (UI)
P2860|SECTION 68 (2) CORRECTION MODULE
P2862|Brief Description
P2864|In addition to the information below, please find a detailed requirements document attached:
P2865|https://csgkarnataka.sharepoint.com/:b:/r/sites/ULMS/Knowledge%20Base/FRS/68%20(2)%20correction%20Module%20FRS_V2.pdf?csf=1&web=1&e=Bvwfha
P2866|Please use the above link to access section 68(2) Filing detailed FRS.
P2868|Every Registrar shall have an authority to issue (whether on complaint or otherwise), any order consistent with this act which he considers necessary in respect of any act or omission of any Sub-Registrar sub-ordinate to him or in respect of the rectification of any error regarding the Book or the office in which any document have been registered.
P2870|Correction Request Sent by SR
P2872|Brief Description
P2873|The property owners shall submit the request to Sub-Registrar Office to rectify the correction in the document, which could have been occurred during the document registration process. For which the SR will check the property description/details and the registered deed document. There are 4 types of corrections which could be made in the registered document:
P2874|Text Correction
P2875|Property Number Correction
P2876|Village Correction
P2877|Party Name Correction
P2891|Correction Request Sent by Sub-Registrar
P2893|Functional Step by Step Process
P2894|The request would be raised to District Registrar (DR), for which the Sub-Registrar shall Log in to the system and select 68(2) Correction Note
P2897|The system shall re-direct the user to the Correction Module and the SRO details are auto fetched and displayed
P2898|The user shall enter the document registration number, based on which the Book Type and FY of registration shall be auto fetched and displayed to the user for confirmation
P2899|After the details are confirmed, then the user shall initiate the search and the property details mapped to the document shall be displayed
P2900|After which the SR shall select the type of correction required as per the request of the property owner and add the correction note
P2901|The SR shall also scan and upload the registered deed document presented by the owners, which will be uploaded in the correction request and along with the details saved for the selected indicators
P2902|After final verification of the details, SR shall submit the request to DR for approval and issue the DR order for the same
P2904|User Interface
P2908|Correction Order Submit by DR
P2910|Brief Description
P2911|The District Registrar shall issue an order based on the remarks received from SR for corrections in the Index II Entry or property description of the registered document.
P2914|Functional Step by Step Process
P2915|The request would be raised by Sub-Registrar (SR) and Sent to DR for which the District Registrar shall Log in to the system and select 68(2) Correction Note
P2916|The system shall re-direct the user to the Correction Module and where all the requests received from the Sub-Registrar Offices will be displayed
P2917|The user shall select an application and view the registered deed document which is submitted by the SR for verification purpose
P2918|After which the user will select the Add Order button to submit the DR order
P2919|The user shall enter the order number, select order date and upload DR order for the correction request received from the SR
P2920|The user shall also enter the Correction Note U/S 68(2) and after confirmation of the details the user shall submit the order details
P2921|The notification will be sent to SR to make the correction in the document
P2922|User Interface
P2926|Correction Note U/S 68(2) Submit by SR
P2928|Brief Description
P2929|The Sub-Registrar shall enter the correction note in the property description as per the order of the District Registrar.
P2932|Functional Step by Step Process
P2933|As per the order submitted by the District Registrar, the corrections shall be made in the document U/S 68(2), for which the SR shall Log in to the system and select 68(2) Correction Note
P2934|The system shall re-direct the user to the Correction Module and where the correction orders received from the DR shall be displayed with the following details:
P2935|Document Number
P2936|DR Order Number
P2937|Order Date
P2938|Order Updated Date and Time
P2939|Section 68(2) Note
P2940|The user shall be required to view the order submitted by the District Registrar, after which SR shall continue to Add Correction details as per the order
P2941|In case Text Correction is selected, then the 68(2) Correction note needs to be mentioned by the SR and submit
P2942|In case Property Number or Village Correction is selected, then the property details shall be displayed, and the user could change the following parameters:
P2943|Village/Area
P2944|Hobli
P2945|Current Property Number and Type
P2946|Old Property Number and Type
P2947|Note: If multiple DR order are submitted against the same property, then the SR shall have the functionality to deactivate the previous orders
P2948|In case Party Name Correction is selected, the details of all the parties shall be displayed and the user could update the name of the presenter, claimant and executant
P2949|Note: Edit option in the existing party details shall not be allowed, only the updated name of the party shall be mentioned by SR as per the DR’s order
P2950|After final verification of all the details, the SR shall click on “Finalize” button to submit the details and the correction note shall be appended in EC and the document

P2982|Section 64: Sub-Registrar who has registered non testamentary document relating to immovable property not wholly situated in his own sub-district shall make memorendum there off and of the endorsement and certificate if any there on, and send the same to any other sub-registrar, sub-ordinate to the same registrar as himself in whose sub-distrct any part of search property situated and such sub-registar shall file the memorendum in his Book – I (Part 1 of Book I as per rule 17)
P2989|Functional Step by Step Process for SECTION 64 Memorandum
P2991|The originating Sub Registrar follows the normal process to get the document registered and registers the document
P2992|The originating Sub Registrar after registration of the document containing the property of the other Sub Registrar jurisdiction, shall generate the Forms 25 and 26 by clicking on the ‘Generate Forms 25 & 26’ button.
P2993|The Forms 25 and 26 shall get displayed on the Sub Registrar’s dashboard along with the particulars
P2994|The Sub Registrar shall Digitally Sign by clicking on ‘Digital Sign’ button and shall send to the other (concerned) jurisdictional Sub Registrar
P2995|(Note: The other (concerned) jurisdictional Sub Registrar’s address should auto-populate based on the Index Village or Road Master) to send Forms 25 and 26)
P2996|The Forms 25 and 26 should appear in the dashboard of the other (concerned) jurisdictional Sub Registrar
P2997|The other (concerned) jurisdictional Sub Registrar shall click on ‘View Forms 25 and 26’ button to view the same and shall file Form 25 by clicking on the ‘File Form 25’ button; the filing document number along with date gets generated both in Forms 25 and 26
P2998|The other (concerned) jurisdictional Sub Registrar shall view Forms 25 and 26 by clicking on ‘View Forms 25 and 26 for Digital Sign’ button, and shall Digitally Sign by clicking on ‘Digital Sign’ button and shall send digitally signed Form 26 to the originating Sub Registrar by clicking on ‘Send back to Originating SR’ button
P2999|The Form 26 should appear in the dashboard of the originating Sub Registrar
P3000|The originating Sub Registrar shall view the digitally signed Form 26 by clicking on ‘View Digitally Signed Form 26’ button and shall file Form 26 by clicking on ‘File Form 26 and Complete’ button
P3002|Brief Description
P3004|Section 65: Procedure where documents relates to in several districts (1), every Sub-Registrar on registering a Non-testamentary document relating to immovable property situated in more districts than 1 shall also forward a copy there off and of the endorsement and certificate (if any) there on, together with a copy of the map or plan (if any) mentioned in section 21, to Registrar of every district in which any part of such property is situated other than the district in which his own Sub-District is situated
P3006|(2), the Registrar on receiving the same shall file in his Book – I, the copy of the document and the copy of the map are plan (if any), and shall forward a memorundum of the document to each of the Sub-Registrars sub-ordinate to him, within whose Sub-District any part of such property is situate; and every sub-registrar receiving such memorundum shall file it in his Book – I.

P3027|Brief Description
P3029|Section 66: Procedure after registration of documents relating to land—
P3030|(1) On registering any non-testamentary document relating to immovable properly, the Registrar shall forward a memorandum of such document to each Sub-Registrar subordinate to himself in whose sub-district any part of the property is situate
P3031|(2) The Registrar shall also forward a copy of such document, together with a copy of the map or plan (if any) mentioned in section 21, to every other Registrar in whose district any part of such property is situate
P3032|(3) Such Registrar on receiving any such copy shall file it in his Book No. 1, and shall also send a memorandum of the copy to each of the Sub-Registrars subordinate to him within whose sub-district any part of the property is situate
P3033|(4) Every Sub-Registrar receiving any memorandum under this section shall file it in his Book No.

P3068|Book 1 Filing Part II - For copies of maps or plans mentioned in Section 21
P3070|Brief Description
P3072|(1) No non-testamentary document relating to immovable property shall be accepted for registration unless it contains a description of such property sufficient to identify the same.
P3073|(2) Houses in towns shall be described as situate on the north or other side of the street or road (which should be specified) to which they front, and by their existing and former occupancies, and by their numbers if the houses in such street or road are numbered.
P3074|(3) Other houses and lands shall be described by their name, if any, and as being the territorial division in which they are situate, and by their superficial contents, the roads and other properties on to which they abut, and their existing occupancies, and also, whenever it is practicable, by reference to a Government map or survey.
P3075|(4) No non-testamentary document containing a map or plan of any property comprised therein shall be accepted for registration unless it is accompanied by a true copy of the map or plan, or, in case such property is situate in several districts, by such number of true copies of the map or plan as are equal to the number of such districts.
P3079|Functional Step by Step Process
P3081|The Sub Registrar follows the normal process to get the document registered and registers the document
P3082|After the SR ‘Checks and Registers’, the document number gets generated
P3083|The SR shall allocate to DEO to Print the Endorsement by clicking on ‘Print Endorsement’ button (as in the usual process of registration)
P3084|(Note: But along with it, the print option to print the document number on Maps and Plans has to be provided)
P3085|The DEO shall print document number on Maps and Plans by clicking on ‘Print Document No. on Maps & Plans’ button
P3086|While scanning the Deed Document by clicking on the ‘Scan’ button, the Plans and Maps must also be scanned along with

P3206|Filing of documents through FRUITS application
P3207|Filing of documents through FRUITS application is the process for facilitating the Registration of Mortagage deed and Filing of copy Form-3 declaration for loans or advances sanctioned by the Commercial banks, Rural Banks and Cooperative Societies for agriculture purpose
P3209|Filing of Form 3 for Agriculture Loan through FRUITS
P3210|Brief Description
P3211|If the loan amount is less the 3 lakh those documents will be considered under Form 3 for Filing. Filing of Mortgage deed through Form 3 does not require party to be present in the office of the Sub Registrar. Since this is with respect to agriculture loan, Government has given exemption of Stamp Duty and Registration Fee. Hence there is no Market Value Calculation, Stamp Duty and Registration Fee
P3213|Functional Step by Step Process
P3214|Sub Registrar shall login to the Kaveri system and clicks on “Filing of documents from FRUITS”
P3215|The system shall display the detailed page which has Form-3, Form-3 with Court Orders, Mortgage & Other and Status of the filing document
P3216|Sub Registrar shall select Form 3 document for Filing
P3219|After clicking on Form 3, all the Form 3 requests submitted by various applicants to banks are displayed. The banks create applications in FRUITS application (developed by NIC).  SRO views applications submitted via FRUITS on SRO dashboard. Each Form 3 document has the following details
P3220|View Form 3 – It is a digitally signed Form, received from bank (FRUITS) which has information on Date of receipt, Bank details (Bank Name), Farmer related details (party details—Name, Address), Property details (survey number, extent), Loan amount (Consideration Amount)
P3226|View Summary – Same details of Form 3 which is generated in Kaveri application based on the Bhoomi data
P3231|Document received details (Date and Time), Reference Number and Deed Type
P3232|The Sub Registrar shall accept the document for filing by digitally signing on the Form 3 document
P3233|Filing data should be stored in Book 1 Part IV
P3234|After Digitally Signing, an XML file along with the reference number shall be sent to Bhoomi
P3236|Filing of Form 3 if there is a Court Order on the property
P3238|When the Form 3 application is received from Fruits, auto search will happen to find the court orders
P3239|If the Court Order is found, the Sub Registrar has to decide whether to Register or Reject the application
P3240|If it is accepted, the Document gets Filed
P3241|If it is rejected, then the rejected status shall be sent back to the FRUITS application through XML file
P3244|Mortgage Deed through FRUITS
P3246|Brief Description
P3247|If the Loan amount advanced by the Bank is more the 3 lakh, then those documents will be considered as Mortgage Deed documents. Such documents should follow the usual process of registration. It doesn’t come under filing of documents.

P3311|MEMO CONCEPT
P3313|In case of change of Jurisdiction during Registration of Document, then the Origination SR (ie., Registration SR ie., R-SR) sends a MEMO to the Jurisdictional SR (J-SR)
P3316|Registration SR (R-SR) Login
P3317|1. 2 Buttons shall be present in the Registration SR (R-SR) Login - 'Print' & 'Send' buttons
P3318|2. Here, Print button for printing the Form 25
P3319|and Send button for marked as sent by R-SR to Jurisdictional SR (J-SR)
P3322|Jurisdictional SR (J-SR) Login
P3323|1. 3 Buttons shall be present in the Jurisdictional SR (J-SR) Login - 'View', 'Accept' and 'Print' buttons
P3324|2. Here, 'View' button for viewing the Form 25
P3325|'Accept' button for accepting the Form 25 and sending back Form 26 to the R-SR
P3326|'Print' button for printing the Forms 25 and 26
P3328|On clicking on 'Accept' button, the following things must happen:
P3329|1. Creation/Generation of New Document ID
P3330|2. Execution Date - will be the Same
P3331|3. Presentation Date - will change
P3332|4. Stamp 1 to Stamp 5 Date & Time - as Current Dates only
P3333|5. CD No. ()
P3334|6. R-SRO will be changed to J-SRO
P3335|7. Document No. & Final Registration No. Generated
P3336|8. All the Transaction Tables must be Updated Simultaneously - Document Master, Party Master, Property Master, Property Schedule Master, Property No. Details (Tables)
P3337|9. Update Document Master with Final Registration No., Book Type and Part Details
P3338|10. Generate Form 26 and send the same to R-SR
P3340|Registration SR (R-SR) Login
P3341|1. Generate and Print Endorsement at R-SR - must Print Only 60 Certificate
P3342|2. Replace OLD Registration No. with NEW Registration No.
P3346|(ii) A separate file shall also be opened for filing copies translations presented under sections 19 and 62 of the act or under rule 12(1). The copies and translation placed in this file shall be connected by a cross reference with the entry in the register
P3347|As per the provision of the Act, the copies of the translation can be scanned along with the original document and stored
P3349|(iii) A separate file shall also be maintained for communications received from officers of other departments intimating the cancellation, modification or rectification of transactions evidenced by papers previously filed or registered
P3350|As per the provision of the Act, the copies of the translation can be scanned along with the original document and stored

P3359|EXCEPTIONAL CASES
P3361|Attendance at Private Residences (Private Attendance)
P3363|Brief Description:
P3364|In the exigencies, ill-health or detained in custody, if requested for visiting to private residence on payment of private residence fee and conveyance, the SR visits the private residence of the party or detention place and registers the document.
P3366|Process Flow Diagram:
P3369|Figure: Process Flow Diagram for Private Attendance

P3384|After the DEO prints and uploads the document summary, the SR confirmation is required
P3385|Document Registration
P3386|But the SR keeps the document pending (‘Document Kept Pending’) during the document registration process where the reason for pending gets recorded in the Minute Book and Endorsement is printed on the document
P3387|(Note: Once the DEO prints the Endorsement, the pending status should revert back to SR login)
P3388|Exceptional Cases
P3389|As the document is kept pending, it comes under exceptional case
P3390|The applicant has to request for private attendance (either before or after submission of application form) depending on the exigencies (an application is made for the same, if required) along with Payment of Private Attendance Fee & Medical Certificate
P3391|On payment of fee for private attendance, the application gets retrieved from the Minute Book
P3392|(Note: 1. Rs.1000 per person is charged as fee for private attendance (transportation arrangement for the SR has to be made by the applicant only); 2. Two identifying witnesses are required (to be present at the given location);  3. The SR would visit the party/parties at their private residences after working hours (in the evening) based on his/her availability and time constraints in usual scenario)
P3393|The party has to Book an Appointment and shall mention the Location for Private Attendance; usually the SR visits the applicant on the same day based on the medical certificate (ie., the medical condition of the applicant); ifn’t the SR will schedule another day based on his/her availability – in either case, SR will intimate the applicant
P3394|Then the SR will pay visit to private residence of the party (ie., the executant for sale deed & presenter for the will) within his jurisdiction; if out of SR’s jurisdiction, then a commission is sent to the jurisdictional SR where he follows the below steps:
P3395|Verify the party’s identity
P3396|Takes the party’s photo & thumb impression
P3397|Get identified by 2 witnesses
P3398|Records the Deposition
P3399|Takes the signature of the party and 2 witnesses
P3400|If the SR is satisfied, then after arriving back to the SRO office, shall start the registration process by clicking on ‘Check and Register’ button, thereby generating the registration number (ie., the SR registers the document)
P3401|From this step onwards, follow the usual process and get the document registered
P3402|DR needs to be notified (by SR) after the registration process is completed
P3404|Process Abstract
P3406|Registration Rules, 1965 (Chapter X)
P3407|1. Application for attendance at a private residence
P3408|An application for attendance at a private residence shall be in writing and shall, in all possible cases, be signed by the person seeking the concession though it may be presented by any person to the Registering officer.
P3409|2. Attendance at a private residence
P3410|A requisition for attendance at a private residence shall be complied with as early as possible. If compliance world interferes with the regular business of the office or involve the closing of the office and if the case does not fall under the proviso to Section 31 of the Act, a commission should if practicable, be issued. Every such attendance by a Sub-Registrar or under his orders shall be reported to the Registrar within 24 hours.
P3411|3. Attendance at a private residence by the Registrar/Sub-Registrar
P3412|A Registrar may attend at a private residence situated within the limits of his district even though it may not lie within the sub-district for the purpose.
P3413|4. Persons exempt from personal appearance
P3414|(i) Persons exempt by law from personal appearance in Court are, under the Code of Civil Procedure, 1908
P3415|(a) women, who according to the customs and manners of the country, ought not to be compelled to appear in public, and
P3416|(b) persons of rank especially exempted by the Government.
P3417|(ii) A list of persons exempted under clause (i) (b) shall be obtained by the Registrar from the High Court or District Court and communicated to every Sub-Registrar in his district.
P3418|(iii) When in the course of attendance at a private residence the Registering officer is required to record in respect or the same document the admission of execution of persons not entitled to the concession, the request may be complied with, provided that an attendance fee is levied in respect of each such person.
P3419|5. Form and procedure of payment of commission
P3420|(i) A commission issued under Section 33 or Section 38 shall be prepared in the form hereunder* and shall, when the person to be examined resides within the sub-district, be addressed ordinarily by the registering office to one of his clerks, if any. When the person to be examined resides in another sub-district, whether within the same district or in another district, the commission shall be directed to the Sub-Registrar of the latter sub-district. The Sub-Registrar receiving a commission so addressed may, if he cannot attend personally, redirect it to any officer of his establishment vide also Sections 75 and 76 of the Code of Civil procedure, 1908.
P3421|The form above referred to.
P3422|*Commission under Section 33 or Section 38 of the Indian Registration Act, 1908.
P3423|To
P3424|The District Registrar/Sub-Registrar.
P3425|Whereas the accompanying power-of-attorney/document, dated the ……………. and purporting to have been executed by A.B. son of ……………… residing in your sub-district …………….. has been presented for attestation/registration in this office and whereas it is necessary that it should be ascertained whether it has been voluntarily executed by the person by whom it purports to have been examined in connection.
P3426|Executed 					take 					       ----------------------------- you are hereby requested to ----------------------- the examination of
P3427|therewith 						order 				            ……….. upon the interrogatories hereunto attached and to return this commission with the examination of the said ………….. to this office on or before the day of …………..
P3428|Given under my hand and seal this day ………… of ………….. 20 …..
P3430|Signature of Registering Officer.
P3431|Seal.
P3432|(ii) When the commission is for the examination of an executant, and has been executed, the Commissioner shall return the document to the office from which it was issued endorsed as follows.
P3433|Having attended the …………. at residence of A.B., son of C.D., at ……….. I have this day examined the said A.B., who has been identified to my satisfaction by E. F., son of G. H., etc., resident of …………. And the said A.B., admitted (or denied) the voluntary execution of this power-of-attorney/the execution of this document.
P3434|Left thumb impression of executant when necessary.
P3436|Full signature of the executant.
P3438|Signature of witnesses.

P3441|(iii) Where receipt of consideration is acknowledged before the Commissioner, he shall add the following clause to this endorsement:
P3442|“and acknowledged receipt of Rs. ……….. (or goods to be specified) being consideration (in whole or in part).”
P3443|(iv) Where consideration is paid in the presence of the Commissioner, he shall ad the following to the endorsement.
P3444|“I also certify that Rs. ……….. (or goods to be specified) were paid (or delivered) in my presence to the said A.B. by.”
P3445|The signature of the payee and payer shall also be taken below this endorsement, as provided in the specimen form in Schedule B.
P3446|(v) On receiving the Commissioner’s report, the Registering Officer shall, if satisfied as to the execution of the document, make the following endorsement below the report.
P3447|“From the foregoing report I am satisfied that this Power-of Attorney/document has been voluntarily executed by the said A.B.”
P3448|Date:
P3449|Signature of Registering Officer.
P3450|6. Manner of examination of witnesses
P3451|A commissioner may examine witnesses in the same manner as a Registering Officer and persons who may be required to give evidence before a Commissioner and who refuse to do so shall be subject to the penalties and punishments which they would incur for the same offence if committed in a Registration Office.
P3452|7. Manner of examination by the Registering Officer
P3453|A Registering Officer may examine the Commissioner personally in his office touching any of the circumstances connected with the discharge of his commission, especially with reference to the voluntary nature of the admission of execution.

P3497|Karnataka Stamp Act, 1957
P3498|1. Instrument of conveyance, etc. undervalued how to be dealt with.
P3499|(1) If the registering officer appointed under the Registration Act, 1908 (Central Act XVI of 1908) while registering any instrument of,-
P3500|(a) Conveyance [section 2(1)(d)];
P3501|(b) Gift [Article 28(a)];
P3502|(c) Exchange of property (Article 26);
P3503|(d) Settlement (Article 48-A(i))
P3504|(e) Reconstitution of Partnership (Article 40-B (a));
P3505|(f) Dissolution of partnership (Article 40-C (a));
P3506|(g) An agreement to sell covered under sub-clause (i) of clause (e) of Article 5;
P3507|(h) a lease covered under item 8 [(vi)] 8 of Article 30;
P3508|(i) A power of Attorney covered under clause (e), clause (ea) and clause (eb) of Article 41;
P3509|(j) Release 6 [(Article 45-(a)]6;
P3510|(k)Conveyance under a decree or final order of any Civil Court
P3511|has reason to believe having regard to the estimated market value published by the Committee constituted under section 45B, if any or otherwise, that the market value of the property which is the subject matter of such instrument has not been truly set forth, he shall after arriving at the estimated market value, communicate the same to the parties and unless the parties pay the duty on the basis of such valuation, shall keep pending the process of registration and refer the matter along with a copy of such instrument to the Deputy Commissioner for determination of the market value of property and the proper duty payable thereon.
P3512|(l) Agreement [Article 5(f)] 1957: KAR. ACT 34]
P3513|(m) Award [Article 11(a)]
P3514|(n) Trust [Article 54 (A) (iii)]
P3515|(o) Transferable Development Rights [ Article 20(7)]
P3516|(2) On receipt of a reference under sub-section (1), the Deputy Commissioner shall, after giving the parties a reasonable opportunity of being heard and after holding an inquiry in such manner as the State Government may by rules prescribe, determine by order as for as may be within ninety days from the date of receipt of such reference the market value of the property which is the subject matter of instrument specified in sub-section (1) and the duty payable thereon. The difference, if any, in the amount of duty, shall be payable by the person liable to pay the duty with interest at twelve percent per annum if he does not pay within ninety days from the date of order of the Deputy Commissioner
P3517|Provided that the payment of interest is not applicable to instruments executed prior to 31st day of March, 2006.
P3518|(3) The Deputy Commissioner may, suo motu within two years from the date of registration of any instrument specified in sub-section (1) not already referred to him under sub-section (1), call for and examine the instrument for the purpose of satisfying himself as to the correctness of the market value of the property which is the subject matter of any instrument specified in subsection (1) and the duty payable thereon and if after such examination he has reason to believe that the market value of such property has not been truly set forth in the instrument, he may determine by order the market value of such property and the duty payable thereon in accordance with the procedure provided for in sub-section (2). The difference, if any, in the amount of duty, shall be payable by the person liable to pay the duty with interest at twelve percent per annum if he does not pay within ninety days from the date of order of the Deputy Commissioner:
P3519|Provided that nothing in this sub-section shall apply to any instrument registered before the commencement of the Karnataka Stamp (Amendment) Act, 1975
P3520|Provided further that the payment of interest is not applicable to instruments executed prior to 31st day of March 2006.
P3521|(4) The order of the Deputy Commissioner under sub-section (2) or (3) shall be communicated to the person liable to pay the duty. A copy of every such order shall be sent to the registering officer concerned.
P3522|(5) Any person aggrieved by an order of the Deputy Commissioner under sub-section (2) or sub-section (3) may, prefer an appeal before the Regional Commissioner and all such appeals 1957: KAR. ACT 34] Stamp 532 shall be preferred within such time and be heard and disposed off in such manner as the State Government may by rules prescribe
P3523|Provided that no appeal shall be admitted unless the person aggrieved has deposited, in the prescribed manner, fifty percent of the difference in the amount of duty as determined by the Deputy Commissioner under sub-section (2) or (3):
P3524|Provided further that where after the determination of the market value by the Appellate Authority or determined again by the Deputy Commissioner on a remand of the case the stamp duty borne is found to be sufficient, the amount deposited shall be returned to the person concerned:
P3525|Provided also that such person shall pay the difference in duty along with interest at twelve percent per annum if he does not pay with in ninety days from the date of order of the Deputy Commissioner or sixty days from the date of order of the Appellate Authority, so however, the payment of interest is not applicable to instruments executed prior to eighteenth day of August 1999

P3567|Process Abstract
P3569|Karnataka Stamp Act, 1957 (CHAPTER IV INSTRUMENTS NOT DULY STAMPED)
P3570|1. Examination and impounding of instruments
P3571|(1) Every person having by law or consent of parties’ authority to receive evidence, and every person in charge of a public office, except an officer of police, before whom any instrument, chargeable in his opinion, with duty, is 1957: KAR. ACT 34 produced or comes in the performance of his functions, shall, if it appears to him that such instrument is not duly stamped, impound the same.
P3572|(2) For that purpose every such person shall examine every instrument so chargeable and so produced or coming before him, in order to ascertain whether it is stamped with a stamp of the value and description required by the law in force in the State of Karnataka when such instrument was executed or first executed: Provided that,
P3573|(a) nothing herein contained shall be deemed to require any Magistrate or Judge of a Criminal Court to examine or impound, if he does not think fit so to do, any instrument coming before him in the course of any proceeding other than a proceeding under Chapter XII or Chapter XXXVI of the Code of Criminal Procedure, 1898;
P3574|(b) in the case of a Judge of the High Court, the duty of examining and impounding any instrument under this section may be delegated to such officer as the Court appoints in this behalf.
P3575|(3) For the purposes of this section, in cases of doubt, the Government may determine, — (a) what offices shall be deemed to be public offices; and (b) who shall be deemed to be persons in charge of public offices.
P3576|2. Instruments not duly stamped inadmissible in evidence, etc.
P3577|No instrument chargeable with duty shall be admitted in evidence for any purpose by any person having by law or consent of parties’ authority to receive evidence, or shall be acted upon, registered or authenticated by any such person or by any public officer, unless such instrument is duly stamped: Provided that,
P3578|(a) any such instrument not being an instrument chargeable with a duty not exceeding fifteen naye paise]1 only, or a mortgage of crop [Article 35 (a) of the Schedule] chargeable under clauses (a) and (b) of section 3 with a duty of twenty-five naye paise shall, subject to all just exceptions, be admitted in evidence on payment of the duty with which the same is chargeable, or, in the case of an instrument insufficiently stamped, or the amount required to make up such duty, together with a penalty of five rupees, or, when ten times the amount of the proper duty or deficient portion thereof exceeds five rupees, of a sum equal to ten times such duty or portion;
P3579|(b) where a contract or agreement of any kind is effected by correspondence consisting of two or more letters and any one of the letters bears the proper stamp, the contract or agreement shall be deemed to be duly stamped;
P3580|(c) nothing herein contained shall prevent the admission of any instrument in evidence in any proceeding in a Criminal Court, other than a proceeding under Chapter XII or Chapter XXXVI of the Code of Criminal Procedure, 1898;
P3581|(d) nothing herein contained shall prevent the admission of any instrument in any Court when such instrument has been executed by or on behalf of the Government, or where it bears the certificate of the Deputy Commissioner as provided by section 32 or any other provision of this Act and such certificate has not been revised in exercise of the powers conferred by the provisions of Chapter VI
P3582|3. Admission of instrument where not to be questioned
P3583|Where an instrument has been admitted in evidence such admission shall not, except as provided in section 58, be called in question at any stage of the same suit or proceeding on the ground that the instrument has not been duly stamped. 1957: KAR. ACT 34] Stamp 528
P3584|4. Admission of improperly stamped instruments
P3585|The State Government may make rules providing that, where an instrument bears a stamp of sufficient amount but of improper description, it may, on payment of the duty with which the same is chargeable, be certified to be duly stamped, and any instrument so certified shall then be deemed to have been duly stamped as from the date of its execution.
P3586|5. Instruments impounded how dealt with
P3587|(1) When the person impounding an instrument under section 33 has by law or consent of parties authority to receive evidence and admits such instrument in evidence upon payment of a penalty as provided by section 34 or of duty as provided by section 36, he shall send to the Deputy Commissioner an authenticated copy of such instrument, together with a certificate in writing, stating the amount of duty and penalty levied in respect thereof, and shall send such amount to the Deputy Commissioner or to such person as he may appoint in this behalf.
P3588|(2) In every other case, the person so impounding an instrument shall send it in original to the Deputy Commissioner
P3589|6. Deputy Commissioner's power to refund penalty paid under sub-section (1) of section 37
P3590|(1) When a copy of an instrument is sent to the Deputy Commissioner under sub-section (1) of section 37, he may, if he thinks fit, refund any portion of the penalty in excess of five rupees which has been paid in respect of such instrument.
P3591|(2) When such instrument has been impounded only because it has been written in contravention of section 13 or section 14, the Deputy Commissioner may refund the whole penalty so paid.
P3592|7. Deputy Commissioner's power to stamp instruments impounded.
P3593|(1) When the Deputy Commissioner impounds any instrument under section 33, or receives any instrument sent to him under sub-section (2) of section 37, not being an instrument chargeable with a duty not exceeding fifteen naye paise only or a mortgage of crop [Article 35 (a) of the Schedule] chargeable under clause (a) or (b) of section 3 with a duty of twenty-five naye paise, he shall adopt the following procedure:
P3594|(a) if he is of opinion that such instrument is duly stamped, or is not chargeable with duty, he shall certify by endorsement thereon that it is duly stamped, or that it is not so chargeable, as the case may be;
P3595|(b) if he is of opinion that such instrument is chargeable with duty and is not duly stamped he shall require the payment of the proper duty or the amount required to make up the same, together with a penalty of five rupees; or if he thinks fit; an amount not exceeding ten times the amount of the proper duty or of the deficient portion thereof, whether such amount exceeds or falls short of five rupees: Provided that, when such instrument has been impounded only because it has been written in contravention of section 13 or section 14, the Deputy Commissioner may, if he thinks fit, remit the whole penalty prescribed by this section.
P3596|(2) Subject to any orders made under Chapter VI, every certificate under clause (a) of sub-section (1) shall, for the purposes of this Act be conclusive evidence of the matters stated therein.
P3597|(3) Where an instrument has been sent to the Deputy Commissioner under sub-section (2) of section 37, the Deputy Commissioner shall, when he has dealt with it as provided by this section, return it to the impounding officer.
P3598|8. Instruments unduly stamped by accident
P3599|If any instrument chargeable with duty and not duly stamped, not being an instrument chargeable with a duty not exceeding fifteen naye paise or a mortgage of crop [Article 35 (a) of the Schedule] chargeable under clause (a) or (b) of section 3 with a duty of twenty-five naye paise is produced by any person of his own motion before the Deputy Commissioner within one year from the date of its execution or first execution and such person brings to the notice of the Deputy Commissioner the fact that such instrument is not duly stamped and offers to pay to the Deputy Commissioner the amount of the proper duty, or the amount required to make up the same, and the Deputy Commissioner is satisfied that the omission to duly stamp such instrument has been occasioned by accident, mistake or urgent necessity, he may, instead of proceeding under sections 33 and 39, receive such amount and proceed as next hereinafter prescribed.
P3600|9. Endorsement of instruments on which duty has been paid under section 34, 39 or 40.-
P3601|(1) When the duty and penalty (if any) leviable in respect of any instrument have been paid under section 34, section 39 or section 40, the person admitting such instrument in evidence or the Deputy Commissioner, as the case may be, shall certify by endorsement thereon that the proper duty or, as the case may be, the proper duty and penalty (stating the amount of each) have been levied in respect thereof and the name and residence of the person paying them.
P3602|(2) Every instrument so endorsed shall thereupon be admissible in evidence, and may be registered and acted upon and authenticated as if it had been duly stamped, and shall be delivered on his application in this behalf to the person from whose possession it came into the hands of the officer impounding it, or as such person may direct: Provided that,
P3603|(a) no instrument which has been admitted in evidence upon payment of duty and a penalty under section 34, shall be so delivered before the expiration of one month from the date of such impounding, or if the Deputy Commissioner has certified that its further detention is necessary and has not cancelled such certificate;
P3604|(b) nothing in this section shall affect order XIII, rule 9 of the First Schedule to the Code of Civil Procedure, 1908.

P3649|Process Abstract
P3651|Registration Act, 1908
P3652|1. Provision where delay in presentation is unavoidable,
P3653|(1) If, owing to urgent necessity or unavoidable accident, any document executed, or copy of a decree or order made, in India is not presented for registration till after the expiration of the time hereinbefore prescribed in that behalf, the Registrar, in cases where the delay in presentation does not exceed four months, may direct that, on payment of a fine not exceeding ten times the amount of the proper registration-fee, such document shall be accepted for registration.
P3654|(2) Any application for such direction may be lodged with a Sub-Registrar, who shall forthwith forward it to the Registrar to whom he is subordinate.
P3655|Registration Rules, 1965
P3656|2. Rate of fines for delays, etc.
P3657|(i) The fines for delays in presentation and appearance under Sections 25 and 34 shall be regulated as follows.
P3658|(a) When the delay does not exceed one week after the expiration of the time allowed for presentation, a fine equal to the registration fee or appearance has to be paid
P3659|(b) When the delay exceeds one week but does not exceed one calendar month after the expiration of the time allowed for presentation, a fine equal to twice the registration fee has to be paid
P3660|(c) When the delay exceeds one month but does not exceed two months after the expiration of the time allowed for presentation, a fine equal to five times the registration fee has to be paid
P3661|(d)When the delay exceeds two months but does not exceed four months after the expiration of the time allowed for presentation, a fine equal to ten times registration fee has to be paid
P3662|(ii) The fine shall be levied in addition to the proper registration fee.
P3663|(iii) The term “registration fee” as used in this rule means the fee actually leviable under any of the Articles I to IV of the Table of Fees and notes thereunder.
P3664|3. Calculation of rate of fine in case of documents in duplicate
P3665|When a document is presented for registration in duplicate or triplicate, the fine leviable under Rule 52 shall be calculated as for one document only.
P3666|4. Calculation of fine in certain cases
P3667|Whenever a fine for delay in appearance is levied on more occasions than one in respect of one and the same document, the amount of the fine leviable on the second and subsequent occasions shall be the difference, if any, between the total amount leviable up to the second or subsequent occasion and the fine or fines previously levied in respect of such document.
P3668|5. Application for condoning delay in presentation, etc
P3669|(i) Whenever an application under Section 25 or Section 34 is lodged with a Sub-Registrar, he shall forward the same, whether he considers the reason for delay to be satisfactory or not, for the orders of the Registrar but as laid down in Rule 46(iii) there is no objection to his recording the admission of execution on such a document before forwarding the application to the Registrar.
P3670|(ii) When the Registrar condones the delay either under Section 25(1) or under the proviso to Section 34(1) and directs either the acceptance of the document for registration or its registration as the case may be, the Sub- Registrar, on receipt of orders to that effect from the Registrar shall make an endorsement on the document above the registration certificate in the following form and close the same with his signature and date
P3671|Acceptance for registration/admission to registration was directed by the ……. Registrar of ……. In his Order No. ……….. dated ……… on payment of the fine of Rs. ………… for a delay of ………. Under Section 25/Section 34 of the Indian Registration Act, 1908.
P3672|Date:
P3673|Signature of the Sub-Registrar.
