P3722|Process Abstract
P3724|Registration Act, 1908
P3725|Power-of-attorney recognizable for purposes of section 32
P3726|(1) For the purposes of section 32, the following powers-of-attorney shall alone be recognized, namely:
P3727|(a) if the principal at the time of executing the power-of-attorney resides in any part of 1 [India] in which this Act is for the time being in force, a power-of-attorney executed before and authenticated by the Registrar or Sub-Registrar within whose district or sub-district the principal resides;
P3728|(b) if the principal at the time aforesaid 2 [resides in any part of India in which this Act is not in force], a power-of-attorney executed before and authenticated by any Magistrate;
P3729|(c) if the principal at the time aforesaid does not reside in 1 [India], a power-of-attorney executed before and authenticated by a Notary Public, or any Court, Judge, Magistrate, 3 [Indian] Consul or Vice-Consul, or representative 4 *** of the Central Government:
P3730|Provided that the following persons shall not be required to attend at any registration-office or Court for the purpose of executing any such power-of-attorney as is mentioned in clauses (a) and (b) of this section, namely:
P3731|(i) persons who by reason of bodily infirmity are unable without risk or serious inconvenience so to attend;
P3732|(ii) persons who are in jail under civil or criminal process; and
P3733|(iii) persons exempt by law from personal appearance in court.
P3734|5 Explanation.—In this sub-section “India” means India, as defined in clause (28) of section 3 of the General Clauses Act, 1897 (10 of 1897).
P3735|(2) In the case of every such person the Registrar or Sub-Registrar or Magistrate, as the case may be, if satisfied that the power-of-attorney has been voluntarily executed by the person purporting to be the principal, may attest the same without requiring his personal attendance at the office or Court aforesaid.
P3736|(3) To obtain evidence as to the voluntary nature of the execution, the Registrar or Sub-Registrar or Magistrate may either himself go to the house of the person purporting to be the principal, or to the jail in which he is confined, and examine him, or issue a commission for his examination.
P3737|(4) Any power-of-attorney mentioned in this section may be proved by the production of it without further proof when it purports on the face of it to have been executed before and authenticated by the person or Court hereinbefore mentioned in that behalf.
P3738|Registration Rules, 1965 (Chapter XI)
P3739|1. Examination of a Power-of-Attorney
P3740|(i) When a power of attorney is executed before a Registering Officer he shall, after satisfying himself of the identity of the party and obtaining when necessary his left thumb impression against the signature, authenticate it in the following form
P3741|“Authentication under clause (a) of sub-section (1) of Section 33. The power-of-attorney has been executed by…… of ….. in my presence on the ……… day of ………….20. …………. The said…… is personally known to me/the identity of the said ……… has been proved by the testimony of Sri …………… to my satisfaction, and whose signature are affixed to this endorsement.
P3742|Signature of the person identifying the principal.
P3743|Signature of (Sub) Registrar.”
P3744|Seal.
P3745|Dated:
P3746|(ii) When a Power-of-Attorney which has not been executed before a Registering Officer is presented to him for attestation under the proviso to Section 33 the Registering Officer shall, if he attends himself at the private residence of the principal or if the principal appears in the office behind a purdah in the case of Ghosha ladies and is examined with the help of any witness, adopt the following form of attestation.
P3747|“Attestation under sub-section (2) of Section 33, This Power-of-Attorney has of been voluntarily executed by --------- of ------------ I have satisfied myself in this behalf by personal examination of ------------- by examination of the said ---------- on commission. The said-- -------- is personally known to me. The identity of the said --------- has been proved by the testimony of ---------- to my satisfaction, and whose signature is affixed hereunto.
P3748|Seal.
P3749|Signature of the person identifying Dated. the principal.
P3750|Signature of the (Sub) Registrar.”
P3751|(iii) In authenticating a Power-of-Attorney which occupies more than one sheet of paper, the seal and the signature of the Registering Officer shall be affixed to each sheet.
P3752|(iv) Every interlineation, blank, erasure of alteration in the body of a Power-of-Attorney which is authenticated and not registered shall, at the time of authentication, be detailed in a footnote added to the document below the endorsement of authentication and shall be signed by the Registering Officer even if the party himself has entered a similar note in the document, of there are no interlineations, blanks or erasures of alterations, in the body, that fact shall be noted. Interlineations, etc., in the authentication or attestation endorsements shall be initiated by the Registering Officer.
P3753|2. Procedure regarding Special or General Power-of-Attorney
P3754|(i) Special Powers-of-Attorney for registration purposes produced before a Registering Officer are to be retained and filed in original. If general powers be so produced, their copies with the proper Court fee stamp should be obtained and filed. Both of these should be filed separately.
P3755|(ii) On the original or copy thus filed, a note as follows should be endorsed showing for what purposes the Power-of-Attorney has been employed:
P3756|(a) Document serial No. …………. of daily Register dated …………. has been presented for registration by ……… under authority of this Power-of-Attorney (or Power-of- Attorney of which this is a copy).
P3757|(b) Execution of document No. ……….. registered at page ………… of Book No. ……….. Volume …………. has been admitted by ………. On behalf of ………… under authority of this Power-of-Attorney (or the Power-of-Attorney of which this is a copy).
P3758|Seal.
P3759|Signed A.B.
P3760|Date.
P3761|Registrar or Sub-Registrar.
P3762|(iii) The above not should be recorded on the day on which the power is acted upon, pages, volume, etc., being filled up after registration of the deed.
P3763|3. Attestation of endorsement on a Power-of-Attorney
P3764|One copy of a general power will suffice for the registration of more than one document on the same day or for the registration of different documents presented at different times. The endorsement under Rule 64 should be written on each occasion the power is being acted upon. If there be no space on the copy for the endorsement a fresh paper may be attached thereto.
P3765|4. Form of endorsement
P3766|The endorsement prescribed in Rules 63 and 64 shall be written in English.
P3767|5. Registration of a Power-of-Attorney
P3768|A Power-of-Attorney may be brought to a registering Officer (i) for authentication or attestation, or (ii) for registration, or (iii) for both authentication or attestation and registration. In the first case, he shall merely make the entry prescribed for authentication or attestation; in the second case, he shall register the power in the same manner as any other document; and in the third case, he shall first authenticate or attest the power and then admit it to registration in the usual manner.
P3769|6. Manner of registration of a Power-of-Attorney
P3770|Although a Power-of-Attorney may be registered like any other instrument, it is not valid for registration purposes unless authenticated or attested as the case may be when a person who does not understand the distinction between authentication or attestation and registration, the registering officer should explain the difference to him and give such information as may be necessary.
P3771|7. Powers of registering authorities regarding Power of Attorney
P3772|District Registrars or Sub-Registrars cannot legally authenticate or attest Power-of-Attorney other than those required for registration purpose. A Registering Officer can authenticate or attest a General Power-of-Attorney only when it contains a specific authority to present a document or to admit execution of a document executed by the principal.
P3773|8. Abstracts of Power-of-Attorney
P3774|(i) An abstract in Form No. 12, Schedule A shall be retained to each Power-of-Attorney authenticated of attested by a Registering Officer as prescribed in Rule 25 whether such power is general or special registered or not registered. The notes of interlineations, blanks, erasures and alterations made by the Registering Officer on the original power shall be copied verbatim in the abstract.
P3775|(ii) (a) Each registration office shall maintain a register of all revocations of Power-of-Attorney registered in, or communicated to it, in the prescribed Form 12-A and also note the fact of such revocation in Form No.12 as aforesaid. (b) When notice of a revocation is given to a Registering Officer, he shall send an intimation of the same to such other offices as may be specified by the person on revoking the power.
P3780|Late Appearance (or Parties Appearing at Different Times)
P3782|Brief Description:
P3783|If the parties appear at different times, as per provisions of Sections 23, 24 and 25, the SR can admit execution or appearance.
P3785|Process Flow Diagram:
P3788|Figure: Process Flow Diagram for Late Appearance
P3790|Functional Step by Step Process:
P3792|User Registration (usual flow)
P3793|Document Information (usual flow)
P3794|Property Search (usual flow)
P3795|Property Schedule (usual flow)
P3796|Party Details (usual flow)
P3797|MV, Stamp Duty & Fee Calculation (usual flow)
P3798|Online Document Summary (usual flow)
P3799|SRO Conformation (usual flow)
P3800|Payment (usual flow)
P3801|Appointment Scheduler (usual flow)
P3802|Document Summary/Signature (usual flow)
P3803|On the scheduled date and time, all but one (or more) appear before the SR and follow the usual process of getting the document registered. But as any one (or more) of them is absent, the document is kept pending till his/their appearance
P3804|Document Registration
P3805|But the SR keeps the document pending (‘Document Kept Pending’) during the document registration process where the reason for pending gets recorded in the Minute Book and the Endorsement is printed on the document
P3806|(Note: Once the DEO prints the Endorsement, the pending status should revert back to SR login)
P3807|Exceptional Cases
P3808|(Note: 1. This scenario takes place when there are several executants and they appear at different times (ie., not all of them will be present on the scheduled date and time). Hence, until all of them appear and until DEO captures the required information (ie., capture photo, thumb impression, signature of the parties and 2 witness, etc.), the document is kept pending by the SR;
P3809|2. As the concerned parties (except the one (or more) who goes for Late Appearance) have already made the required payment of fee, no further payment will be required at this stage)
P3810|As the document is kept pending, it comes under exceptional case
P3811|The person(s) who is appearing for Late Appearance, shall report to the SR at SRO; the time and date of his/their appearance will be recorded based on their appearance
P3812|(Note: the person(s) appearing for late attendance are supposed to visit the SRO within 8 months from the date of execution and get the document registered. If they appear within 4 months, no need to make any additional payment; if it exceeds 4 months, the Condonation of Delay applies and he/they have to make the payment accordingly (Refer 26.4 Condonation of Delay under Exceptional Cases); if it exceeds 8 months, then the registration gets terminated automatically)
P3813|As the required payment of fee is already being made (during initial scheduled date and time), the SR allows the application to get retrieved from the Minute Book
P3814|Based on the availability, the SR shall allocate the person(s) appearing for late attendance to the DEO of his choice
P3815|The DEO shall take the capture e-KYC (ie., Biometrics & Photograph) of the person(s) appearing for late appearance and the 2 witnesses (for person(s) appearing late) ie.,
P3816|Verify the party’s identity
P3817|Takes the party’s photo & thumb impression
P3818|Get identified by 2 witnesses
P3819|Takes the signature of the party/parties and 2 witnesses
P3820|(This comes under 7. Online Document Summary step)
P3821|The DEO shall get the Document Summary Printed, Signed (gets the Signature of Presenter, DEO, FDA/SDA and SR) and Uploaded
P3822|(Note: The DEO shall capture and update the Document Summary of the Late Appearing Parties)
P3823|The DEO shall send the same for SR Confirmation
P3824|On Confirmation by the SR by clicking on ‘Check and Register’ button, the Registration Process begins and the Document Number gets generated (This comes under 11. Document Summary/Signature & 12. Document Registration)
P3825|From this step onwards, follow the usual process and get the document registered
P3827|Process Abstract
P3829|Registration Rules, 1965 (Chapter XII)
P3830|1. Enquiry before registration by registering officer.
P3831|(1) Subject to the provisions contained in this Part and in sections 41, 43, 45, 69, 75, 77, 88 and 89, no document shall be registered under this Act, unless the persons executing such document, or their representatives, assigns or agents authorised as aforesaid, appear before the registering officer within the time allowed for presentation under sections 23, 24, 25 and 26: Provided that, if owing to urgent necessity or unavoidable accident all such persons do not so appear, the Registrar, in cases where the delay in appearing does not exceed four months, may direct that on payment of a fine not exceeding ten times the amount of the proper registration fee, in addition to the fine, if any, payable under section 25, the document may be registered.
P3832|(2) Appearances under sub-section (1) may be simultaneous or at different times.
P3833|(3) The registering officer shall thereupon—
P3834|(a) enquire whether or not such document was executed by the persons by whom it purports to have been executed;
P3835|(b) satisfy himself as to the identity of the persons appearing before him and alleging that they have executed the document;
P3836|(c) in the case of any person appearing as a representative, assign or agent, satisfy himself of the right of such person so to appear.
P3837|(4) Any application for a direction under the proviso to sub-section (1) may be lodged with a Sub-Registrar, who shall forthwith forward it to the Registrar to whom he is subordinate.
P3838|(5) Nothing in this section applies to copies of decrees or orders.
P3840|Abstract for 7 Special Articles
P3841|Partition
P3843|Nature of Document: Partition
P3844|Article: 39 Partition
P3845|Sub Article: 39(a), 39(b), 39(c), 39(d)
P3846|Book No.: 1
P3848|Brief Description
P3849|Partition is the division of property among the co-parceners (Joint family members) or co-owners of the properties. i.e., dividing the properties into shares in to meets and bounds
P3850|Sub Articles of Partition based on nature of the property use:-
P3851|(a) Partition of Non-Agriculture Land
P3852|(b) Partition of Agriculture Land
P3853|(c) Partition of Movable Property
P3854|(d) Partition of any Property (In combination of a, b, and c)
P3855|User Input
P3856|In order to calculate the Stamp Duty user, need to provide the below information with respect to the shares (User Input):
P3857|1. No. of Claimants
P3858|2. No. of Executants
P3859|3. No. of Shares
P3860|4. Nature of the property (Agriculture/Non-Agriculture/Miscellaneous (Movable))
P3861|5. Property assigned to the shares
P3862|6. Enter the extent of the share
P3863|7. Enter the Linear measurement of the share East to West, North to South (Only for Non-Agriculture land)
P3864|8. Enter the boundaries of the share East, West, North, South (For both Agriculture and Non-Agriculture land)
P3865|9. No. of Duplicate copies for calculation of Duplicate stamp duty and fee. i.e., Stamp Duty = No. of Duplicate copies*Fixed Stamp Duty (Rs.500) and Registration Fee = Stamp Duty = No. of Duplicate copies*Fixed Registration Fee (Rs.200)
P3866|Process Abstract
P3867|Process Abstract:
P3868|(a) Partition of Non-Agriculture Land
P3869|Fixed Stamp Duty of Rs.1000 per share for eAsthi, BBMP, Corporations, CMC, TMC and Urban development authorities Property and Fees Rs.1000 per share
P3870|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * 1000
P3871|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * 1000
P3872|Fixed Stamp Duty of Rs.500 per share for RDPR (eSwathu) Property and Fees 500 per share
P3873|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * 500
P3874|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * 500
P3876|(b) Partition of Agriculture Land
P3877|Fixed Stamp Duty of Rs.250 per share and Fees Rs.200 per share
P3878|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * 250
P3879|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * 200
P3880|(c) Partition of Movable Property (Money)
P3881|Fixed Stamp Duty of Rs.250 per share Fees Rs.300 per share
P3882|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * 250
P3883|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * 300
P3885|(d) Partition of any Property
P3886|If the shares of the partition are in combination of a, b, c the Total Stamp Duty and Fee payable per share is highest of the a, b, c
P3888|Example 1: Combination of the property “a” and “b” (“a”- non agriculture property, “b”- agriculture property)
P3889|For the properties “a” and “b”, highest stamp duty among “a” and “b” is “a”. Hence property “a” will be considered for calculation of stamp duty and fee
P3890|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * a
P3891|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * 1000 for eAsthi or
P3892|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * 500 for eSwathu
P3893|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * a
P3894|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * 1000 for eAsthi or
P3895|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * 500 for eSwathu
P3897|Example 2: Combination of the property “a” and “c” (“a”- non agriculture property, “c”- movable property)
P3898|For the properties “a” and “c”, highest stamp duty among “a” and “c” is “a”. Hence property “a” will be considered for calculation of stamp duty and fee
P3899|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * a
P3900|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * 1000 for eAsthi or
P3901|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * 500 for eSwathu
P3902|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * a
P3903|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * 1000 for eAsthi or
P3904|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * 500 for eSwathu
P3906|Example 3: Combination of the property “b” and “c” (“b”- agriculture property, “c”- movable property)
P3907|For the properties “b” and “c”, highest stamp duty and registration fee among “b” and “c” is “c”. Hence property “c” will be considered for calculation of stamp duty and fee
P3908|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * c
P3909|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * 250
P3910|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * c
P3911|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * 300
P3912|Example 4: Combination of the property “a”, “b” and “c” (“a” – non agriculture property, “b”- agriculture property, “c”- movable property)
P3913|For the properties “a”, “b” and “c”, highest stamp duty among “a’, “b” and “c” is “a”. Hence property “a” will be considered for calculation of stamp duty and fee
P3914|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * a
P3915|Total Stamp Duty Payable = No. of share * 1000
P3916|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * a
P3917|Total Registration Fee Payable = No. of share * 1000
P3919|Example 5: Any number of properties in a share, the stamp duty payable for a share is highest of “a”, “b” or “c” (“a” – non agriculture property, “b”- agriculture property, “c”- movable property)
P3920|If the shareholder is having 10 properties in a single share, then he needs to pay the stamp duty which is higher among the 10. For example, Person A is having 10 properties in a single share, then he needs to pay single stamp duty of the highest stamp duty of the property.
P3923|Joint Development Agreement
P3925|Nature of Document: JDA
P3926|Article: 5 Agreement
P3927|Sub Article: 5(f) When given for a Developer or Promoter for Development
P3928|Book No.: 1
P3930|Brief Description
P3931|Agreement is an arrangement agreed upon by the parties to develop a property into multi-unit building (Apartment) or Layout and they share among themselves the developed property. JDA will be registered in Book 1.
P3932|User Input
P3933|In order to calculate the Stamp Duty user, need to provide the below information with respect to the property (User Input):
P3934|1. No. of Claimants
P3935|2. No. of Executants
P3936|3. Sharing Ratio of the Landowner
P3937|4. Sharing Ratio of the Builder/Developer
P3938|5. Nature of the property (Agriculture/Non-Agriculture)
P3939|6. Money advanced if any for Landowner
P3940|7. Money advanced if any for Developer
P3941|8. Enter the extent of the land
P3942|9. Enter the Linear measurement of the land East to West, North to South (Only for Non-Agriculture land)
P3943|10 Enter the boundaries of the land East, West, North, South (For both Agriculture and Non-Agriculture land)
P3944|11. Proposed building super built up area
P3945|12. No. of Duplicate copies for calculation of Duplicate stamp duty and fee. i.e., Stamp Duty = No. of Duplicate copies*Fixed Stamp Duty (Rs.500) and Registration Fee = Stamp Duty = No. of Duplicate copies*Fixed Registration Fee (Rs.200)
P3946|Process Abstract
P3947|Process Abstract:
P3948|Example:
P3949|If 2 Person one as owner of land and other as a Developer agrees to develop an apartment, there will be sharing of super built up area. The sharing ratio say about 30:70% i.e.to say landowner may get 30% of super builtup area of building along with number of car parking and any money if agreed to be given and in return and the developer gets 70% of super builtup area and land. The stamp duty is calculated based on total land value of which 70% of land value taken as developer share and total value of building has to be calculated and of which 30% value of building goes to the share of landowner + Value of number of car parking + money if any given has to be taken as another share. The stamp duty and registration fee are calculated on the higher value of the shares.
P3951|Brief Description:
P3952|1 acre of agriculture land which is to be converted is the contribution of the landowner
P3953|Super-builtup area of the building about 10,000 sqm with 100 car-parking is the contribution of the Builder/Developer
P3954|Sharing Ratio = 30:70
P3955|30% of the super-builtup area + 30% car parking for Landowner + if money given any
P3956|70% of the super-builtup area + 70% car parking for Builder
P3957|MV of the Land = 1 cr per acre = 1,00,00,000
P3958|MV of the Super-builtup area = 10,000 (rate per sqm) * 10,000 (sqm of super-builtup area) = 10,00,00,000
P3959|Car Parking Rate = 2 lakh per car parking
P3960|Total Cost of Car parking = 2 lakh * 100 = 2,00,00,000
P3961|Share of the Landowner = 30% of super-builtup area + 30% of car parking area + if money given any i.e., cost of landowner share = 30% of 10,00,00,000 + 30% of car parking = 3,00,00,000 + 60,00,000 + 0= 3,60,00,000 say it is share 1
P3962|Share of the Builder = 70% of land i.e., cost of the land which goes to the builder = 70% of 1,00,00,000 = 70,00,000 say it is share 2
P3963|Now, stamp duty is calculated based on the highest share i.e., 3,60,00,000 (higher value between 3,60,00,000 and 70,000)
P3964|Hence Stamp Duty = 3,60,00,000 * 2% = 7,20,000
P3965|Registration Fee = 3,60,00,000 * 1% = 3,60,000
P3967|Power of Autonomy (PoA) with JDA (Joint Development Agreement)
P3969|Nature of Document: PoA with JDA
P3970|Article: 41 PoA
P3971|Sub Article: 41(ea) When given to a Developer or Promoter for Development of Property
P3972|Book No.: 4
P3974|Brief Description
P3975|(Article 41(ea)): PoA will given to the developer/builder by the landowner along with JDA when an agreement is made for development of multi-unit building (Apartment) or Layout and they share among themselves the developed property. To enable the smooth functioning of development the landowner gives a power of attorney to the developer with JDA and this will be registered in Book 4.
P3976|User Input
P3977|In order to calculate the Stamp Duty user, need to provide the below information with respect to the property (User Input):
P3978|1. No. of Claimants
P3979|2. No. of Executants
P3980|3. Sharing Ratio of the Landowner
P3981|4. Sharing Ratio of the Builder/Developer
P3982|5. Nature of the property (Agriculture/Non-Agriculture)
P3983|6. Money advanced if any for Landowner
P3984|7. Money advanced if any for Developer
P3985|8. Enter the extent of the land
P3986|9. Enter the Linear measurement of the land East to West, North to South (Only for Non-Agriculture land)
P3987|10. Enter the boundaries of the land East, West, North, South (For both Agriculture and Non-Agriculture land)
P3988|11. Proposed building super built-up area
P3989|12. No. of Duplicate copies for calculation of Duplicate stamp duty and fee. i.e., Stamp Duty = No. of Duplicate copies*Fixed Stamp Duty (Rs.500) and Registration Fee = Stamp Duty = No. of Duplicate copies*Fixed Registration Fee (Rs.200)
P3990|Process Abstract
P3991|Process Abstract:
P3992|If the full stamp duty is paid on the agreement, then denoting is given to the PoA. The maximum duty payable will be 200
P3993|Example:
P3994|If the stamp duty is not paid on the JDA, then the following process will be followed.
P3995|2 Person one as owner of land and other as a Developer agrees to develop an apartment, there will be sharing of super built up area. The sharing ratio say about 30:70% i.e.to say landowner may get 30% of super builtup area of building along with number of car parking and any money if agreed to be given and in return and the developer gets 70% of super builtup area and land. The stamp duty is calculated based on total land value of which 70% of land value taken as developer share and total value of building has to be calculated and of which 30% value of building goes to the share of landowner + Value of number of car parking + money if any given has to be taken as another share. The stamp duty and registration fee are calculated on the higher value of the shares.
P3997|Brief Description:
P3998|1 acre of agriculture land which is to be converted is the contribution of the landowner
P3999|Super-builtup area of the building about 10,000 sqm with 100 car-parking is the contribution of the Builder/Developer
P4000|Sharing Ratio = 30:70
P4001|30% of the super-builtup area + 30% car parking for Landowner + if money given any
P4002|70% of the super-builtup area + 70% car parking for Builder
P4003|MV of the Land = 1 cr per acre = 1,00,00,000
P4004|MV of the Super-builtup area = 10,000 (rate per sqm) * 10,000 (sqm of super-builtup area) = 10,00,00,000
P4005|Car Parking Rate = 2 lakh per car parking
P4006|Total Cost of Car parking = 2 lakh * 100 = 2,00,00,000
P4007|Share of the Landowner = 30% of super-builtup area + 30% of car parking area + if money given any i.e., cost of landowner share = 30% of 10,00,00,000 + 30% of car parking = 3,00,00,000 + 60,00,000 + 0= 3,60,00,000 say it is share 1
P4008|Share of the Builder = 70% of land i.e., cost of the land which goes to the builder = 70% of 1,00,00,000 = 70,00,000 say it is share 2
P4009|Now, stamp duty is calculated based on the highest share i.e., 3,60,00,000 (higher value between 3,60,00,000 and 70,000)
P4010|Hence Stamp Duty = 3,60,00,000 * 2% = 7,20,000
P4011|Registration Fee = 3,60,00,000 * 1% = 3,60,000
P4013|EXCHANGE
P4015|Nature of Document: Exchange
P4016|Article: 26
P4017|Sub Article: nil
P4018|Book No.: 1
P4020|Brief Description
P4021|Exchange means Exchange of Properties between two parties of their respective properties. It comes under Article 26 of Stamp Act, 1957.
P4022|For the purpose of calculating the stamp duty, the market value of both the properties of both the parties have to be calculated which are under exchange. The Property which is having higher value in the exchange is considered for the payment and calculation of stamp duty. The other property which is having lower value is exempted.
P4023|User Input
P4024|In order to calculate the Stamp Duty, the user needs to provide the below information with respect to the property:-
P4025|1. No. of Claimants
P4026|2. No. of Executants
P4027|3. No. of Properties
P4028|4. Nature of the property (Agriculture/Non-Agriculture)
P4029|5. Property assigned to the parties
P4030|6. Enter the extent of the properties
P4031|7. Enter the Linear measurement of the share East to West, North to South (Only for Non-Agriculture land)
P4032|8. Enter the boundaries of the share East, West, North, South (For both Agriculture and Non-Agriculture land)
P4033|9. Market Values of the properties
P4034|10. Consideration if any
P4035|11. No. of Duplicate copies for calculation of Duplicate stamp duty and fee. i.e., Stamp Duty = No. of Duplicate copies*Fixed Stamp Duty (Rs.500) and Registration Fee = No. of Duplicate copies*Fixed Registration Fee (Rs.200)
P4037|Process Abstract
P4038|Process Abstract
P4039|Example 1: Say, we have 2 parties - Party 1 and Party 2
P4040|Party 1 has got the property C & Party 2 has got the property D
P4041|Now, in the Exchange, the property D goes to party 1 and the property C goes to party 2
P4042|Supposed to say, property C is 1 acre and property D is 1 acre 10 guntas
P4043|Value of property C is Rs.1 crore and Value of property D is Rs.90 lakh
P4044|So, the stamp duty is calculated on property C ie., Rs.1 crore (because of higher value between them) and property D is exempted
P4045|Though the Article is 26, the rate of stamp duty is as per Conveyance ie., 5%. But as per the property situation ie., Urban Property 2% and Rural property 3% and Cess 10% on the stamp duty.
P4046|Hence, Govt stamp duty payable = [Market Value + Amount Paid (if any)]
P4047|= 1 crore*5% = Rs.500000
P4048|Surcharge = 3% on the stamp duty = Rs.15000
P4049|Cess = 10% 0n stamp duty = Rs.50000
P4050|Total stamp duty paid = Rs.500000 + Rs.15000 + Rs.50000 = Rs.565000
P4051|Registration Fee = 1% on the [Market Value + Amount Paid (if any)] = Rs.100000
P4052|Total Fee to be paid = Total Stamp Duty + Total Registration Fee = Rs.565000 + Rs.100000 = Rs.665000
P4054|Example 2: Say, we have 2 parties - Party 1 and Party 2
P4055|Party 1 has got the property C and has received Rs. 25 lakh from party 2
P4056|Party 2 has got the property D and has received Rs. 5 lakh from party 1
P4057|Now, in the Exchange, the property D goes to party 1 and the property C goes to party 2
P4058|Supposed to say, property C is 1 acre and property D is 1 acre 10 guntas
P4059|Value of property C is Rs.1 crore and Value of property D is Rs.90 lakh
P4060|Total amount to to be considered for Each Party:
P4061|Party 1 = Rs.1 crore + 25 lakh = Rs. 1.25 crore
P4062|Party 2 = Rs.90 lakh + 5 lakh = Rs. 0.95 crore
P4063|So, the stamp duty is calculated on property C ie., Rs.1.25 crore (because of higher value between them) and property D is exempted
P4064|Though the Article is 26, the rate of stamp duty is as per Conveyance ie., 5%. But as per the property situation ie., Urban Property 2% and Rural property 3% and Cess 10% on the stamp duty.
P4065|Hence, Govt stamp duty payable = [Market Value + Amount Paid (if any)]
P4066|= 1.25 crore*5% = Rs.6,25,000
P4067|Surcharge = 3% on the stamp duty = Rs.18,750
P4068|Cess = 10% 0n stamp duty = Rs.62,500
P4069|Total stamp duty paid = Rs. 6,25,000  + Rs. 18,750+ Rs. 62,500 = Rs.7,06,250
P4070|Registration Fee = 1% on the [Market Value + Amount Paid (if any)] = Rs.1,25,000
P4071|Total Fee to be paid = Total Stamp Duty + Total Registration Fee = Rs. 7,06,250 + Rs.1,25,000 = Rs.8,31,250

P4133|APARTMENT
P4135|Nature of Document: Apartment
P4136|Article: 20
P4137|Sub Article: 20(2), 20(2A)(i), 20(2A)(ii), 20(3)
P4138|Book No.: 1
P4140|Brief Description
P4141|Apartment means (a) “Premises” means and includes undivided interest in the land, building and proportionate share in the common areas;
P4142|(b) “Unit” includes flat, apartment, tenement, block or any other unit by whatever name called, constructed or under construction in accordance with the sanctioned plan by the authority competent to sanction a building plan under any law for the time being in force;
P4143|It comes under Article 20 of Stamp Act, 1957.
P4145|User Input
P4146|In order to calculate the Stamp Duty, the user needs to provide the below information with respect to the property:-
P4147|1. No. of Claimants
P4148|2. No. of Executants
P4149|3. Property details
P4150|4. Enter the Extent of the properties:-
P4151|5. Schedule A - Total land area used for the construction of apartment
P4152|6. Schedule B - Undivided share to be given to the unit of apartment purchaser
P4153|7. Schedule C - Super-builtup area of the unit of apartment
P4154|8. Schedule D - Amenities
P4155|9. No. of Car parking
P4156|10. Market Value of the super-builtup area
P4157|11. Consideration
P4159|Process Abstract
P4160|Process Abstract
P4161|For determination of market value and stamp duty for apartment, the total area of the land used for construction as shown in the schedule A has to be entered. The undivided share of land is to be demarcated as in schedule B. Super-builtup area as in schedule C is considered for calculating the market value with the rate prescribed, along with the no. of car parking area
P4163|Article 20 (2) – where it is related to instrument of conveyance executed by a promoter, a land owner or a developer by whatever name called pertaining to the premises of flat or apartment or transfer of share by or in favour of co-operative society or company pertaining to premises or unit and the market value of the property which is the subject matter of conveyance – rate of stamp duty is 5%
P4164|Article 20 (2A) (i) - where an instrument of Conveyance relating to the first sale of Flat/Apartment - where the market value of which is Rs.2000000 or less than Rs.2000000 - rate of stamp duty is 2%
P4165|Article 20 (2A) (ii) - where an instrument of Conveyance relating to the first sale of Flat/Apartment - where the market value of which is above Rs.2500000 but up to and inclusive of Rs.3500000 - rate of stamp duty is 3%
P4166|Article 20 (3) – Sale by BDA/KHB of Flat Flat/Apartment - rate of stamp duty is 5%
P4168|Example:
P4169|Schedule A
P4170|1 acre of total land area = 1089*40 = 43560 sqft = 4047 sqm
P4171|Schedule B
P4172|Say about 10000 sqm of super-builtup area
P4173|Undivided share per sqm = 4047/10000 = 0.4047 * 100 (fixed no. for % calculation) = 40.47 sqm
P4174|Schedule C
P4175|Super-builtup area = 100 sqm (per unit of apartment)
P4176|2 car parking
P4177|Article 20 (2A) (i)
P4179|Calculation
P4180|Market value = super-builtup area * guideline value
P4181|= 100 sqm * 10000
P4182|= Rs.1000000
P4183|Amenities = special features as per annexure values
P4184|Cost of car parking = 2 * Rs.200000 = Rs.400000
P4185|Total Market value = Rs.1000000 + Rs.400000 = Rs.1400000
P4186|Govt stamp duty payable = Total Market value * Rate of stamp duty
P4187|= Rs.1400000 * 2%
P4188|= Rs.28000
P4189|Surcharge = 3% of Rs.28000 = Rs.840
P4190|Cess = 10% of Rs.28000 = Rs.2800
P4191|Total stamp duty payable = Rs.28000 + Rs.840 + Rs.2800 = Rs.31640
P4192|Registration Fees = 1% on the Market value or Consideration (whichever is higher) = Rs.14000
P4193|Total Fee to be paid = Total Stamp Duty + Total Registration Fee = Rs.31640 + Rs.14000 = Rs.45640

P4223|In order to calculate the Stamp Duty, the user needs to provide the below information with respect to the property:-

P4234|11. No. of Duplicate copies for calculation of Duplicate stamp duty and fee. i.e., Stamp Duty = No. of Duplicate copies*Fixed Stamp Duty (Rs.500) and Registration Fee = Stamp Duty = No. of Duplicate copies*Fixed Registration Fee (Rs.200)
P4236|Process Abstract
P4238|Sub-article 30 (1) (i) - where the lease purports to be for a term not exceeding one year in case of residential property
P4239|Rate of Stamp Duty = 0.50%
P4240|Cap Amount = Max Rs.500
P4241|Government Duty = (Annual average rent + fine or premium or money advanced or security deposit) * Rate (Max Rs.500)
P4242|Total Stamp Duty = Government Duty
P4243|Total Registration Fees = 0.5% with Maximum of Rs.200
P4244|Method of Calculation of stamp duty = Total lease amount of the period + any advance amount *0.5% of rate of stamp duty with a maximum of Rs.500
P4245|Total Fee to be paid = Total Stamp Duty + Total Registration Fee
P4247|Example 1: Period of lease 1 year monthly rent of Rs.10000 = Rs.120000
P4248|Total stamp duty = (Rs.120000+Rs.10000(advance))*0.5% = 650, but limited to Maximum of Rs.500 = Rs.500 only
P4250|Sub-article 30 (1) (ii) - where the lease purports to be for a term not exceeding one year in case of commercial or industrial property
P4251|Rate of Stamp Duty = 0.50%
P4252|Government Duty = (Annual average rent + fine or premium or money advanced or security deposit) * Rate
P4253|Total Stamp Duty = Government Duty
P4254|Total Registration Fees = 0.5% with Minimum of Rs.200
P4255|Method of Calculation of stamp duty = Total lease amount of the period + any advance amount * 0.5% of the rate of stamp duty
P4256|Total Fee to be paid = Total Stamp Duty + Total Registration Fee
P4258|Example 1: Period of lease 1 year, monthly rent rate of Rs.10000 = Rs.120000
P4259|Total stamp duty will be (Rs.120000 + Rs.10000(advance))*0.5% = Rs.650
P4260|Example 2: Period of lease 1 year, monthly rent rate of Rs.500 = Rs.6000
P4261|Total stamp duty will be Rs.6000*0.5% = Rs.30, but a minimum of Rs.200 = Rs.200 only
P4263|Sub-article 30 (1) (iii) - where the lease purports to be for a term exceeding one year and not exceeding ten years
P4264|Rate of Stamp Duty = 1%
P4265|Government Duty = (Annual average rent + fine or premium or money advanced or security deposit) * Rate
P4266|Total Stamp Duty = Government Duty
P4267|Total Registration Fees = 0.5% with Minimum of Rs.200
P4268|Method of Calculation of stamp duty = (Annual average rent + advance) * rate of stamp duty
Annual average = 1st year rent at the rate of 10000 = 120000.00
                                2nd year 5% increase                       = 126000.00
                                3rd year 5% increase                        = 132300.00
                                4th year 5% increase                        = 138915.00
                                5th year 5% increase                        = 145860.75
                                6th year 5% increase                        = 153153.78
                                7th year 5% increase                        = 160811.46
                                8th year 5% increase                        = 168852.03
                                9th year 5% increase                         = 177294.63
                              10th year 5% increase                         = 186159.36
                                                           Total                          =1509347.01
Annual average rent = Total/10 = Rs.1509347.01/10 = Rs.150934.70 (should be rounded off to next thousand) = Rs.151000
Total Fee to be paid = Total Stamp Duty + Total Registration Fee
P4270|Example 1: (151000 + 10000) * 1% = Rs.1610 is the stamp duty
P4271|Sub-article 30 (1) (iv) - where the lease purports to be for a term exceeding ten years and not exceeding twenty years
P4272|Rate of Stamp Duty of Market value = 2%
P4273|Government Duty = (Annual average rent + fine or premium or money advanced or security deposit) * Rate
P4274|Total Stamp Duty = Government Duty
P4275|Total Registration Fees = 0.5%
P4276|Method of Calculation of stamp duty = Same as above explained (sub-article 30(1)(iii)) for 20 years’ average + advance * 2% rate
P4277|Total Fee to be paid = Total Stamp Duty + Total Registration Fee
P4279|Sub-article 30 (1) (v) where the lease purports to be for a term exceeding twenty years and not exceeding thirty years
P4280|Rate of Stamp Duty = 3%
P4281|Government Duty = (Annual average rent + fine or premium or money advanced or security deposit) * Rate
P4282|Total Stamp Duty = Government Duty
P4283|Total Registration Fees = 0.5%
P4284|Method of Calculation of stamp duty = Same as above explained (sub-article 30(1)(iii)) for 30 years’ average + advance * 3% rate
P4285|Total Fee to be paid = Total Stamp Duty + Total Registration Fee
P4287|30 (1) (vi) where the lease purports to be for a term exceeding thirty years or in perpetuity or does not purport to be for any definite term
P4288|Rate of Stamp Duty = 5%
P4289|Government Duty = ((Annual average rent + fine or premium or money advanced or security deposit) or (market value of the property)) whichever is higher * Rate
P4290|Surcharge Value = If urban Property, then 2% on Stamp Duty and
P4291|If Rural Property 3% on Stamp Duty
P4292|Cess Value = 10% on Stamp Duty
P4293|Total Stamp Duty = Government Duty + Surcharge Value + Cess Value
P4294|Total Registration Fees = 1% * ((Annual average rent + fine or premium or money advanced or security deposit) or (market value of the property)) whichever is higher
P4295|Method of Calculation of stamp duty = Same as above explained (sub-article 30(1)(iii)) for the period or Market value of the property, whichever is higher * rate of stamp duty
P4296|Total Fee to be paid = Total Stamp Duty + Total Registration Fee

P4363|Rate of Stamp Duty = Three rupee for every one hundred rupees or part thereof on the total amount or value of; the average annual royalty and average annual payment based on the final price offer obtained in the auction calculated on the estimated average annual production as per the approved mining plan
P4364|Stamp Duty = 3% * Royalty + Estimated Annual Average Production
P4365|Registration Fees = 1% * Royalty + Estimate Annual Average Production
P4366|Total Fee to be paid = Total Stamp Duty + Total Registration Fee
P4368|Sub-article 30 (3) (b) (4) where the lease purports to be for a term exceeding thirty years or in perpetuity or does not purport to be for any definite term
P4369|Rate of Stamp Duty = Five rupee for every one hundred rupees or part thereof on the total amount or value of; the average annual royalty and average annual payment based on the final price offer obtained in the auction calculated on the estimated average annual production as per the approved mining plan
P4370|Stamp Duty = 5% * Royalty + Estimated Annual Average Production
P4371|Surcharge Value = 3% * Stamp Duty
P4372|Cess Value = 10% * Stamp Duty
P4373|Total Stamp Duty = Government Stamp Duty + Surcharge Value + Cess Value
P4374|Total Registration Fees = 1% * Royalty + Estimate Annual Average Production
P4375|Total Fee to be paid = Total Stamp Duty + Total Registration Fee
P4379|ENDORSEMENT
P4381|“Endorsement” and “endorsed” include and apply to an entry in writing by a registering officer on a rider or covering slip to any document tendered for registration under this Act.
P4383|Contents in the Endorsement
P4384|Endorsement will have the following details:
P4385|1. 10A Certificate - under Karnataka Stamp Act 1957 Section 10A
P4386|10A Certificate will have the details of amount of Stamp Duty paid by the Party along with the Challan Number and Date of Remittance. The Certificate will be signed by the proper officer/Deputy Commissioner of Stamps/Sub Registrar
P4389|2. Exemption
P4390|If Exemption is there, Exemption particulars (both Stamp Duty as well as Registration Fee) are recorded
P4391|3. Denoting
P4392|If Denoting is there, Denoting certificate is recorded
P4393|4. Presentation Endorsement
P4394|Presentation Endorsement will have the Sub Registrar Office name where the Document is presented, Date and Time and Name of the Presenter with the following Fees
P4395|Registration Fee
P4396|Duplicate Fee
P4397|Scanning Fee
P4398|Mutation Fee in case of Agriculture Land
P4399|Miscellaneous
P4401|5. Party Information Endorsement
P4402|Party Information Endorsement will have the Presenter details such as Presenter Name, Photograph and Signature followed by Sub Registrar Signature.
P4403|Execution Admission by the Claiming Parties which includes Claimant Name, Photograph and Signature followed by Sub Registrar Signature.
P4404|Execution Admission by the Executing Parties which includes Executor/s Name, Photograph and Signature followed by Sub Registrar Signature
P4405|If appearance of the Party as per section 88 exemption is given and the particulars of the exemption letter will be recorded
P4407|6. Identifying Witness (Identifiers) Endorsement
P4408|Identifying Witness Endorsement will have the Name, Address and Signature of the two Witness followed by Sub Registrar Signature.
P4411|7. Payment before the Sub Registrar
P4412|If the Consideration (of amount) of the transaction is paid in front of Sub Registrar either by cash or cheque/DD, the same is to be recorded and signatures of the parties paying and receiving are taken; then the Sub Registrar will sign it
P4414|“The Payment is made before me in Cash (in Rupee) ……………………. or Cheque/DD Number ………………… dated …………. by Sri./Smt. ………………………………. to Sri./Smt. ………………………..”
P4415|Signature of the Parties
P4416|Signature of the Sub Registrar
P4418|(Note: There shall be fields to capture the payment of consideration before Sub Registrar as shown above)
P4420|8. Registration Certificate as per Section 60 of Registration Act, 1908
P4421|Registration Certificate will have the Book Number, Document Number which consists of Office name in Trilateral Letters i.e., Abbreviation of the Sub Registrar’s Office Name, Book Number and Serial Number of the Document of the year and Date followed by Sub Registrar Signature
P4423|(Note: The Endorsement is usually printed on the back of the Deed Document; if in case the space isn’t available on the back of the deed document, then a separate sheet/s is used for printing the endorsement)
P4424|Endorsement for PENDING DOCUMENTS
P4426|Sub Registrar may keep the documents pending due to various reasons which is to be recorded in the Minute Book 6
P4428|Pending for determination of undervaluation, referred to District Registrar under section 45A of Karnataka Stamp Act, 1957
P4429|Pending for short levy of Stamp Duty, impounded under section 33 of Karnataka Stamp Act, 1957
P4430|Pending for condonation of delay, under section 25 of Registration Act, 1908, document exceeding 4 months from the date of last execution read with Rule 52 of Registration Rule, 1965
P4431|Pending for appearance of the parties under section 34 of Registration Act, 1908.
P4432|Pending for private attendance under section 31 of Registration Act, 1908
P4435|Pending for determination of undervaluation, referred to District Registrar under section 45A of Karnataka Stamp Act 1957
P4437|The market value or consideration set forth in the document is less than estimated guidance value published under section 45B of Karnataka Stamp Act, 1957, then the party has got option to pay stamp duty as per the guidance value determined by the SR or if he dis-agrees, the document may be kept pending from registration and referred to the DR for determination of valuation. At this stage, the document is suspended from the further process of registration until the DR Order and payment is made by the party.
P4439|For the document kept pending,
P4440|“The document is referred u/s 45A of KS Act, 1957 for determination of under-valuation”
P4441|should be printed on the Endorsement of the document.
P4442|Signature of the Sub Registrar
P4444|Also, having kept the document pending, there should be a record in the Minute Book 6 in the following manner.
P4446|“………………… (Remarks: (if any, to be made by the SR here)) ……………………”
P4448|Signature of the Sub Registrar
P4450|(Note: 1. The fields in the above table should be auto-fetched when the SR keeps the document kept pending u/s 45A and there should be provision to record remarks by the SR;
P4451|2. There should be a provision for payment of stamp duty and registration fee through add challan;
P4452|3. There should be provision to record the remarks by the SR;
P4453|4. Instead of Certificate u/s 60 of Registration Act, 1908, the pending remarks should be recorded as mentioned above (ie., the remarks made by SR)
P4455|After payment of stamp duty (on the Orders of the District Registrar), the document suspended from the further process of registration has to be retrieved from the pending status to continue registration. After continuing the registration, the document gets registered. Having registered the document, there shall be recording in the minute book regarding the valuation assessed by the DR and stamp duty fee paid by the party on the orders of the DR in the following table
P4457|Final Registration Number of the document – should be auto-fetched
P4459|Signature of the Sub Registrar
P4461|There should be remarks entered in the endorsement recording the stamp duty and registration fee paid by the party on the orders of the DR. The final certificate of registration u/s 60 of RS Act, 1908 shall be printed on the endorsement of the document

P4525|Pending for short levy of Stamp Duty, impounded under section 33 of Karnataka Stamp Act, 1957
P4527|The document presented for registration is under-stamped ie., in which the stamp duty isn’t paid as per the articles of stamp. Under the provision of section 33 of Karnataka Stamp Act, 1957, the document is impounded. The document is kept pending from registration and impounded for payment of difference of stamp duty and penalty. At this stage, the document is suspended from the further process of registration until the payment of stamp duty and penalty under the Orders of the DR.

P4531|should be printed on the Endorsement of the document.

P4611|Pending for Condonation of Delay under section 25 of Registration Act, 1908, document exceeding 4 months from the date of last execution read with Rule 52 of Karnataka Registration Rules, 1965
P4613|If the document presented for registration exceeds 4 months from the date of execution, the document may be kept pending from registration and referred to the DR for condonation of delay in presentation. At this stage, the document is suspended from the further process of registration until the Orders of the DR for condonation of delay and payment of fine is made by the party.
P4615|For the document kept pending,
P4616|“The document is referred u/s 25 of KS Act, 1957 for condonation of delay”
P4617|should be printed on the Endorsement of the document.
P4619|Signature of the Sub Registrar
P4621|Also, having kept the document pending, there should be a record in the Minute Book 6 in the following manner.
P4623|After 4 months from the Date of Execution, any one of the following mentioned below (based on the duration of delay) should appear in the minute book 6:
P4624|Not exceeding one week – fine equal to registration fee (1 time)
P4625|Exceeding one week not exceeding one month – fine equal to 2 times the registration fees
P4626|Exceeding one month not exceeding two months – fine equal to 5 times the registration fees
P4627|Exceeding two months not exceeding four months – fine equal to 10 times the registration fees
P4628|“………………… (Remarks: (if any, to be made by the SR here)) ……………………”
P4629|Final Registration Number of the document – should be auto-fetched
P4631|Signature of the Sub Registrar
P4633|(Note: 1. Only one among the 4 options mentioned above should appear in the minute book 6;
P4634|2.  There should be a provision for entering the amount of fine to be levied depending upon the duration of delay;
P4635|3. There should be a provision for payment of fine through add challan;
P4636|4. There should be a provision to record fine paid (to be auto-fetched) from the challan
P4637|5. There should be provision to record the remarks by the SR;
P4638|6. Instead of Certificate u/s 60 of Registration Act, 1908, the pending remarks should be recorded as mentioned above (ie., the remarks made by SR)
P4640|After payment of fine and on the Orders of the DR, the document suspended from the further process of registration has to be retrieved from the pending status to continue registration.

P4885|Pending for Private Attendance under section 31 of Registration Act, 1908
P4887|In the exigencies, ill-health or detained in custody, if requested for visiting to private residence on payment of private residence fee and conveyance, the SR visits the private residence of the party or detention place and registers the document.
P4888|SCENARIO 1
P4889|Where the document is Presented and kept pending for private residence attendance
P4891|On the presentation of the document, the parties to the document appear before the SR for admission of execution, under special circumstances any of the claimant/s or executant/s due to exigencies, ill-health or detained in custody, on request for private residence attendance, the document will be kept pending to admit the execution at their private residence. So on payment of private residence attendance fee and conveyance, the SR shall visit the place where the party resides. The SR admits the execution recording the address, boundaries of the place, time and takes the photo and biometrics of the party and the witnesses, also records the deposition of the party. The SR after arriving back to the SR office, orders for registration.

P4962|SCENARIO 2
P4963|Where the document is Presented and kept pending for private residence attendance; but party residing Outside the Jurisdiction of the SR
P4965|On the presentation of the document, the parties to the document appear before the SR for admission of execution, under special circumstances any of the claimant/s or executant/s due to exigencies, ill-health or detained in custody, on request for private residence attendance, the document will be kept pending to admit the execution at their private residence. So on payment of private residence attendance fee and conveyance, if the party is residing outside the jurisdiction of the SR, then the commission for private residence attendance has to be sent to the jurisdictional SR under whose jurisdiction the party resides. The SR of the concerned jurisdiction shall visit the place where the party resides. The SR of the concerned jurisdiction admits the execution recording the address, boundaries of the place, time and takes the photo and biometrics of the party and the witnesses, also records the deposition of the party. The SR of the concerned jurisdiction after arriving back to the SR office will send the commission report to the originating SR for further process registration. After receiving the commission report, the document will be ordered for registration.

P5204|Stamp Duty on DUPLICATE DOCUMENT presented along with the original document
P5207|Usually in case of Deeds of Partition, Lease, Agreement, Settlement, Duplicate, Counterpart, Supplemental Deeds, Further Charge on Simple Mortgage, Subsidiary, Collateral, Additional and Substituted Security, etc., along with the original deed, the duplicate or triplicate copies of the deed will be presented for registration to enable the parties to have the copy of the document for each one.
P5209|Under these circumstances, the stamp duty paid as per the applicable article of stamps is calculated and additional stamp duty for each duplicate copy at the rate of Rs.500 each is also paid. The original stamp duty paid as per the article of the stamp act is certified on the original document under section 10A of Karnataka Stamp Act, 1957. The remaining stamp duty paid for each duplicate shall be recorded on the duplicate copies in 10A Certificate. After 10A certificate of Rs.500 on the duplicate, there shall be denoting of the original stamp duty paid on the original deed to be recorded as follows:
P5211|“I hereby certify that on production of the original document, I have satisfied myself that the stamp duty of Rs…………. has been paid thereon”
P5213|Sub Registrar & Proper Officer

P5260|CANCELLATION DEED
P5262|Brief Description
P5263|Brief Description
P5264|Cancellation, etc, of a registered document. ----
P5265|(i) On the registration of a document which revokes or cancels or rectifies an error, in, or modifies the terms of a document previously registered in the same class or Register book or of a return of lands acquired under the Land Acquisition Act or of a document received and filed under Section 89 (vide Rule 17), or on the receipt of a communication from a Revenue Officer of from a Court which intimates a similar revocations, cancellation, rectification or modification, a note shall be entered at the foot of the entry of the latter document of communication as under.---
P5267|“This Document/Communication revokes (cancels, rectifies or modifies) Document No. of ……. Copied/the document filed/ the return filed at pages ….. Volume of book/supplement to Book No. 1. (Part)/file book ……………… and at the foot of the previous entry or of the document previously registered or filed a note shall be entered as shown below”
P5269|“This document/return has been revoked (cancelled, rectified or modified by Document No………. of ………… copied/the document filed/ the return filed at pages……. Volume of book/ supplement to Book No.1(part)/file book”
P5271|(ii) When the revocation, cancellation rectification or modification is of a document relating to immovable property, a corresponding note shall also be entered in Index No. II and when it relates to the rectification of any particular item entered Index, I,II,III or IV, a note of rectification shall also be entered in the respective index against the particular item rectified.

P5522|REGISTRATION AND CANCELLATION OF WILL
P5524|Registration of Will
P5526|Brief Description
P5528|Brief Description:
P5529|Will is a Testamentary Document executed by the testator with respect to his/her will to distribute his/her property after his/her demise. Will may be either Registered in the Office of the Sub-Registrar or Deposited in the Office of the District-Registrar. Will might either be Registered or Not. It has its own validity.
P5530|For Registration of Will, the testator may get registered as below:
P5532|SCENARIO 1
P5533|The Testator gets his Will Registered
P5535|27.1.2.1 Brief Description
P5536|Brief Description:
P5537|Registration of Will
P5539|27.1.2.2 Process Flow
P5540|Process Flow Diagram:
P5543|Figure 1: Process Flow Diagram for Will Registration (by the Testator)
P5545|27.1.2.3 Functional Step by Step Process
P5546|Functional Step by Step Process:
P5547|For Registration of Will, the testator may get registered as below:
P5548|User Registration (usual flow)
P5549|Applicant Login
P5550|To file the application online, the applicant shall browse for Kaveri Online Services ie., Kaveri2.0
P5551|The applicant shall register as a user by creating user id and password
P5552|The applicant shall now login in the Kaveri2.0 portal
P5553|Document Information (usual flow)
P5554|The applicant shall click on ‘Start a New Application’
P5555|The applicant shall select ‘Document Registration’ from among the ‘Type of Service’ option on the screen
P5556|Property Search (NOT APPLICABLE)
P5557|The applicant shall select ‘Will/Miscellaneous’ from among the ‘Type of Document’ option
P5558|The applicant shall select ‘Will’ from within the ‘Will/Miscellaneous’ option
P5559|Property Search isn’t required
P5560|Document Information (usual flow)
P5561|The applicant shall select the following details from the dropdown menu
P5562|Nature of Document as ‘Will’
P5563|Article – Not Applicable ‘Will’
P5564|Sub-article – Not Applicable ‘Will’
P5565|Book Number as ‘3’ (will be auto-fetched)
P5566|Property Schedule (NOT APPLICABLE)
P5567|Property Schedule isn’t required
P5568|Party Details
P5569|The Presenter – the presenter will be Testator only
P5570|The Executant – the Presenter himself/herself is the Executant
P5571|Claimant - there shall be No Claimant
P5572|Witness – 2 witnesses shall be required
P5573|(Note: The can be more than one executants; In case the applicant (testator) applies for Private Residence Attendance by clicking on the private attendance checkbox, then the flow should follow the private attendance procedure)
P5574|MV, Stamp Duty & Fee Calculation
P5575|No MV (Market Valuation) and No SD (Stamp Duty)
P5576|RF (Registration Fee) = Rs.200 (fixed)
P5577|Scanning Fee (Rs.35/- per page of scanning)
P5578|(Note: The concept of Condonation Delay isn’t applicable in Registration of Will ie., the will document can be registered even after 8 months from the date of execution of will document)
P5579|Online Document Summary (usual flow)
P5580|(Note: As Will is a Confidential Document, the copy of the document shall be uploaded, but only the SR shall have the access to view it)
P5581|SRO Confirmation (usual flow)
P5582|Follow the usual flow, but the SR and FDA/SDA shall verify only the Total Payable Amount
P5583|Payment (usual flow)
P5584|Appointment Scheduler (usual flow)
P5585|Document Summary/Signature (usual flow)
P5586|Document Registration (usual flow)
P5587|(Note: The Registered Will goes to Book 3 (for Testamentary documents))
P5588|Exceptional Cases (usual flow)
P5589|(Note: In case the applicant (testator) applies for Private Residence Attendance by clicking on the private attendance checkbox in the Party Details screen, then the flow should follow the private attendance procedure)
P5593|SCENARIO 2
P5594|The Will Registration after the death of the Testator
P5596|27.1.3.1 Brief Description
P5597|Brief Description:
P5598|If the Will is returned by the Testator, and not registered till his/her demise, the beneficiary or the legal heirs may present the will for registration after the demise of the Testator.

P5602|Please use the above link to access Will after death of testator detailed FRS.

P5632|Party Details
P5633|The Presenter is either a beneficiary or a legal heir
P5634|The Executant can either be the Authority Adopted, Executors (2 Witnesses), and Scribe (Lawyer/Deed Writer), (as the Testator who was supposed to the executant is demised)
P5635|(Note: The applicant has to upload the Death Certificate of the Testator in the upload section)
P5636|The Beneficiary (as called here in this case, instead of Claimant) is the beneficiary or legal heirs
P5637|MV, Stamp Duty & Fee Calculation

P5643|Death Certificate
P5644|Deed Document
P5645|Annexures/Enclosures – ID Proof & Address Proof of Beneficiary/ Legal heirs, Witnesses and Scribe (Lawyer/Deed Writer), etc.
P5646|SRO Confirmation (usual flow)
P5647|Payment (usual flow)
P5648|Appointment Scheduler (usual flow)
P5649|Document Summary/Signature
P5650|On the day of Appointment, only the Presenter and the Beneficiary (ie., the claimant/s) shall appear before the Sub-Registrar at SRO
P5651|The Executant (Executors (2 Witnesses) or Authority Adopted) shall be SUMMONED by the Sub-Registrar at DIIFFERENT TIMES (for each executant) to take their Deposition (by SR), Photo & Thumb Impression (by DEO)
P5652|The Generation & Print of Summary must be done for each executor separately. An option has to be provided for the same

P5654|(Note: The Registered Will goes to Book 3 (for Testamentary documents))

P5824|MARRIAGE REGISTRATION MODULE
P5826|Brief Description
P5828|In addition to the information below, please find a detailed requirements document attached:
P5829|https://csgkarnataka.sharepoint.com/:w:/r/sites/ULMS/Knowledge%20Base/FRS/Submitted/Marriage%20Registration%20Module_V3.0.docx?d=wd99ada19c4b64b21a7a44903e47c72ad&csf=1&web=1&e=20yN0J
P5830|Please use the above link to access Marriage registration detailed FRS.
P5832|Brief Description
P5833|Marriage is “a religious sacrament in which a man and a woman are bound in a permanent relationship for the physical, social, spiritual, procreation and sexual needs; a formal union and social and legal contract between two individuals that unites their lives legally, economically, and emotionally”. The contractual marriage agreement usually implies that the couple has legal obligations to each other throughout their lives or until they decide to divorce. Being married also gives legitimacy to sexual relations within the marriage.
P5834|Overview
P5835|Any citizen can avail the service. This service generates a certificate which is accepted as legal proof for marriage. The SR is the person authorized to issue Marriage certificate and also solemnize the marriage as the case may be. This service can be availed at the SRO.
P5836|Marriages are registered under following acts:
P5837|1. Hindu Marriage Act, 1955
P5838|2. Special Marriage Act, 1954
P5839|3. Parsi Marriage and Divorce Act, 1936
P5840|4. Karnataka Marriage (Registration and miscellaneous provision) Act, 1976
P5841|Marriage under Hindu Marriage Act can be registered in the office of the Registrar of Marriages situated in the jurisdiction of which marriage has taken place or where bride or bride-groom resides. Though actual marriage rituals can be performed outside, the filing or registration of marriage is done at SRO. When it is a special marriage it is done through notice for intended marriage and the SR solemnizes the marriage in his presence in his office.
P5842|Actors
P5843|Bride and Groom – Presents the relevant records/documents for registration
P5844|Witnesses – witnessing the marriage
P5845|Objector – one who objects to the marriage (special marriage intended notice & special marriage of other forms)
P5846|SRO – Scrutinizes document, admits for registration, signs the marriage certificate
P5847|Operators (FDA/SDA) – Enters data, captures/attaches photo, generates registration number scans and archives documents
P5848|Kaveri 2.0 – Software used for registration
P5850|Inputs
P5851|Age proof
P5852|Proof of residence (both Permanent & Current)
P5853|Place of marriage (Marriage card)
P5854|Application in prescribed form for Marriage Registration
P5855|Declaration by the Bride and Groom
P5856|Memorandum of Marriages containing details of bride, groom and witnesses
P5857|Enclosures
P5859|Output
P5860|The Marriage Certificate is issued by the Sub Registrar (SR/MO/RoM)

P5990|The system requests the applicant to fill in the following entry fields (in general):

P5995|(Note: 1. B2 size (500mm*707 mm) Joint photo of the bridegroom and the bride should be in jpg format; size shouldn’t exceed 2 MB; 2. The uploaded Joint photo of the bridegroom and the bride shall be manually verified by the SR/MO/ FDA/SDA before proceeding for the payment and booking appointment and also physically during the day of appointment at SRO)
P5997|The applicant has to fill the following entry fields for Bridegroom Details:
P5998|Salutation (mandatory field)
P5999|First Name, Middle Name, Last Name (only First Name is mandatory field)
P6000|Date of Birth (DoB) (optional field)
P6001|Age (in years) (should auto-fetch once DoB is entered; system should register only if age>=21 years for bridegroom) (mandatory field)
P6002|(Note: 1. The age of the bridegroom must be greater than or equal to 21 years; 2. The age must be rounded off to the lower whole number ie., if the age is 21.3 years, then then system should take 21 years only; if the age is 21.9 years, then then system should take 21 years only ie., the system must always take the lower side value until it is a whole number; 2. If DoB is fed, then the age should auto-fetch; 3. If the applicant doesn’t know his DoB, then the applicant must enter the age directly and must get a Doctor Certificate with Affidavit (or Other Supporting Document) and upload it in the upload document section))
P6003|Mobile Number (mandatory field)
P6004|Email ID (mandatory field)
P6005|Current Place of Residence (Current Address) – House No./Building Name, Street Address, State, District, Taluka, Hobli/Ward, Village/City, Pincode (all are mandatory fields)
P6006|Permanent Place of Residence (Permanent Address) – House No./Building Name, Street Address, State, District, Taluka, Hobli/Ward, Village/City, Pincode (all are mandatory fields)
P6007|(Note: A ‘Checkbox’ saying ‘Permanent address is same as Current address’ has to be given after Current Place of Residence (and before Permanent Place of Residence); if checked, then the same details as entered in the Current Place of Residence should be auto-filled in place of Permanent Place of Residence; ifn’t checked, then the applicant has to manually entered the required details)
P6008|Status of Marriage (at the time of marriage) – Unmarried/ Divorcee/ Widow(er) (mandatory field)

P6110|We hereby declare,
P6111|(I) That a valid marriage was solemnized between us and that the marriage is, capable of being registered under section 8 of the marriage is, capable of being registered under section 8 of the Hindu Marriage Act, 1955 (Central Act 25 of 1955)
P6112|(II) That the conditions laid down in section 5 of the said Act have been satisfied and

P6125|Scan & Upload of Signed Application Form(s) & Annexures/Enclosures by the Applicant

P6171|Display of Fee Structure

P6178|Total Payable Amount = Rs…… (= a + b + c + d)
P6179|(Note: At this stage, the system has to just show the calculation of (breakup of) the fee and the applicant doesn’t make any payment; at a later stage, after verification and approval of data entered by the applicant by FDA/SDA/SR/RoM only, the applicant has to make the payment; the breakup fee calculation is being shown to ensure that the applicant has to be kept informed about the fee he/she is supposed to pay (transparency))
P6181|Submission of Application Form
P6182|The applicant clicks on ‘Submit’ button to submit the application online. The applicant will get an application number generated as acknowledgement and receives an email/sms as notification
P6183|On submission of application, a dialogue box/ flash message should appear stating ‘Your application has been successfully submitted’
P6184|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also status gets updated in the applicant login (and department login) on Kaveri2.0; the application gets logged in in order in the SR login)
P6186|Steps taken by SR/RoM at SR Office to Register Marriage and to secure Marriage Certificate to the Applicant(s)
P6188|SR/RoM Login and Allocation/Verification by SR/RoM
P6189|The Sub Registrar (SR)/ Registrar of Marriage (RoM) shall login to the Kaveri 2.0 system
P6190|From the menu on his dashboard, the SR/RoM shall click on ‘Marriage Application’
P6191|The SR/RoM receives the marriage applications irrespective of their types in one place. Table is supposed to be FIFO
P6192|The SR/RoM can take the following actions:
P6193|View - The SR/RoM views the firm application by clicking on ‘View’ button, and then allocates to FDA/SDA/Self
P6194|Allocate – The SR/RoM can allocate the firm application to FDA/SDA/Self by clicking on ‘Allocate’ button
P6195|(Note: 1. The dropdown which fetches all the FDA/SDAs in the office who are present on that particular day; 2. In case there are many FDAs/SDAs, then SR/RoM has the option allocate to a particular FDA/SDA of his choice from the dropdown list)
P6196|(Note: If the SR/RoM allocates to himself/herself by choosing the ‘Self’ in the drop menu, then the action button must change to ‘Verify’ and NOT ‘Allocate’. In this case, there are NO FDA/SDA Remarks on the UI. The SR/RoM himself/herself verifies (the application and enclosures) and sends back to the applicant by clicking on ‘Send to Applicant’ button)
P6198|FDA/SDA Login and Verification by the FDA/SDA
P6199|The FDA/SDA shall login
P6200|All the applications regardless of their type that the SR/RoM allocates are supposed to show on FDA/SDA dashboard. Table is supposed to be FIFO.
P6201|The FDA/SDA shall start the verification process by selecting ‘Start Verification’ button
P6202|The FDA/SDA Application Verification screen is split into 2 sides:
P6203|LHS (Left hand side of the page) – the Summary sheet containing all the details which the applicant has entered (marriage details, bridegroom details, bride details, witness details, fee details), a section to add his Remarks and a button to send it back to the SR/RoM
P6204|RHS (Right hand side of the page) – contains the Uploads by the applicant which are Form I and 1-A and the Enclosures/Annexures
P6205|The FDA/SDA shall crosscheck the applications and the enclosures thoroughly; FDA/SDA fills a Checklist mentioning Remarks for each category – marriage details, bridegroom details, bride details, witnesses’ details, uploaded documents; sends it back to the SR/RoM with Remarks by clicking on ‘Send back to SR’ button
P6207|Verification & Approval by the SR/RoM
P6208|After the application is received from FDS/SDA, the SR/RoM shall login and shall view the remarks made by FDA/SDA by clicking on ‘View’ (to view the remarks) and shall add his/her own remarks and shall send it back to the applicant for either to attend to the remarks made and resend the application (in the applicant login, ‘Your application cannot be registered as it is, Kindly Refer to the Sub Registrar Remarks to know more’ message shall be displayed) or to proceed to ‘Make Payment’
P6209|The SR/RoM has additional checkbox which if ticked, then it takes the applicant to ‘Make Payment’ option; ifn’t ticked will request the applicant to make changes (as per remarks made by SR/RoM) in the application and re-submit the application
P6210|(Note: 1. When the changes (attend to SR/RoM remarks) are requested by the SR/RoM to the applicant, the system takes the applicant to the ‘Marriage Details’ screen with the pre-filled data being retained as earlier over which he can over-write to make changes and then shall send back to the SR/RoM by pressing the ‘Submit’ button; 2. If the applicant wants to change the type of marriage, then he/she has to re-apply)
P6211|On the Remarks section, there is a checklist - If the checkbox in the checklist is ticked, the remarks are voluntary; ifn’t ticked, then the remarks are mandatory
P6212|(Note: 1. In either case, the applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0 dashboard; also the status of the application gets updated in the applicant (and department) login/dashboard on Kaveri2.0; 3. Until unless ‘Can Proceed to Make Payment’ is checked, the applicant can’t proceed to make the payment)
P6213|(Note: 1. If the applicant is willing to attend to the remarks made by SR/RoM, then resend the application after corrections are made. If not willing to attend to the remarks made by SR/RoM, then the system shall generate the Endorsement with Reason for Rejection; 2. Based on the remarks made by the FDA/SDA, the SR/RoM has the option to change (make modification) to the number of pages to be scanned)
P6215|Payment of Fee by the Applicant
P6216|After SR/RoM accepts and approves the application, the applicant shall login in the Kaveri 2.0 portal and make the Payment of Fee (Total Payable Amount) online through K2 portal by clicking on ‘Make Payment’ button
P6217|(Note: The system should show the calculated fee (freeze it and no changes can be made by the applicant at this stage) before applicant makes the payment and then give ‘Make Payment’ option)

P6219|Marriage Certificate Fee = Rs.20 (fixed)

P6234|Cancel Appointment and
P6235|Re-schedule Appointment

P6238|Marriage Registration at SRO on the Day of Appointment

P6241|Allocation & Approval by SR/RoM for Registration
P6242|The SR/RoM shall login to the Kaveri 2.0 system
P6243|From the menu on his dashboard, the SR/RoM shall click on ‘Marriage Application’
P6244|The SR/RoM shall click on ‘Marriage Appointment’ button
P6245|(Note: All the marriage appointments irrespective of their types for the day are listed in a table)
P6246|The SR/RoM can take the following actions:
P6247|Allocate – the SR/RoM can allocate the firm appointments to FDA/SDA/Self by clicking on ‘Allocate’ button so that they can verify, capture/edit the required details and generate the necessary forms/documents needed for registration
P6248|(Note: 1. The dropdown which fetches all the FDA/SDAs in the office who are present on that particular day; 2. In case there are many FDAs/SDAs, then SR/RoM has the option allocate to a particular FDA/SDA of his choice from the dropdown list)
P6249|Approve – after the FDA/SDA has generated the required forms, they are sent to the SR/RoM for approval; The SR/RoM shall approve by clicking on the ‘Approve’ button and only after his/her approval, the marriage registration number shall be generated by the system and shall be sent back to the FDA/SDA for further processing
P6250|(Note: Generally, the SR/RoM shall allocate to FDA/SDA to verify, capture/edit the required details and generate the necessary forms/documents needed for registration. But in an extra-ordinary situation where there are NO FDA/SDAs (either absent on that particular day or permanently position is absent), the SR/RoM allocates to himself/herself by choosing the ‘Self’ in the drop menu, then the action button must change to ‘Start Registration’ and NOT ‘Allocate’. Then the SR/RoM himself/herself verifies, captures/edits the required details and generates the necessary forms/documents and shall approve the application in the system by clicking on the ‘Check & Register’ button)
P6252|Steps taken by the FDA/SDA to Process the Marriage Registration Certificate
P6253|The marriage appointments allocated to the FDA/SDA by the SR/RoM have to be displayed on the FDA/SDA dashboard. The table shall be in FIFO
P6254|The FDA/SDA shall click on the ‘Start Registration’ button
P6255|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also status gets updated in the applicant login (and department login) on Kaveri2.0)
P6256|The FDA/SDA shall Review the Physical Documents, Application Summary and if everything is in order
P6257|The FDA/SDA shall Capture/Modify/Edit the basic details of the 3 Witnesses (ifn’t captured earlier or if change is required)
P6258|(Note: 1. The age of the witnesses should be >= 18 years; 2. Capture the details of 3 witnesses at the beginning (ie., during filling up the application form of notice); but later SDA/FDA should be given an ‘Edit Option’ to change the witness details keeping in mind the exigencies (death, cancellation, busy schedule, not showing up on the particular day, etc.))
P6259|(Note: In the case where the FDA/SDA edits the witness(es)’ details, then the marriage application form (Form 1) (if any & if required) has to be printed again and signatures of the bridegroom, bride and 3 witnesses have to be taken again)
