P6265|(Note: Marriage registration number isn’t generated yet)
P6266|(Note: Required Forms – Marriage Registration Certificate (without Registration Number, Fee Payment Receipt)
P6267|(Note: Here, the SDA/FDA has to be given an edit option to edit the details (if and wherever required))
P6268|The FDA/SDA shall Generate the Summary Report by clicking the ‘Generate Certificate and Forms’ button
P6269|The FDA/SDA shall send it (above mentioned certificate and forms) to the SR/RoM for Approval (Check & Register) by clicking on ‘Send back to SR/RoM’ button
P6270|The SR/RoM shall view the certificate and forms generated by the FDA/SDA, and shall approve the application in the system by clicking on the ‘Check & Register’ button
P6271|The system generates Marriage Registration Certificate with Registration Number
P6272|(Note: pop up message should appear once he clicks on ‘Check and Register’ saying that ‘Are you sure to Register? You cannot change later’ for which the SR/RoM has to confirm)
P6273|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also the status gets updated in the applicant login (and department login))
P6274|In the back end, the Index of Marriage are stored in Index Register (Forms V & VI for wife and husband separately)
P6275|After Approval by SR/RoM, the firm registration certificate is pushed back to the FDA/SDA dashboard
P6276|The FDA/SDA shall click on the ‘Print Certificate and Forms’ button to Print the Marriage Certificate (with Registration Number) (and other forms wherever required) and shall get them signed by the applicants and the witnesses (if/wherever required)
P6277|The FDA/SDA shall physically affix the colour joint-photo of the couple and get the cross-signatures of bridegroom and bride on the pasted joint photo of the couple (as the joint photo of the couple isn’t captured by the FDA/SDA)
P6278|The FDA/SDA shall get Seal and Signature affixed by the SR/RoM on the Marriage Certificate (with Registration Number) (and other forms wherever required)
P6279|The FDA/SDA shall click on the ‘Upload’ button
P6280|The FDA/SDA upload screen shall display the following:
P6281|Details: Application Number, Application Type, Date of Marriage

P6285|Marriage Application Form (ie., Memorandum of Marriage) (Form I)

P6287|Form II (Endorsement – having received memorandum of marriage by SRO and having registered in the system)

P6290|Enclosures/Annexures
P6291|The FDA/SDA shall click on the ‘Save and Send back to SR/RoM’ button and shall forward it to the SR
P6292|The SR shall view the documents uploaded by FDA/SDA, then shall click on ‘Digital Sign & Complete’ button to put his digital signature, encrypt and to upload all the saved documents
P6293|(Note: pop up message should appear once he clicks on ‘Digital Sign & Complete’ button saying that ‘Are you sure to digitally sign and upload? Once uploaded, the details cannot be changed’ for which the SR/RoM has to confirm)
P6294|(Note: An option may be provided for the SR/RoM if re-scan is required; on clicking on the ‘Send for Re-scan’ button by SR/RoM, it would request the SDA/FDA to re-scan and send it back to SR/RoM; and then SR/RoM shall view the scanned documents and shall proceed further by clicking on the ‘Digital Sign & Complete’ button)
P6295|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also the status gets updated in the applicant login (and department login))
P6296|Any Additional Payment by the applicant(s)/objector(s) is made through Cash; the applicant(s)/objector(s) receives an Acknowledgement (a copy of Fee Receipt has to be generated by the FDA/SDA on receipt of cash)
P6297|The scanned documents are sent to RGoM (Registrar General of Marriage)
P6298|The Marriage Registration Certificate is handed over physically to the applicant after receiving the acknowledgement and the applicant shall collect the Marriage Certificate
P6299|The Sakala is updated
P6301|(Note: 1. If applied for CC (Certified Copy) for Marriage Certificate, then Digitally/Physically Signed Copy from SR/RoM may be issued by filling appropriate form; 2. In case any changes to be done to the Registration of Marriage due to erroneous data entry, the same shall be done in the system with approval from SR)

P6392|Special Marriage (Intended Marriage Notice)
P6393|Brief Description:
P6394|The applicant chooses the ‘Special Marriage (Intended Marriage Notice)’ from among the three options if the following conditions are satisfied:
P6395|If the applicant/s (bride &/or bridegroom) are of different religion (other than hindu religion) or combination of hindu with other religion, and
P6396|Marriage hasn’t happened yet (pre-marriage)

P6405|Functional Step by Step Process:
P6407|Steps taken by Citizen/ Applicant to file a Marriage Registration Online
P6409|Applicant Login
P6410|To file the application online, the applicant shall browse for Kaveri Online Services ie., Kaveri2.0
P6411|The applicant shall register as a user by creating user id and password
P6412|The applicant shall now login in the Kaveri2.0 portal
P6413|The applicant shall click on ‘Start a New Application’
P6414|The applicant shall click on ‘Marriage Registration’ on the screen
P6416|The applicant shall select the ‘Type of Marriage’
P6417|(Note: The applicant shall select among the three types of marriage options as per the requirement (satisfying certain conditions). The following types of marriage options are available to the applicant:
P6418|Hindu Marriage,
P6419|Special Marriage (Intended Marriage Notice) and
P6420|Special Marriage of Other Forms

P6422|(Note: The applicant shall choose the ‘Special Marriage (Intended Marriage Notice)’ from among the three options if the following conditions are satisfied:
P6423|If the applicant/s (bride &/or bridegroom) are of different religion (other than hindu religion) or combination of hindu with other religion, and
P6424|Marriage is yet to happened (pre-marriage)
P6426|The system requests the applicant to fill in the following entry fields (in general):
P6427|The applicant has to fill the following entry fields for Marriage Details:
P6428|Whether the Applicant is Bridegroom or Bride? (Radio button) (mandatory field)
P6429|Date of Marriage (signifies the date on which the marriage took place) – NOT APPLICABLE as the marriage hasn’t yet happened
P6430|Address of Marriage Venue (Place of Marriage) - House No./Building Name, Street Address, State, District, Taluka, Hobli/Ward, Village/City, Pincode (all are mandatory fields) – NOT APPLICABLE as the marriage hasn’t yet happened
P6431|Photo of the Bridegroom (upload) (mandatory field)
P6432|Photo of the Bride (upload) (mandatory field)
P6433|(Note: 1. Passport size (51mm*51mm) photos of the bridegroom and the bride should be in jpg format; size shouldn’t exceed 2 MB; 2. The uploaded photos of the bridegroom and the bride shall be manually verified by the SR/MO/ FDA/SDA before proceeding for the payment and booking appointment and also physically during the day of appointment at SRO)
P6434|The applicant has to fill the following entry fields for Bridegroom Details:
P6435|Salutation (mandatory field)
P6436|First Name, Middle Name, Last Name (only First Name is mandatory field)
P6437|Date of Birth (DoB) (optional field)
P6438|Age (in years) (should auto-fetch once DoB is entered; system should register only if age>=21 years for bridegroom) (mandatory field)
P6439|(Note: 1. The age of the bridegroom must be greater than or equal to 21 years; 2. The age must be rounded off to the lower whole number ie., if the age is 21.3 years, then then system should take 21 years only; if the age is 21.9 years, then then system should take 21 years only ie., the system must always take the lower side value until it is a whole number; 2. If DoB is fed, then the age should auto-fetch; 3. If the applicant doesn’t know his DoB, then the applicant must enter the age directly and must get a Doctor Certificate with Affidavit (or Other Supporting Document) and upload it in the upload document section)
P6440|Occupation (mandatory field)
P6441|Mobile Number (mandatory field)
P6442|Email ID (mandatory field)
P6443|Current Place of Residence (Current Address) – House No./Building Name, Street Address, State, District, Taluka, Hobli/Ward, Village/City, Pincode (all are mandatory fields)
P6444|Permanent Place of Residence (Permanent Address) – House No./Building Name, Street Address, State, District, Hobli/Ward, Village/City, Pincode (all are mandatory fields)
P6445|(Note: A ‘Checkbox’ saying ‘Permanent address is same as Current address’ has to be given after Current Place of Residence (and before Permanent Place of Residence); if checked, then the same details as entered in the Current Place of Residence should be auto-filled in place of Permanent Place of Residence; ifn’t checked, then the applicant has to manually entered the required details)
P6446|Duration of Residence at Current Address (Length of Residence) (calendar) (in days/months/years) (mandatory field)
P6447|Status of Marriage (at the time of marriage) – Unmarried/ Divorcee/ Widow(er) (mandatory field)
P6448|(Note: If any one of Divorcee/Widow is chosen, a pop up message (dialogue box)(flash message) should appear which says ‘please upload the supporting document in the upload page’ while uploading the other documents (then corresponding document supporting the same should be uploaded at a later stage while uploading the forms and enclosures in the ‘Upload Documents’ screen) ie., for Unmarried – no need to upload any document; for Divorcee – divorce certificate; for Widow(er) – widow(er)/death certificate of spouse)
P6449|Father’s Details – Salutation, First Name, Middle Name, Last Name (Salutation and First Name are mandatory fields)
P6450|Mother’s Details – Salutation, First Name, Middle Name, Last Name (Salutation and First Name are mandatory fields)
P6452|The applicant has to fill the following entry fields for Bride Details:
P6453|Salutation (mandatory field)
P6454|First Name, Middle Name, Last Name (only First Name is mandatory field)
P6455|Date of Birth (DoB) (optional field)
P6456|Age (in years) (should auto-fetch once DoB is entered; system should register only if age>=18 years for bridegroom) (mandatory field)
P6457|(Note: The age of the bridegroom must be greater than or equal to 18 years; 2. The age must be rounded off to the lower whole number ie., if the age is 18.3 years, then then system should take 18 years only; if the age is 18.9 years, then then system should take 18 years only ie., the system must always take the lower side value until it is a whole number; 2. If DoB is fed, then the age should auto-fetch; 3. If the applicant doesn’t know his DoB, then the applicant must enter the age directly and must get a Doctor Certificate with Affidavit (or Other Supporting Document) and upload it in the upload document section))

P6629|Steps taken by SR/MO at SR Office to Register Marriage and to secure Marriage Certificate to the Applicant(s)
P6630|SR/MO Login and Allocation/Verification by SR/MO
P6631|Sub Registrar (SR)/ Marriage Officer (MO) shall login to the Kaveri 2.0 system
P6632|From the menu on his dashboard, the SR/MO shall click on ‘Marriage Application’
P6633|The SR/MO receives the marriage applications irrespective of their types in one place. Table is supposed to be FIFO
P6634|The SR/MO can take the following actions:
P6635|View - The SR/ MO views the firm application by clicking on ‘View’ button, and then allocates to FDA/SDA/Self
P6636|Allocate – The SR/ MO can allocate the firm application to FDA/SDA/Self by clicking on ‘Allocate’ button
P6637|(Note: 1. The dropdown which fetches all the FDA/SDAs in the office who are present on that particular day; 2. In case there are many FDAs/SDAs, then SR/ MO has the option allocate to a particular FDA/SDA of his choice from the dropdown list)
P6638|(Note: If the SR/MO allocates to himself/herself by choosing the ‘Self’ in the drop menu, then the action button must change to ‘Verify’ and NOT ‘Allocate’. In this case, there are NO FDA/SDA Remarks on the UI. The SR/MO himself/herself verifies (the application and enclosures) and sends back to the applicant by clicking on ‘Send to Applicant’ button)
P6640|FDA/SDA Login and Verification by the FDA/SDA
P6641|The FDA/SDA shall login
P6642|All the applications regardless of their type that the SR/MO allocates are supposed to show on FDA/SDA dashboard. Table is supposed to be FIFO.
P6643|The FDA/SDA shall start the verification process by selecting ‘Start Verification’ button
P6644|The FDA/SDA Application Verification screen is split into 2 sides:
P6645|LHS (Left hand side of the page) – the Summary sheet containing all the details which the applicant has entered (marriage details, bridegroom details, bride details, witness details, fee details), a section to add his Remarks and a button to send it back to the SR/MO
P6646|RHS (Right hand side of the page) – contains the Uploads by the applicant which are Application Form and the Enclosures/Annexures
P6647|The FDA/SDA shall crosscheck the applications and the enclosures thoroughly; FDA/SDA fills a checklist mentioning Remarks for each category – marriage details, bridegroom details, bride details, witnesses’ details, uploaded documents; sends it back to the SR/MO with Remarks by clicking on ‘Send back to SR/MO’ button
P6649|Verification & Approval by the SR/MO
P6650|After the application is received from FDS/SDA, the SR/MO shall login and shall view the remarks made by FDA/SDA by clicking on ‘View’ (to view the remarks) and shall add his/her own remarks and shall send it back to the applicant for either to attend to the remarks made and resend the application (in the applicant login, ‘Your application cannot be registered as it is, Kindly Refer to the Sub Registrar Remarks to know more’ message shall be displayed) or to proceed to ‘Schedule an Appointment’ to physically handover the application form of notice to the SR/MO at SR Office (SRO)
P6651|The SR/MO has additional checkbox which if ticked, then it takes the applicant to make ‘Schedule an Appointment’ option; ifn’t ticked will request the applicant to make changes (as per remarks made by SR/MO) in the application and re-submit the application
P6652|(Note: 1. When the changes (attend to SR/MO remarks) are requested by the SR/MO to the applicant, the system takes the applicant to the ‘Marriage Details’ screen with the pre-filled data being retained as earlier over which he can over-write to make changes and then shall send back to the SR/MO by pressing the ‘Submit’ button; 2. If the applicant wants to change the type of marriage, then he/she has to re-apply)
P6653|On the Remarks section, there is a checklist - If the checkbox in the checklist is ticked, the remarks are voluntary; ifn’t ticked, then the remarks are mandatory
P6654|(Note: 1. In either case, the applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0 dashboard; also the status of the application gets updated in the applicant (and department) login/dashboard on Kaveri2.0; 3. Until unless ‘Schedule an Appointment’ is checked, the applicant can’t proceed to book an appointment)
P6655|(Note: 1. If the applicant is willing to attend to the remarks made by SR/MO, then resend the application after corrections are made. If not willing to attend to the remarks made by SR/MO, then the system shall generate the Endorsement with Reason for Rejection; 2. Based on the remarks made by the FDA/SDA, the SR/MO has the option to change (make modification) to the number of pages to be scanned)

P6728|If objection is not valid, then shall follow step 83
P6729|(Note: The applicants & objector(s) receive an email/sms as notification; also the status gets updated in the applicant login)
P6730|(Note: After the end of the 30 days (objection notice period), and in case (i) if no objection is filed or (ii) objection is over-ruled, then the SR/MO shall approve the application and the applicant receives an email/sms as notification and can proceed to make payment option

P6766|(Note: The Form I Notice generated has to be sent to the Applicants and Objector(s) through Post & Email/SMS calling for all the concerned parties (the Applicants, Objector(s) and their Witness(es)) to be present for the Enquiry)
P6767|On the scheduled date and time summoned by the SR/MO, in the presence of both Applicants & Objector(s), the SR/MO Conducts an Enquiry
P6768|Both the Applicants & the Objector(s) are given an opportunity to put forth their defence
P6769|‘Appointment for Enquiry’ is on the marriage appointment screen
P6770|He clicks on ‘Start Enquiry’ button
P6771|The screen displays the following:
P6772|Objection Number
P6773|Objector Name
P6774|Applicant Number
P6775|Applicant Name
P6776|Two Textboxes – one for each of the Depositions (Objector(s) & Applicants)
P6777|One Textbox for SR Order
P6778|Two buttons – Over-rule Objection, Sustain Objection
P6779|An option to Print (and download) Enquiry Order
P6780|If the witnesses won’t turn up on the scheduled date and time summoned by the SR/MO or if the party/objector(s) request for official summons to be issued by the SR/MO for the witness(es) to be present for the enquiry, then the SR/MO issues summons to the required/requested witness(es) (see Form II – Summons to Witness(es)
P6781|On request, the SR/MO can issue summons to witness(es) from either/both party and objector side; 3. The party/objector should first make a payment ie., Fee for issuing Summons to the Witnesses to be paid by Objector = Rs.0.50 (for each witness) through K2 and should inform the SR/MO, then only the SR/MO will Issue Summons to the witness(es) and schedules for another date and time calling them for an Enquiry; for this enquiry (2nd sitting), no official form I notice will be sent again to the applicants and the objector(s)
P6782|After the enquiry, the SR/MO writes the Inquiry Results (Inquiry Order/ Speaking Order) based on the merits and Records Inquiry Results & Scans the Order. The SR/MO can either Over-rule or Sustain the objection
P6783|If the objection is sustained, then the marriage application can’t be registered; the marriage shall not be solemnized, Refusal Order (Inquiry Results) shall be printed by FDA/SDA and given to the parties.
P6784|(Note: 1. The marriage applications status in the system to be changed to ‘Objection Raised and Sustained’ with an option to view the ‘Inquiry Order’
P6785|If the objection is over-ruled, then the marriage application is unaffected; then step 83 shall be followed
P6786|(Note: 1. The applicant & objector receive an email/sms as notification; also status gets updated in the applicant login; 2. Both the applicants and the objector(s) receive a scanned copy of the Inquiry Order via email (downloadable form) and/or post and/or physically handed over just after the enquiry)
P6787|(Note: After the end of the 30 days (objection notice period), and in case no objections are filed, the applicants along with 3 witnesses shall appear before the SR/MO for solemnization of their marriage within the next 2 calendar months (Total period shall not exceed 3 calendar months from the date of filing of notice))
P6788|After the end of the 30 days (objection notice period), and in case no objections are filed or in case the objection is over-ruled (ie., not sustained), then the SR/MO shall approve the application and the applicant receives an email/sms as notification and can proceed to make payment option
P6789|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also the status gets updated in the applicant login (and department login) on Kaveri2.0)

P6841|Steps taken by SRO (SR/MO) to Register Marriage and to secure Marriage Certificate to the Applicant(s)
P6842|On the day of appointment, the Applicants (bridegroom, bride) along with witnesses shall appear before SR/MO and shall present the physical application forms and memorandum of declaration
P6844|Oath & Solemnisation of Marriage by SR/MO
P6845|The parties (bridegroom, bride) along with 3 witnesses shall appear before SR/MO and shall get the marriage Solemnized as per the prescribed procedures:
P6846|SR/MO will seek to confirm that the applicants are about to enter into a marriage on their own free will,
P6847|Applicants will take the Solemn and Binding Marriage Oaths,
P6848|The Marriage Oath is signed by bride, bridegroom and counter-signed by SR/MO, and
P6849|The Marriage Declaration Document is signed by bride, bridegroom, 3 witnesses and counter-signed by SR/MO
P6850|(Note: The venue can either at SR Office (SRO) or at any other place (outside SRO) as requested by the applicant/parties by paying an additional charge (ie., fee for marriage outside the marriage office = Rs.15). The SR has the option either to accept or reject to visit the venue requested (any place outside SRO) based on his/her availability; if SR accepts (obliges), then the SR/MO would visit that venue to solemnise the marriage (the applicant has to arrange for transportation from and back to SRO office); if SR rejects to visit that venue, then the parties along with witnesses have to personally visit that SRO only on the given date and time of appointment)
P6852|Allocation & Approval by SR/MO for Registration
P6853|The SR/MO shall login to the Kaveri 2.0 system
P6854|From the menu on his dashboard, the SR/MO shall click on ‘Marriage Application’
P6855|The SR/MO shall click on ‘Marriage Appointment’ button
P6856|(Note: All the marriage appointments irrespective of their types for the day are listed in a table)
P6857|The SR/MO can take the following actions:
P6858|Allocate – the SR/MO can allocate the firm appointments to FDA/SDA/Self by clicking on ‘Allocate’ button so that they can verify, capture/edit the required details and generate the necessary forms/documents needed for registration
P6859|(Note: 1. The dropdown which fetches all the FDA/SDAs in the office who are present on that particular day; 2. In case there are many FDAs/SDAs, then SR/MO has the option allocate to a particular FDA/SDA of his choice from the dropdown list)
P6860|Approve – after the FDA/SDA has generated the required forms, they are sent to the SR/MO for approval; The SR/MO shall approve by clicking on the ‘Approve’ button and only after his/her approval, the marriage registration number shall be generated by the system and shall be sent back to the FDA/SDA for further processing
P6861|(Note: Generally, the SR/MO shall allocate to FDA/SDA to verify, capture/edit the required details and generate the necessary forms/documents needed for registration. But in an extra-ordinary situation where there are NO FDA/SDAs (either absent on that particular day or permanently position is absent), the SR/MO allocates to himself/herself by choosing the ‘Self’ in the drop menu, then the action button must change to ‘Start Registration’ and NOT ‘Allocate’. Then the SR/MO himself/herself verifies, captures/edits the required details and generates the necessary forms/documents and shall approve the application in the system by clicking on the ‘Check & Register’ button)
P6864|Steps taken by the FDA/SDA to Process the Marriage Registration Certificate
P6865|The marriage appointments allocated to the FDA/SDA by the SR/MO have to be displayed on the FDA/SDA dashboard. The table shall be in FIFO
P6866|The FDA/SDA shall click on the ‘Start Registration’ button
P6867|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also status gets updated in the applicant login on Kaveri2.0)
P6868|The FDA/SDA shall Review the Physical Documents, Application Summary and if everything is in order
P6869|The FDA/SDA shall Capture/Modify/Edit the basic details of the 3 Witnesses (ifn’t captured earlier or if change is required)
P6870|(Note: 1. The age of the witnesses should be >= 18 years; 2. Capture the details of 3 witnesses at the beginning (ie., during filling up the application form of notice); but later SDA/FDA should be given an ‘Edit Option’ to change the witness details keeping in mind the objection period of 30 days (in case of exigencies (death, cancellation, busy schedule, not showing up on the particular day, etc.))
P6871|(Note: In the case where the FDA/SDA edits the witness(es)’ details, then the marriage application form (if any & if required) has to be printed again and signatures of the bridegroom, bride and 3 witnesses have to be taken again)

P6875|Applicant Details – Bridegroom Details, Bride Details, Witness Details

P6877|(Note: Marriage registration number isn’t generated yet)
P6878|(Note: Required Forms – Marriage Registration Certificate (without Registration Number, Fee Payment Receipt)
P6879|(Note: Here, the SDA/FDA has to be given an edit option to edit the details (if and wherever required))
P6880|The FDA/SDA shall Generate the Summary Report by clicking the ‘Generate Certificate and Forms’ button
P6881|The FDA/SDA shall send it (above mentioned form) to the SR/MO for approval (check & register) by clicking on ‘Send back to SR/MO’ button
P6882|The SR/MO shall view the certificate and forms generated by the FDA/SDA, and shall approve the application in the system by clicking on the ‘Check & Register’ button
P6883|The system generates Marriage Registration Certificate with Registration Number
P6884|(Note: pop up message should appear once he clicks on ‘Check and Register’ saying that ‘Are you sure to Register? You cannot change later’ for which the SR/MO confirm
P6885|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also the status gets updated in the applicant login (and department login))

P6889|The FDA/SDA shall physically affix the colour joint-photo of the couple if required and get the cross-signatures of bridegroom and bride on the pasted joint photo of the couple (as the joint photo of the couple isn’t captured by the FDA/SDA)

P6903|(Note: pop up message should appear once he clicks on ‘Digital Sign & Complete’ button saying that ‘Are you sure to digitally sign and upload? Once uploaded, the details cannot be changed’ for which the SR/MO has to confirm)
P6904|(Note: An option may be provided for the SR/MO if re-scan is required; on clicking on the ‘Send for Re-scan’ button by SR/MO, it would request the SDA/FDA to re-scan and send it back to SR/MO; and then SR/MO shall view the scanned documents and shall proceed further by clicking on the ‘Digital Sign & Complete’ button)

P7036|The applicant has to fill the following entry fields for Marriage Details:

P7038|Date of Marriage (signifies the date on which the marriage took place)

P7041|(Note: 1. B2 size (500mm*707mm) Joint photo of the bridegroom and the bride should be in jpg format; size shouldn’t exceed 2 MB; 2. The uploaded Joint photo of the bridegroom and the bride shall be manually verified by the SR/MO/FDA/SDA before proceeding for the payment and booking appointment and also physically during the day of appointment at SRO)
P7043|The applicant has to fill the following entry fields for Bridegroom Details:
P7044|Salutation (mandatory field)
P7045|First Name, Middle Name, Last Name (only First Name is mandatory field)
P7046|Date of Birth (DoB) (optional field)
P7047|Age (in years) (should auto-fetch once DoB is entered; system should register only if age>=21 years for bridegroom) (mandatory field)
P7048|(Note: 1. The age of the bridegroom must be greater than or equal to 21 years; 2. The age must be rounded off to the lower whole number ie., if the age is 21.3 years, then then system should take 21 years only; if the age is 21.9 years, then then system should take 21 years only ie., the system must always take the lower side value until it is a whole number; 2. If DoB is fed, then the age should auto-fetch; 3. If the applicant doesn’t know his DoB, then the applicant must enter the age directly and must get a Doctor Certificate with Affidavit (or Other Supporting Document) and upload it in the upload document section)
P7049|Occupation (mandatory field)
P7050|Mobile Number (mandatory field)
P7051|Email ID (mandatory field)
P7052|Current Place of Residence (Current Address) – House No./Building Name, Street Address, State, District, Taluka, Hobli/Ward, Village/City, Pincode (all are mandatory fields)
P7053|Permanent Place of Residence (Permanent Address) – House No./Building Name, Street Address, State, District, Taluka, Hobli/Ward, Village/City, Pincode (all are mandatory fields)
P7054|(Note: A ‘Checkbox’ saying ‘Permanent address is same as Current address’ has to be given after Current Place of Residence (and before Permanent Place of Residence); if checked, then the same details as entered in the Current Place of Residence should be auto-filled in place of Permanent Place of Residence; ifn’t checked, then the applicant has to manually entered the required details)
P7055|Duration of Residence at Current Address (Length of Residence) (calendar) (in days/months/years) (mandatory field)
P7056|Status of Marriage (at the time of marriage) – Unmarried/ Divorcee/ Widow(er) (mandatory field)
P7057|(Note: If any one of Divorcee/Widow is chosen, a pop up message (dialogue box)(flash message) should appear which says ‘please upload the supporting document in the upload page’ while uploading the other documents (then corresponding document supporting the same should be uploaded at a later stage while uploading the forms and enclosures in the ‘Upload Documents’ screen) ie., for Unmarried – no need to upload any document; for Divorcee – divorce certificate; for Widow(er) – widow(er)/death certificate of spouse)

The age must be rounded off to the lower whole number ie., if the age is 21.3 years, then then system should take 21 years only; if the age is 21.9 years, then then system should take 21 years only ie., the system must always take the lower side value until it is a whole number; 2. If DoB is fed, then the age should auto-fetch; 3. P7244|The SR/MO receives the marriage applications irrespective of their types in one place. Table is supposed to be FIFO
P7245|The SR/MO can take the following actions:
P7246|View - The SR/ MO views the firm application by clicking on ‘View’ button, and then allocates to FDA/SDA/Self

P7248|(Note: 1. The dropdown which fetches all the FDA/SDAs in the office who are present on that particular day; 2. In case there are many FDAs/SDAs, then SR/ MO has the option allocate to a particular FDA/SDA of his choice from the dropdown list)
P7249|(Note: If the SR/MO allocates to himself/herself by choosing the ‘Self’ in the drop menu, then the action button must change to ‘Verify’ and NOT ‘Allocate’. P7251|FDA/SDA Login and Verification by the FDA/SDA

P7256|LHS (Left hand side of the page) – the Summary sheet containing all the details which the applicant has entered (marriage details, bridegroom details, bride details, witness details, fee details), a section to add his Remarks and a button to send it back to the SR/MO
P7257|RHS (Right hand side of the page) – contains the Uploads by the applicant which are Application Form and the Enclosures/Annexures
P7258|The FDA/SDA shall crosscheck the applications and the enclosures thoroughly; FDA/SDA fills a checklist mentioning Remarks for each category – marriage details, bridegroom details, bride details, witnesses’ details, uploaded documents; sends it back to the SR/MO with Remarks by clicking on ‘Send back to SR/MO’ button
P7260|Verification & Approval by the SR/MO

P7262|The SR/MO has additional checkbox which if ticked, then it takes the applicant to make ‘Schedule an Appointment’ option; ifn’t ticked will request the applicant to make changes (as per remarks made by SR/MO) in the application and re-submit the application

When the changes (attend to SR/MO remarks) are requested by the SR/MO to the applicant, the system takes the applicant to the ‘Marriage Details’ screen with the pre-filled data being retained as earlier over which he can over-write to make changes and then shall send back to the SR/MO by pressing the ‘Submit’ button; 2. If the applicant wants to change the type of marriage, then he/she has to re-apply)
P7264|On the Remarks section, there is a checklist - If the checkbox in the checklist is ticked, the remarks are voluntary; ifn’t ticked, then the remarks are mandatory

Until unless ‘Schedule an Appointment’ is checked, the applicant can’t proceed to book an appointment)

P7391|If the witnesses won’t turn up on the scheduled date and time summoned by the SR/MO or if the party/objector(s) request for official summons to be issued by the SR/MO for the witness(es) to be present for the enquiry, then the SR/MO issues summons to the required/requested witness(es) (see Form II – Summons to Witness(es)
P7392|On request, the SR/MO can issue summons to witness(es) from either/both party and objector side; 3. The party/objector should first make a payment ie., Fee for issuing Summons to the Witnesses to be paid by Objector = Rs.0.50 (for each witness) through K2 and should inform the SR/MO, then only the SR/MO will Issue Summons to the witness(es) and schedules for another date and time calling them for an Enquiry; for this enquiry (2nd sitting), no official form I notice will be sent again to the applicants and the objector(s)
P7393|After the enquiry, the SR/MO writes the Inquiry Results (Inquiry Order/ Speaking Order) based on the merits and Records Inquiry Results & Scans the Order. The SR/MO can either Over-rule or Sustain the objection
P7394|If the objection is sustained, then the marriage application can’t be registered; the marriage shall not be solemnized, Refusal Order (Inquiry Results) shall be printed by FDA/SDA and given to the parties.
P7395|(Note: 1. The marriage applications status in the system to be changed to ‘Objection Raised and Sustained’ with an option to view the ‘Inquiry Order’
P7396|If the objection is over-ruled, then the marriage application is unaffected; then step 83 shall be followed
P7397|(Note: 1. The applicant & objector receive an email/sms as notification; also status gets updated in the applicant login; 2. Both the applicants and the objector(s) receive a scanned copy of the Inquiry Order via email (downloadable form) and/or post and/or physically handed over just after the enquiry)
P7398|(Note: After the end of the 30 days (objection notice period), and in case no objections are filed, the applicants along with 3 witnesses shall appear before the SR/MO for solemnization of their marriage (NO restrictions on marriage solemnization period of 90 days for registration of marriage as in case of special marriage (intended marriage notice) ie., Total period can exceed 3 calendar months from the date of filing of notice)
P7399|After the end of the 30 days (objection notice period), and in case no objections are filed or in case the objection is over-ruled (ie., not sustained), then the SR/MO shall approve the application and the applicant receives an email/sms as notification and can proceed to make payment option
P7400|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also the status gets updated in the applicant login (and department login) on Kaveri2.0)
P7404|Form II
P7405|(see. Rule 6(4))

A sum of Rs. ....................................... being your travelling and other expenses for one day is herewith sent.

P7425|2nd Payment of Fee by the Applicant
P7426|The applicant shall enter the Number of Additional Copies (of the Final Marriage Registration Certificate) if required. Hence an option must be provided for the same
P7427|(Note: For each additional copy of marriage registration certificate, Rs.10 per copy will be charged; which gets reflected in the Display of Fee Structure page)
P7428|After SR/MO accepts and approves the application, the applicant logins in the Kaveri 2.0 portal and makes the 2nd Payment of Fee (Total Payable Amount) online through K2 portal by clicking on ‘Make Payment’ button
P7429|(Note: The system should show the calculated fee (freeze it and no changes can be made by the applicant at this stage) before applicant makes the payment and then give ‘Make Payment’ option)
P7430|Marriage Registration Fee = Rs.10 (fixed)
P7431|Scanning Fee = Rs.35 (per page of scanning)
P7432|(Note: Shall show the Names of the Forms and Annexures that need to be scanned)
P7433|No. of Additional Copies (of Marriage Registration Certificate) = Rs.10 (per copy)
P7434|Total Payable Amount =  Rs….. (= a + b + c)
P7435|The applicant shall receive an Acknowledgement from the K2 for making the online payment
P7436|On making the payment, a dialogue box/ flash message should appear stating ‘Your payment has been successfully made’
P7437|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also the status gets updated in the applicant login on Kaveri2.0)
P7438|The applicant can choose ‘Print Challan’, ‘Go to Home’ or ‘Book Appointment’.
P7440|Scheduling 2nd Appointment with the SR/MO by the Applicant
P7441|Once payment is being made, the applicant shall Schedule an Appointment (for Oath & Solemnisation) with the SR/MO by clicking on ‘Schedule Appointment’ button
P7442|The applicant shall select the Date and Time
P7443|Once the schedule is booked, then a dialogue box/ flash message should appear stating ‘Your appointment for 15th November, 2021 is successfully scheduled. Please reach the office 15 minutes early for a smooth registration experience’
P7444|The screen displays the applicant’s date, time and venue; and two buttons:
P7445|Cancel Appointment and
P7446|Re-schedule Appointment
P7447|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also the status gets updated in the applicant (department) login on Kaveri2.0)
P7449|Steps taken by SRO (SR/MO) to Register Marriage and to secure Marriage Certificate to the Applicant(s)
P7450|On the day of appointment, the Applicants (bridegroom, bride) along with witnesses shall appear before SR/MO and shall present the physical application forms and memorandum of declaration
P7452|Allocation & Approval by SR/MO for Registration
P7453|The SR/MO shall login to the Kaveri 2.0 system
P7454|From the menu on his dashboard, the SR/MO shall click on ‘Marriage Application’
P7455|The SR/MO shall click on ‘Marriage Appointment’ button
P7456|(Note: All the marriage appointments irrespective of their types for the day are listed in a table)
P7457|The SR/MO can take the following actions:
P7458|Allocate – the SR/MO can allocate the firm appointments to FDA/SDA/Self by clicking on ‘Allocate’ button so that they can verify, capture/edit the required details and generate the necessary forms/documents needed for registration
P7459|(Note: 1. The dropdown which fetches all the FDA/SDAs in the office who are present on that particular day; 2. In case there are many FDAs/SDAs, then SR/MO has the option allocate to a particular FDA/SDA of his choice from the dropdown list)
P7460|Approve – after the FDA/SDA has generated the required forms, they are sent to the SR/MO for approval; The SR/MO shall approve by clicking on the ‘Approve’ button and only after his/her approval, the marriage registration number shall be generated by the system and shall be sent back to the FDA/SDA for further processing
P7461|(Note: Generally, the SR/MO shall allocate to FDA/SDA to verify, capture/edit the required details and generate the necessary forms/documents needed for registration. But in an extra-ordinary situation where there are NO FDA/SDAs (either absent on that particular day or permanently position is absent), the SR/MO allocates to himself/herself by choosing the ‘Self’ in the drop menu, then the action button must change to ‘Start Registration’ and NOT ‘Allocate’. Then the SR/MO himself/herself verifies, captures/edits the required details and generates the necessary forms/documents and shall approve the application in the system by clicking on the ‘Check & Register’ button)
P7464|Steps taken by the FDA/SDA to Process the Marriage Registration Certificate
P7465|The marriage appointments allocated to the FDA/SDA by the SR/MO have to be displayed on the FDA/SDA dashboard. The table shall be in FIFO
P7466|The FDA/SDA shall click on the ‘Start Registration’ button
P7467|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also status gets updated in the applicant login on Kaveri2.0)
P7468|The FDA/SDA shall Review the Physical Documents, Application Summary and if everything is in order
P7469|The FDA/SDA shall Capture/Modify/Edit the basic details of the 3 Witnesses (ifn’t captured earlier or if change is required)
P7470|(Note: 1. The age of the witnesses should be >= 18 years; 2. Capture the details of 3 witnesses at the beginning (ie., during filling up the application form of notice); but later SDA/FDA should be given an ‘Edit Option’ to change the witness details keeping in mind the objection period of 30 days (in case of exigencies (death, cancellation, busy schedule, not showing up on the particular day, etc.))
P7471|(Note: In the case where the FDA/SDA edits the witness(es)’ details, then the marriage application form (if any & if required) has to be printed again and signatures of the bridegroom, bride and 3 witnesses have to be taken again)
P7472|The FDA/SDA Screen displays the following:
P7473|Application Details – Application Number, Date of Submission, Application (Marriage) Type, Date of Marriage (whichever is applicable)
P7474|Marriage Details – Date and Place (Address) of Marriage
P7475|Applicant Details – Bridegroom Details, Bride Details, Witness Details
P7476|A ‘Generate Certificate and Forms’ button to generate the required forms – the details of the applicants have to be auto-fetched in the set format to generate the required forms
P7477|(Note: Marriage registration number isn’t generated yet)
P7478|(Note: Required Forms – Marriage Registration Certificate (without Registration Number, Fee Payment Receipt)
P7479|(Note: Here, the SDA/FDA has to be given an edit option to edit the details (if and wherever required))
P7480|The FDA/SDA shall Generate the Summary Report by clicking the ‘Generate Certificate and Forms’ button
P7481|The FDA/SDA shall send it (above mentioned form) to the SR/MO for approval (check & register) by clicking on ‘Send back to SR/MO’ button
P7482|The SR/MO shall view the certificate and forms generated by the FDA/SDA, and shall approve the application in the system by clicking on the ‘Check & Register’ button
P7483|The system generates Marriage Registration Certificate with Registration Number
P7484|(Note: pop up message should appear once he clicks on ‘Check and Register’ saying that ‘Are you sure to Register? You cannot change later’ for which the SR/MO confirm
P7485|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also the status gets updated in the applicant login (and department login))
P7486|In the back end, the Index of Marriage are stored in Index Register (Forms V & VI for wife and husband separately)
P7487|After Approval by SR/MO, the firm registration certificate is pushed back to the FDA/SDA dashboard
P7488|After approval, the FDA/SDA shall click on the ‘Print Certificate and Forms’ button to Print the Marriage Certificate (with Registration Number) (and other forms wherever required) and shall get them signed by the applicants and the witnesses (if/wherever required)
P7489|The FDA/SDA shall physically affix the colour joint-photo of the couple if required and get the cross-signatures of bridegroom and bride on the pasted joint photo of the couple (as the joint photo of the couple isn’t captured by the FDA/SDA)
P7490|The FDA/SDA shall get Seal and Signature affixed by the SR/MO on the Marriage Certificate (with Registration Number) (and other forms wherever required)
P7491|The FDA/SDA shall click on the ‘Upload’ button
P7492|The FDA/SDA upload screen shall display the following:
P7493|Details: Application Number, Application Type, Date of Marriage
P7494|Scan and Preview buttons: to scan and preview Certificate & Forms, Annexures/Enclosures
P7495|‘Save and Send back to SR/MO’ button
P7496|The FDA/SDA shall Scan and Preview (by clicking the ‘Scan’ and ‘Preview’ buttons respectively) the following documents:
P7497|Application Form of Notice
P7498|Signed Marriage Certificate
P7499|Enclosures/Annexures
P7500|The FDA/SDA shall click on the ‘Save and Send back to SR/MO’ button and shall forward it to the SR
P7501|The SR shall view the documents uploaded by FDA/SDA, then shall click on ‘Digital Sign & Complete’ button to put his digital signature, encrypt and to upload all the saved documents
P7502|(Note: pop up message should appear once he clicks on ‘Digital Sign & Complete’ button saying that ‘Are you sure to digitally sign and upload? Once uploaded, the details cannot be changed’ for which the SR/MO has to confirm)
P7503|(Note: An option may be provided for the SR/MO if re-scan is required; on clicking on the ‘Send for Re-scan’ button by SR/MO, it would request the SDA/FDA to re-scan and send it back to SR/MO; and then SR/MO shall view the scanned documents and shall proceed further by clicking on the ‘Digital Sign & Complete’ button)
P7504|(Note: The applicant receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also the status gets updated in the applicant login (and department login))
P7505|Any Additional Payment by the applicant(s)/objector(s) is made through Cash; the applicant(s)/objector(s) receives an Acknowledgement (a copy of Fee Receipt has to be generated by the FDA/SDA on receipt of cash)
P7506|The scanned documents are sent to RGoM (Registrar General of Marriage)
P7507|The Marriage Registration Certificate is handed over physically to the applicant after receiving the acknowledgement and the applicant shall collect the Marriage Certificate
P7508|The Sakala is updated
P7510|(Note: 1. If applied for CC (Certified Copy) for Marriage Certificate, then Digitally/Physically Signed Copy from SR/MO may be issued by filling appropriate form; 2. In case any changes to be done to the Registration of Marriage due to erroneous data entry, the same shall be done in the system with approval from SR)

P7556|Validations and Controls
P7557|The system should record application details online through web
P7558|The system should accept payment and captures payment detail
P7559|The system should allow modification/deletion of entered data before registration and after registration with orders from the MO
P7560|The system should allow Approval/Rejection of application with mechanism for entering the mandatory remarks in the system and generation of endorsement against the same
P7561|The system should notify MO/FDA/Applicants if application is rejected through e-mail, SMS, notification or endorsement
P7562|The system should record notice of intended marriage in the system online
P7563|The system should Display the notice on public portal after authorization of the MO in the system
P7564|The system should generate marriage certificate
P7565|The system should capture delivery details once the certificate is issued
P7566|The system should show status of application
P7567|The system should display the application filed for editing by the applicant before registration
P7568|The system should display marriage records on query
P7569|The system should display available date and time for solemnization in case of special marriage
P7570|The system should book a slot for solemnization on request
P7571|The system should capture photographs of couple
P7572|The system should generate MIS reports based on data captured in the system. There should be authorization mechanism for role based access to reports for generation and view
P7573|The system should generate Summary Report for verification of data entered and make provision for correction if any changes found
P7574|Once the applicant submits the application and after the application being verified & approved by FDA/SDA/SR/MO, the ‘URL and Barcode’ should be generated on the Application Form of Notice so that later, it would be helpful for the Objector to file an Objection on payment of Objection Fee
P7580|FIRM REGISTRATION MODULE
P7582|Brief Description
P7583|In addition to the information below, please find a detailed requirements document attached:
P7584|https://csgkarnataka.sharepoint.com/:w:/r/sites/ULMS/Knowledge%20Base/FRS/Approved/FRS/Firm%20registration%20FRS.docx?d=w2d77d0a31812432fa19596d8e03eb8d0&csf=1&web=1&e=UI9vdZ
P7585|Please use the above link to access Firm registration detailed FRS.
P7586|Brief Description
P7587|Firm is a collection of professionals need to work in the same direction under one roof. Firm comprised of capital, finance, asset, shareholders, funds, employee and many more. Different firms have different criteria to work as depend upon its theme and area of operating. These differentials in the numbers of elements of the varied firms bring a different set of rules and regulations for registration. Here, the partnership firm gets registered in accordance with Indian Partnership Act, 1932 read with Karnataka Partnership (Registration of Firms) Rules, 1954.
P7588|Overview
P7589|Any two or more citizens can enter into partnership and register the partnership firm. The firm is registered in the office of District Registrar of the concerned jurisdiction. Firm can be registered after following the procedure laid down in Section 59 of the Indian Partnership Act, 1932.
P7590|Actors
P7591|Applicant – fills the application form online, uploads require documents, makes payment, schedules appointment, collects firm registration certificate
P7592|Agent/Presenter – represents the applicant (on applicant’s behalf) at DRO at the time of firm registration
P7593|DR – scrutinizes document and application forms, admits for registration, signs firm registration certificate and issues the registration certificate
P7594|FDA/SDA – scrutinizes document and application forms, captures/edits data, scans and uploads the documents
P7595|System – generates summary report, generates and sends email/sms, generates registration number
P7596|Kaveri 2.0 – software used in registration
P7597|Inputs
P7598|Filled & Signed Application Form (New Firm/ Amendment/ Re-constitution or Dissolution) in the prescribed format
P7599|Certified Original copy of the Partnership deed or the document detailing the terms and condition of partnership
P7600|A specimen of the Affidavit that is certifying the details that are mentioned in the partnership deed and the documents
P7601|Proof of Authorisation for the Agent (GPA/Letter of Authority/ Vakalat/ etc.)
P7602|Enclosures/Annexures (ID Proof, Address Proof, etc.)
P7604|Output
P7605|Certificate of Firm Registration in Form C with DR’s seal and signature affixed

P7664|Roles, Hierarchy and Responsibilities

P7668|Types of Firm Registration
P7670|Brief Description
P7671|Firm is a collection of professionals need to work in the same direction under one roof. Firm comprised of capital, finance, asset, shareholders, funds, employee and many more
P7672|a. New Firm Registration – means constitution or declaration of terms and conditions of a firm signed by two or more individuals to carry out a business or institution
P7673|b. Amendment of Firm – means any change with respect to name or location of the principal place of business, change in the place of business other than the principal place of business, alteration in the name or permanent address of the partners, notice of election by person admitted to the benefit of partnership on attaining majority
P7675|c. Re-constitution – means change in the constitution of the firm
P7677|d. Dissolution of Firm – means dissolution of the firm

P7682|Figure 1: Registration of Firm (Page 1)

P7687|Functional Step by Step Process:
P7689|Steps taken by Citizen/ Applicant to file Firm Registration Online
P7690|Applicant Login
P7691|To file the application online, the applicant shall browse for Kaveri Online Services ie., Kaveri 2.0
P7692|The applicant shall register as a user by creating user id and password
P7693|The applicant shall now login in the Kaveri 2.0 portal
P7694|The applicant shall click on ‘Start a New Application’
P7695|The applicant shall click on ‘Firm Registration’ on the screen
P7696|The applicant shall select the required service (among the four options) as per the requirement. The services provided are:
P7697|Registration of New Firm,
P7698|Amendment to the Constitution of Firm and
P7699|Re-constitution of Firm
P7700|Dissolution of Firm
P7701|New Firm Registration
P7702|For New Firm Registration, the system requests the applicant to fill in the following entry fields:
P7703|Details (User Inputs) to be captured for the New Firm:
P7705|Firm Details:
P7706|Name of the Firm (mandatory field)
P7707|(Note: 1. The system shall search in the Index of Register of Firms for registered firms with same name and address exist. If found in search, or if name of firm is objectionable, the system shall ask for change in name of the firm; 2. Objectionable Names NOT to be used are: Crown, Emperor, Empress, Empire, Imperial, King, Queen, Royal, or words expressed or implying the sanction, approval or patronage of Government)
P7708|Place of Business
P7709|Principal Place – House No./Building Name, Street Address, State, District, Taluka, Hobli/Ward, Village/City, Pincode (all are mandatory fields)
P7710|Other Place(s) ie., the Branch(es) – House No./Building Name, Street Address, State, District, Taluka, Hobli/Ward, Village/City, Pincode (all are mandatory fields) (if required and only if branch(es) exist, hence an optional field)
P7711|(Note: Provision must be made for the applicant to enter any number of branches as he wishes)
P7712|Duration of the firm (calendar) (mandatory field)
P7713|(Note: The applicant has to mention either (i) Select a date (date, month, year) from the calendar or (ii) At Will (indefinite time))
P7715|Presenter Details:
P7716|Whether Presenter is Partner or Agent? (radio button; keep default as partner which can be changed) (mandatory field)
P7717|(Note: If presenter is an Agent, then the system must capture the below mentioned details (from ii to ix); If presenter is a Partner, then the system must capture the below mentioned details (from ii to ix) and should ensure that the information captured should be auto-fetched in Partner Details page under Partner 1)
P7718|Salutation (mandatory field)
P7719|First Name, Middle Name, Last Name (mandatory field)
P7720|Date of Birth (calendar) (optional field)
P7721|Age (in years) (mandatory field)
P7722|(Note: 1. Age (in years) should auto-fetch once DoB is entered; 2. The system should register only if age>=18 years)
P7723|Gender (mandatory field)
P7724|Mobile Number (mandatory field)
P7725|Email ID (optional field)
P7726|Permanent Address – House No./Building Name, Street Address, State, District, Taluka, Hobli/Ward, Village/City, Pincode (all are mandatory fields)
P7727|(Note: Only the partners can be applicants (ie., not the agent))
P7728|Partner Details:
P7729|Name of the Partners in full (mandatory field)
P7730|(ie., Salutation-Name-Relationship-Relative Name)

P7732|First Name, Middle Name, Last Name (only First Name is mandatory field)
P7733|Relationship (dropdown) (Eg: D/o, MG, C/o, S/o, M/o, W/o) (mandatory field)
P7734|Relative Name (manually enter data) (Eg: father/ mother/husband/ wife/care-of/guardian (if minor) name, etc.) (mandatory field)
P7735|Date of Birth (calendar) (optional field)
P7736|Age (in years) (mandatory field)
P7737|(Note: 1. Age (in years) should auto-fetch once DoB is entered; 2. Even a minor can be a partner)
P7738|Gender (mandatory field)
P7739|Occupation (optional field)
P7740|Mobile Number (mandatory) (mandatory field)

P7744|Permanent Address – House No./Building Name, Street Address, State, District, Taluka, Hobli/Ward, Village/City, Pincode (all are mandatory fields)
P7745|(Note: to provide for ‘same as above’ button; if checked, then the Permanent Address should be auto-fetched from Current Address)
P7746|(Note: to provide for ‘same as above’ button; if checked, then the Permanent Address should be auto-fetched from Current Address)
P7747|(Note: fields from i to x (under Partner Details) should be captured SEPARATELY for all the partners)
P7749|Station & Date Details:
P7750|Station (ie., Place) (mandatory field)
P7751|Date (calendar) (mandatory field)
P7753|Witness Details:
P7754|Details of Witness 1:
P7755|(Note: note/message – ‘witness who must be either a Gazetted Officer, Advocate, Attorney, Vakil, Honorary Pleader, Chartered Accountant or Registered State Auditor’ must be shown on the top of the witness page)
P7756|Salutation (mandatory field)
P7757|First Name, Middle Name, Last Name (mandatory field)
P7758|Date of Birth (calendar) (optional field)
P7759|Age (in years) (mandatory field)
P7760|(Note: 1. Age (in years) should auto-fetch once DoB is entered; 2. The system should register only if age>=18 years)

P8354|For RE-CONSTITUTION OF FIRM
P8355|Stamp Duty on Deed of Re-constitution (ie., Change in the Constitution) of Firm
P8356|Where immovable property contributed as share by a partner or partners remains with the firm at the time of outgoing in whatever manner by such partner(s) on re-constitution of such firm, the Stamp Duty = 3% on the MV (Market Value) of the immovable property remaining with the firm
P8357|In any other case, Stamp Duty = Rs.1000 (fixed)
P8358|Registration Fee = Rs.25 (fixed)
P8359|Application Fee = Rs.1 (fixed)
P8360|Scanning Fee = Rs.35 (per page of scanning)
P8361|(Note: Total number of pages to be scanned = X+1 (Acknowledgement))
P8362|Total Payable Amount (in Rs.) = a(i) or a(ii) + b + c + d
P8364|For DISSOLUTION OF FIRM
P8365|Stamp Duty on Deed of Dissolution of Firm
P8366|Where immovable property contributed as share by a partner or partners remains with the firm at the time of outgoing in whatever manner by such partner(s) on re-constitution of such firm, the Stamp Duty = 3% on the MV (Market Value) of the immovable property remaining with the firm
P8367|In any other case, Stamp Duty = Rs.1000 (fixed)
P8368|Registration Fee = Rs.25 (fixed)
P8369|Application Fee = Rs.1 (fixed)
P8370|Scanning Fee = Rs.35 (per page of scanning)
P8371|(Note: Total number of pages to be scanned = X+1 (Acknowledgement))
P8372|Total Payable Amount (in Rs.) = a(i) or a(ii) + b + c + d
P8373|(Note: During the verification process, FDA/SDA/DR would have verified the data (say, number of pages entered by the applicant to be scanned); if any changes are needed, then necessary changes would be made by DR)
P8374|Example:
P8375|In the formation of firm at the initial stage,
P8376|Ram – had given his land
P8377|Shyam – had given his machinery
P8378|At the time of Dissolution,
P8379|Use-case 1: ram & shyam get back their respective properties ie., in the same ratio as they had invested in the beginning, then 2 = Rs.1000 (fixed)
P8380|Use-case 2: ram & shyam won’t get back their respective properties ie., not in the same ratio as they had invested in the beginning, then 2 = 3% on the MV (Market Value) of the immovable property
P8381|Submission of Application Form
P8382|The applicant clicks on ‘Submit’ button to submit the application online. The applicant will get an application number generated as acknowledgement and receives an email/sms as notification
P8383|On submission of application, a dialogue box/ flash message should appear stating ‘Your application has been successfully submitted’
P8384|(Note: The applicant (and/or presenter/agent and all the partners) receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also status gets updated in the applicant login (and department login) on Kaveri2.0; the application gets logged in in order in the SR login)
P8386|Steps taken by DRO to Register Firm and to secure Firm Registration Certificate to the Applicant(s)
P8388|DR Login and Allocation/Verification by DR
P8389|District Registrar (DR) shall login to the Kaveri 2.0 system
P8390|From the menu on his dashboard, the DR shall click on ‘Firm Application’
P8391|The DR receives the firm applications irrespective of their types in one place. Table is supposed to be FIFO
P8392|The DR can take the following actions:
P8393|View - The DR views the firm application by clicking on ‘View’ button, and then allocates to FDA/SDA/Self
P8394|Allocate – The DR can allocate the firm application to FDA/SDA/Self by clicking on ‘Allocate’ button
P8395|(Note: 1. The dropdown which fetches all the FDA/SDAs in the office who are present on that particular day; 2. In case there are many FDAs/SDAs, then DR has the option allocate to a particular FDA/SDA of his choice from the dropdown list)
P8396|(Note: If the DR allocates to himself/herself by choosing the ‘Self’ in the drop menu, then the action button must change to ‘Verify’ and NOT ‘Allocate’. In this case, there are NO FDA/SDA Remarks on the UI. The DR himself/herself verifies (the application and enclosures) and sends back to the applicant by clicking on ‘Send to Applicant’ button)
P8398|FDA/SDA Login and Verification by the FDA/SDA
P8399|The FDA/SDA shall login
P8400|All the applications regardless of their type that the DR allocates are supposed to show on FDA/SDA dashboard. Table is supposed to be FIFO.
P8401|The FDA/SDA shall start the verification process by selecting ‘Start Verification’ button
P8402|The FDA/SDA Application Verification screen is split into 2 sides:
P8403|LHS (Left hand side of the page) – the Summary sheet containing all the Details which the Applicant has entered (firm details, presenter details, partner details, witness details, stamp duty & fee details), a section to add his Remarks and a button to send it back to the DR
P8404|RHS (Right hand side of the page) – contains the Uploads by the Applicant which are Forms and Enclosures
P8405|The FDA/SDA shall crosscheck the application(s) and the enclosures thoroughly; FDA/SDA fills a Checklist mentioning Remarks for each category – firm details, presenter details, partner details, witness details, stamp duty & fee details; sends it back to the DR with Remarks by clicking on ‘Send back to DR’
P8407|Verification & Approval by the DR
P8408|After the application is received from FDS/SDA, the DR shall login and shall view the remarks made by FDA/SDA by clicking on ‘View’ button and shall add his/her own remarks filling the checklist and shall send it back to the applicant by clicking on ‘Send to Applicant’ for either to attend to the remarks made and resend the application (in the applicant login, ‘Your application cannot be registered as it is, Kindly Refer to the Sub Registrar Remarks to know more’ message shall be displayed) or to proceed to make payment
P8409|The DR has an additional checkbox named ‘Can proceed to Make Payment’ which if ticked, then it takes the applicant to make payment option; ifn’t ticked will request the applicant to make changes (as per remarks made by DR) in the application and re-submit the application
P8410|(Note: 1. When the changes (attend to DR remarks) are requested by the DR to the applicant, the system takes the applicant to the ‘Firm Details’ page (ie., the first page in each type of required service as requested by the applicant) with the pre-filled data being retained as earlier over which he can over-write to make changes and then shall send back to the DR by pressing the ‘Submit’ button; 2. If the applicant wants to change the type of service, then he/she has to re-apply)
P8411|On the Remarks section, there is a checklist - If the checkbox in the checklist is ticked, the remarks are voluntary; ifn’t ticked, then the remarks are mandatory
P8412|(Note: 1. In either case, the applicant (and/or presenter/agent and all the partners) receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0 dashboard; also the status of the application gets updated in the applicant dashboard on Kaveri2.0; 3. Until unless ‘Can proceed to Make Payment’ is checked, the applicant can’t proceed to make the payment)
P8413|(Note: 1. If the applicant is willing to attend to the remarks made by DR, then resend the application after corrections are made. If not willing to attend to the remarks made by DR, then the system shall generate the Endorsement with Reason for Rejection; 2. Based on the remarks made by the FDA/SDA, the DR has the option to change (make modification) to the number of pages to be scanned)
P8414|Payment of Fee by the Applicant
P8415|After DR accepts and approves the application, the applicant shall login in the Kaveri 2.0 portal and make the Payment of Fee (Total Payable Amount) online through K2 portal by clicking on ‘Make Payment’ button
P8416|(Note: The system should show the calculated fee (freeze it and no changes can be made by the applicant at this stage) before applicant makes the payment and then give ‘Make Payment’ option)

P8501|Firm Registration at DRO on the Day of Appointment
P8502|On the day of appointment, the Applicant or Presenter/Agent will appear before the DR and shall present the physical copy of the documents (Filled & Signed Application Form (New Firm/ Amendment/ Re-constitution or Dissolution), Certified Original copy of the Partnership deed, a specimen of the Affidavit that is certifying the details that are mentioned in the partnership deed, Proof of Authorisation for the Agent (GPA/Letter of Authority/Vakalat/etc.) and other Supporting Documents & Enclosures)
P8504|Allocation & Approval by DR for Registration
P8505|The DR shall login to the Kaveri 2.0 system
P8506|From the menu on his dashboard, the DR shall click on ‘Firm Application’
P8507|The DR shall click on ‘Firm Appointment’ button
P8508|All the firm appointments irrespective of their type of services requested for the day are listed in a table
P8509|The DR can take the following actions:
P8510|Allocate – the DR can allocate the firm appointments to FDA/SDA/Self by clicking on ‘Allocate’ button so that they can verify, capture/edit the required details and generate the necessary forms/documents needed for registration
P8511|(Note: 1. The dropdown which fetches all the FDA/SDAs in the office who are present on that particular day; 2. In case there are many FDAs/SDAs, then DR has the option allocate to a particular FDA/SDA of his choice from the dropdown list)
P8512|Approve – after the FDA/SDA has generated the required forms, they are sent to the DR for approval; The DR shall approve by clicking on the ‘Approve’ button and only after his/her approval, the firm registration number shall be generated by the system and shall be sent back to the FDA/SDA for further processing
P8514|Steps taken by the FDA/SDA to Process the Firm Registration Certificate
P8515|The firm appointments allocated to the FDA/SDA by the DR have to be displayed on the FDA/SDA dashboard. The table shall be in FIFO
P8516|The FDA/SDA shall Review the Physical Documents, Application Summary and if everything is in order ie., the FDA/SDA shall crosscheck the application(s) and the enclosures thoroughly – application form(s), partnership deed copy (with proper stamp duty), affidavit, proof of authorisation for the agent and other enclosures (ie., firm details, presenter details, partner details, witness details, deed copy details, uploaded documents)
P8517|The FDA/SDA shall click on the ‘Start Registration’ button
P8518|(Note: The applicant (and/or presenter/agent and all the partners) receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also status gets updated in the applicant login (and department login) on Kaveri2.0)
P8519|The FDA/SDA shall first Verify the Authenticity of the Presenter/Agent either by generating an OTP (to the presenter’s (partner/agent) mobile number as entered in the filled application form) and entering it in the system or by physically verifying the Letter of Proof of Authorisation for the Agent (GPA/Letter of Authority/Vakalat/ etc.)
P8520|(Note: The system should allow the FDA/SDA to proceed further ONLY after confirmation of the Authenticity of the Presenter (Partner/Agent))

P8526|(Note: Firm registration number isn’t generated yet)
P8527|(Note: Required Forms – Firm Registration Certificate (without Registration Number, Fee Payment Receipt)
P8528|The FDA/SDA shall Generate the Summary Report by clicking the ‘Generate Certificate and Forms’ button
P8529|The FDA/SDA shall send it (above mentioned certificate and forms) to the DR for Approval (Check & Register) by clicking on ‘Send back to DR’ button
P8530|The DR shall view the certificate and forms generated by the FDA/SDA, and shall approve the application in the system by clicking on the ‘Check & Register’ button
P8531|The system generates Firm Registration Certificate with Registration Number
P8532|(Note: pop up message should appear once he clicks on ‘Check and Register’ saying that ‘Are you sure to Register? You cannot change later’ for which the DR has to confirm)
P8533|(Note: The applicant (and/or presenter/agent and all the partners) receives an email/sms; the applicant receives a notification on Kaveri2.0; also the status gets updated in the applicant (and department) login)

P8581|(Note: 1. If applied for CC (Certified Copy) for Firm Registration Certificate, then Digitally Signed/ Physically Signed Copy from DR may be issued by filling appropriate form by paying corresponding fee ie., Copies from the register of firms = Rs.10 (for each 100 words or part thereof); 2. In case any changes to be done to the Register of Firms due to erroneous data entry, the same shall be done in the system with approval from DR)
P8583|(Note: As per section 10-A of Karnataka Stamp Act, 1957, the stamp duty has to be paid within 2 months from the date of execution of the deed document. If it exceeds 2 months, then the document can be Impounded under section 33 of Karnataka Stamp Act, 1957. As per section 39 of Karnataka Stamp Act, 1957, the payment of the proper duty or the amount required to make up the same, together with a penalty of five rupees; or if he thinks fit; an amount not exceeding ten times the amount of the proper duty or of the deficient portion thereof, whether such amount exceeds or falls short of five rupees has to be made)

P8649|Validations and Controls
P8650|The system should record application details
P8651|The system should search firm’s name and address in its database and give alert if firm with same name as applied for exists or is objectionable. It should not allow registration with existing name and address or if objectionable. The application form will have the mechanism for the applicant to check for firms’ names that are not feasible
P8652|The system should calculate firm registration fee on basis of department’s business rules
P8653|The system should verify the authenticity of the presenter (partner/agent) before starting the firm registration process at DR office
P8654|The system should print acknowledgement and fee receipt on updating of payment details in the system
P8655|The system should generate unique firm registration number once the registration process is completed
P8656|The system should enable updating in register of firms and it’s Index in case of request for amendment/re-constitution/dissolution
P8657|The system should enable printing of respective endorsements and certificate based on application type whether registration, amendment, re-constitution or dissolution of firm
P8658|The system should enable scan and upload of the documents attached with the application
P8659|The system should make automatic accounting entry for each transaction in A-Register
P8660|The system should generate CVC reports based on data captured in the system. There should be authorization mechanism for role-based access to reports for generation and view
P8661|The system should generate summary report for verification of data entered and make provision for correction if any changes found before registration
P8663|ADDITIONS to the FRS – UPDATION REQUIRED
P8665|1. Discussion with Saurabh Sir, Bhoja Naik Sir and Prashanth
P8666|If restriction is Y and restrictiontype is NOT 'pyki' then no transaction should be allowed, and a message should be shown with restriction description and type a popup
P8667|If restriction is Y and restrictiontype is 'pyki' then 11ESketch should be asked
P8668|If availableextent is zero, then the transaction should not be allowed
P8669|If availableextent is less than totalextent, then the sale is part, and 11E sketch should be asked
P8670|If Category is 'PRV' then sale and all other transactions should be allowed; for others no Sale should be allowed
P8671|If a property is multi-owner, then insist on 11ESketch number.
P8674|2. Discussion with Saurabh Sir, Bhoja Naik Sir and Prashanth
P8675|Please see the process -
P8676|Document is sent to SRO with summary sheet having only one party details (photo and fingerprint)
P8677|SRO keeps it on hold (CD119)
P8678|The application is now in Minute Book list also and in DEO login for generate endorsement.
P8679|The endorsement to have the 1st section (10A Certificate), the 2nd section with the parties present photo and biometric.
P8680|After that when other parties appear then the SRO releases it by going to minute book report and sends it for DEO to take the remaining picture and biometric.
P8681|The DEO takes remaining photo and biometric, sends the updated summary sheet back to SRO again
P8682|This time the SRO approves it and sends back for final Generation of Endorsement
P8683|Now endorsement is generated with 2nd section having remaining party photo and biometric and the 3rd section (60A Certificate). At this stage the final registration number is generated.
P8687|Dear All,
P8689|We had a discussion to review the e-Aasthi and e-Swathu integrations and following are the conclusions -
P8691|E-Aasthi Property -
P8693|<responsecode> is to be looked into before allowing transactions. It has to be zero ‘0’ for transaction to proceed or else the contents in the <exceptionmessage> has to be shown.
P8694|There are 4 types of <propertycategory> -> Site, Building, Apartment or any other such as multi-ownership, masters for which will be provided by NIC.
P8695|In case of Building as Property Category -> <floordetails> tag is to be used and all <floorarea> tag within <floor> tags are to be added to get the built-up area. <sitearea> tag to be used for getting the underlying area size.
P8696|In case of Apartment/multi-ownership -> <apartmentdetails> tag has to be used and the <superbuiltuparea> value has to be used for identifying the area.
P8697|In case of Site as Property Category -> <sitearea> tag to be used for getting the land size.
P8698|The <propertyclassification> tag will contain two options - Adhikrita (for A-Khatha) and Anadhikrita (for B-Khatha) in Kannada. Only Adhikrita to be allowed for transaction. The exact Kannada words for performing the match will be shared by NIC.
P8700|E-Swathu Property –
P8702|<responsecode> is to be looked into before allowing transactions. It has to be zero ‘0’ for transaction to proceed or else the contents in the <exceptionmessage> has to be shown.
P8703|<propertytype> tag will have the following values -> 1. Site, Building, Apartment/ Row/Villa and Building with multiple owners. The masters for the same shall be provided by NIC.
P8704|<propertycategory> tag contains the following values -> residential, commercial, industrial, KIADB, etc. The masters for the same shall be provided by NIC.
P8705|<propertyclassification> tag contains the following values -> Gramthana, Layout, Industrial Purpose, etc. The masters for the same shall be provided by NIC.
P8706|Property Form depends upon classification.
P8707|The following are the type of JSLIP files that have to be sent for different types of deed documents -
P8708|Type A - Ownership Change will take place such as Gift Deed, Sale Deed, etc.
P8709|Type B - Mortgage/Release Case there would be mortgage and release ID. Only liability changes. Bank Auction deed, Mortgage with possession and without possession, etc.
P8710|Type C - Partition Deed Case, with cash transaction - Ownership Change
P8711|without cash transaction - With new property getting created
P8712|Type D - Rectification Deed, Correction Deed, etc. against previous deed transaction
P8713|Type Z - Cancellation Deed, Settlement Deeds, existing deeds to be captured

P8775|MOJINI Concept - 11E Sketch, Parties (Transferer (Co-Transferers) & Transferee (Co-Transferees)), Schedules, Schedule to Party Mapping, Calculation of SD & RF based on No. of Schedules
P8777|I. Conditions In case of agriculture land where 11E Sketches are required:
P8778|Party to Party
P8779|if Full Extent of land is Transacted, then NO 11E Sketch is required
P8780|if Part Extent of land is Transacted, then 11E Sketch is required and the available extent should be less than total extent
P8781|If available extent is zero, then Registration should not happen
P8782|In case of Partition/Settlement/Gift/Release
P8783|Single property divided into several schedules (share) – single 11E Sketch is enough which contains single transferer and multiple transferees (ie., co-transferees)
P8784|Single transferer and Single transferee,
P8785|For Full Extent - then 11E Sketch isn’t required
P8786|For Part Extent - then 11E Sketch is required
P8788|II. Parties
P8789|Transferer (and Co-transferers) – are Executants
P8790|Transferee (and Co-transfees) – are Claimants
P8791|(Note: 1. There shall be one Transferer and remaining are Co-transferers
P8792|2. There shall be one Transferee and remaining are Co-transferees)
P8794|III. Schedule to Party Mapping
P8795|This arises in case of Partition, Settlement, Gift, Release and Exchange
P8796|In case of single doner, single property (single schedule) and single donee, then there will be single SD and single RF
P8797|In case of single doner, single property (single schedule) and multiple donees, then there will be single SD and single RF
P8798|In case of multiple doners, single property (single schedule) and single donee, then there will be single SD and single RF
P8799|In case of multiple doners and single property (divided into multiple schedule) for multiple donees, then SD and RF will be on the number of schedules
P8800|In case of multiple doners, multiple properties (divided into multiple schedule) and single donee, then there will be multiple SD and multiple RF based on the number of schedules
P8801|In case of multiple doners, multiple properties (divided into multiple schedule) and multiple donees, then there will be multiple SD and multiple RF based on the number of schedules
P8803|III. Fields to Capture/Record for Part Property
P8804|Transferer (and Co-transferers) – Name, Gender, Address, ID Type, ID No., Mobile No., Name of Relative, Relationship, Ownercategory (PRV)
P8805|Transferee (and Co-transfees) – usual process as in Kaveri 2.0
P8806|Schedule - Acre, Gunta and f-gunta (ie., Ext_acre, Ext_gunta, Ext_fgunta)
P8807|Party to Schedule Mapping
P8808|Calculation of SD and RF based on the No. of Schedules

Finally, insert the data with new application number.

---TABLE 55---
T55R0| || Property 1 || Property 2
T55R1| || Agriculture (Bhoomi) || Agriculture (Bhoomi)
T55R2|a. MV (Market Valuation) || 10 lakh = MV1 || 5 lakh = MV2
T55R3|b. SD (Stamp Duty) || 50,000 (=5% of MV) = SD1 || 25,000 (=5% of MV) = SD2
T55R4|/ Case 1: Exemption as % of Total SD || / Case 1: Exemption as % of Total SD || / Case 1: Exemption as % of Total SD
T55R5|c. Exemption on SD = 75% (say) / (ie., 75% on Total SD = SD1 + SD2) || Here, SD1 + SD2 = 50,000 + 25,000 = 75,000 / Exemption = 75% of 75,000 = 56,250 || Here, SD1 + SD2 = 50,000 + 25,000 = 75,000 / Exemption = 75% of 75,000 = 56,250
